### [1] [RecSoup: Efficient Model Merging Approach for Controllable Multi-Objective Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21220)
*Zidong Jia,Guofu Xie,Xiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 RecSoup，一种基于模型合并的两阶段轻量方法实现可控多目标推荐（CMOR）：先通过联合考虑目标特定重要性与跨目标相关性的剪枝缓解参数干扰，再用可学习的偏好-系数映射进行自适应参数合并，实现推理时动态权衡且无需联合重训或生成模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CMOR 方法依赖输出级重排或参数生成模块：前者限制可行权衡范围，后者带来高昂训练与推理开销。模型合并可复用多个目标专属模型，但直接应用于推荐系统面临缺乏共享线性区域与跨目标参数干扰的挑战。

Method: 两阶段框架：①剪枝策略，同时考虑目标特定重要性与跨目标相关性以缓解参数干扰；②自适应参数合并，学习偏好到合并系数的映射，在推理时动态控制权衡。理论分析证明其在无平滑约束下可完整遍历 Pareto 前沿。

Result: 多基准实验表明 Pareto 前沿质量与可控性均提升，推理时延较参数生成方法快 165–228 倍。

Conclusion: RecSoup 以轻量模型合并方式在效果与效率上优于参数生成范式，为 CMOR 提供无需联合重训的可控方案。

Abstract: Recommender systems often need to balance multiple conflicting objectives. Controllable Multi-Objective Recommendation (CMOR) enables dynamic adjustment of objective trade-offs at inference time without retraining, adapting to shifting user or system requirements. Existing CMOR approaches typically rely on output-level reranking or parameter-generating modules, which either limit the scope of feasible trade-offs or incur substantial training and inference cost. Model merging provides a promising alternative by reusing multiple objective-specific models via parameter combination. However, its direct application to recommender systems is challenging due to the lack of a shared linear regime and cross-objective interference. To address these issues, we propose RecSoup, a lightweight two-stage approach for effective CMOR via specialized model merging. RecSoup first mitigates parameter interference across objectives through a pruning strategy that jointly considers objective-specific importance and cross-objective relevance. It then performs adaptive parameter merging via a learnable preference-to-coefficient mapping, allowing dynamic trade-off control at inference time without joint retraining or additional generative modules. Theoretically, we prove that RecSoup outperforms the parameter generation paradigm in effectiveness and efficiency, as it can fully navigate the Pareto front without smoothness constraints. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that RecSoup achieves improved Pareto front quality and effective controllability while delivers 165--228x faster inference-time latency than parameter generation approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/35798642/RECSOUP.git.

</details>


### [2] [Aligning LLMs with Sparse Retrieval via Adaptive Sparse Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21877)
*Tongna Chen,Minghan Li,Guodong Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的稀疏检索框架，采用动态α截断的输入自适应稀疏对齐，在MS MARCO与6个BEIR数据集上优于SPLADE++，相比Lion-sp索引存储降低6.4倍、端到端查询延迟降低11倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM语义表示强但用于一阶段检索延迟与存储成本高；SPLADE等神经稀疏检索器高效但编码器容量有限，表征能力受限。目标是缩小二者差距。

Method: 动态α截断策略：依据SPLADE表示的累积激活质量，为每个输入选择特定的监督支持集（不依赖固定支持预算），引导LLM保留显著词汇信号、抑制低信息激活；随后进行检索微调，结合对比学习、知识蒸馏与稀疏正则化。

Result: 在MS MARCO及6个BEIR数据集上优于SPLADE++，与LLM稀疏检索器效果相当；相比Lion-sp，索引存储减少6.4倍，端到端查询延迟降低逾11倍。

Conclusion: 输入自适应稀疏对齐可使LLM在保持检索有效性的同时显著降低存储与延迟，实现良好的效果—效率权衡。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) offer strong semantic representations, their application to first-stage retrieval incurs substantial latency and storage costs. Neural sparse retrievers such as SPLADE provide efficient retrieval but rely on relatively compact encoders with limited representational capacity. To bridge this gap, we propose an LLM-based sparse retrieval framework with input-adaptive sparse alignment. Specifically, we introduce a dynamic $α$-truncation strategy that selects input-specific supervision supports based on the cumulative activation mass of SPLADE representations. This strategy guides the LLM to preserve salient lexical signals while suppressing less informative activations, without relying on a fixed support budget. Subsequent retrieval fine-tuning combines contrastive learning, knowledge distillation, and sparsity regularization to improve retrieval effectiveness. Experiments on MS MARCO and six selected BEIR datasets demonstrate improvements over SPLADE++ while maintaining competitive effectiveness against LLM-based sparse retrievers. Compared with Lion-sp, our method reduces index storage by 6.4-fold and end-to-end query latency by over 11-fold, achieving a favorable effectiveness-efficiency trade-off.

</details>

### [3] [The World Won't Wait: Evaluating and Teaching Agents Under a Live Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20665)
*Ningxin Pan,Hanyu Li,Yeqing Shen,Ning Ding,Yehui Tang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RealtimeBench评测实时环境下的LLM智能体，并给出双时间尺度离线递归自我改进方法TORSI以提升快速学生策略的实时表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的推理与工具调用耗时较长，在持续变化的环境中，本可正确的动作可能因延迟而失效；现有评测多允许暂停游戏，掩盖了时延带来的性能损失。

Method: 构建RealtimeBench，含6类游戏、180个任务，游戏在推理期间持续运行，并进行暂停/不暂停的配对评测；提出TORSI：慢速教师在各次尝试间研究失败案例、在暂停游戏中验证修正方案并撰写可复用指令，快速学生在游戏过程中直接行动而不调用教师，依据验证分数决定是否采纳更新。

Result: 配对评测显示GPT-6.1-sol在暂停条件下优于更快的Jev，但在游戏持续运行时落后；两轮TORSI将Jev在0–100进度量表上的平均测试分从13.51提升至24.19，六类游戏均提升；生成的指令在无需调参的情况下也提升了另外三个模型的平均分。

Conclusion: 结果表明实时智能的关键在于跨时间尺度组织推理、学习与行动，使强LLM智能体在持续变化的世界中保持有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can solve complex interactive tasks, yet reasoning and tool calls take time. In continuously changing environments, an otherwise correct action may arrive too late. We introduce RealtimeBench, with 180 tasks across six game types, to evaluate agents while games continue running during inference. Paired evaluations show that GPT-6.1-sol outperforms the faster Jev when games are paused, but falls behind when they keep running. We propose Two-Timescale Offline Recursive Self-Improvement (TORSI): a slow teacher studies failures and tests corrections in paused games between attempts, then writes reusable instructions for a fast student. The student acts without teacher calls during gameplay, and validation scores determine which updates are adopted. Two rounds raise Jev's average test score from 13.51 to 24.19 on a 0--100 progress scale, improving all six game types. The instructions also improve average scores for three other models without retuning. These findings point toward real-time intelligence: enabling powerful LLM agents to remain effective in a continuously changing world by organizing deliberation, learning, and action across timescales.

</details>


### [4] [Evaluating Model Capabilities After Benchmark Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20667)
*Morio Hamada,Jehyeok Yeon,Matteo Migliarini,Justin Olive,Magda Dubois*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基准分数饱和后，其正确解答的完整转录（transcripts）仍蕴含能力信号：配对评判（pairwise judging）在模拟饱和实验中把预测误差降至仅用饱和分数的1/5.3，但该类信号在分布偏移下泛化不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI能力快速提升导致基准趋于饱和（多模型逼近满分）从而丧失区分度，削弱其对安全论证与前沿政策的支撑价值；而基准条目实际产生的信息远多于一个标量分数，即模型解题的完整转录，作者希望检验这些转录能否在分数饱和后继续用于能力评估。

Method: 用历史基准与模型版本回溯式模拟饱和：仅向协议提供饱和基准上"正确解答"的转录，考察其能否预测在未饱和的留出（held-out）条目上测得的能力；比较三类代表性信息抽取协议——配对评判、单转录绝对打分、以及基于确定性转录特征或嵌入的预测。

Result: 饱和转录保留超出基准准确率的能力信息；在所评协议中配对评判最优，相较仅用饱和基准分数的基线将预测误差降低5.3倍；该能力相关信息分布于各转录片段，但集中于推理与工具调用等特定信息通道；协议预测力在分布偏移下并不总是泛化（例如低估2025年初首批推理模型的能力）。

Conclusion: 饱和基准的任务转录中仍存在可预测能力的信号，但要以之延长现有基准的有效寿命，需要专门设计、能在分布偏移下稳健抽取此类信号的方法。

Abstract: Rapid advances in AI capabilities are causing benchmarks to saturate. Once several models approach perfect scores, a benchmark loses its power to differentiate between them, and with it its utility for informing AI safety cases and frontier policies. Yet benchmark items produce far more information than the scores we typically extract from them: a full transcript of how each model solved the task. We investigate whether these transcripts can be used to assess model capabilities even after benchmark scores have saturated. We simulate saturation retrospectively using historical benchmark and model releases, and test whether protocols that access only correctly solved transcripts from saturated benchmarks can predict capabilities measured on held-out, unsaturated items. We evaluate representative contemporary approaches for extracting information from model transcripts: pairwise judging, single-transcript absolute scoring, and predictions from deterministic transcript features or embeddings. We find that saturated transcripts retain capability information beyond benchmark accuracy. Amongst our evaluated protocols, pairwise judging performs best, reducing prediction error by 5.3 times compared to a baseline using only saturated benchmark scores. This capability-relevant information is distributed across transcript sections, but concentrated in specific channels of information such as reasoning and tool-calls. However, the predictive power of protocols does not always generalise across distribution shifts -- for example by underestimating the capabilities of the first reasoning models introduced in early 2025. Saturated benchmarks thus retain capability-predicting signal in their task transcripts, but extending the lifetime of existing benchmarks requires methods designed to extract these signals robustly.

</details>


### [5] [Value-Informed Schedule Trimming for Accelerated Sequential Monte Carlo Guidance in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20669)
*Jaehyeon Kim,Ziseok Lee,Siyoung Choi,Kyungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VISTA：一种免训练方法，通过动态规划在固定预算下自动选择离散扩散模型的稀疏引导时间步，逼近全引导概率路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励引导的离散扩散可在测试时对齐下游目标，但每步都用SMC引导计算代价高；现有稀疏引导调度是固定启发式，跨任务效果差异大。

Method: 将稀疏引导建模为对理想全引导概率路径的近似，推导可估计的ℓ1路径偏差上界；用少量全引导热启动生成收集价值统计量，据此构造经验替代目标并用动态规划精确求解最优引导步集合。

Result: 在文本、生物序列和图像生成任务上自动发现任务相关调度，多数设置优于手工稀疏调度，部分指标接近或超过全引导；毒性文本生成中唯一毒性比0.99（全引导1.00），单代延迟从204.23 s降至38.16 s，加速5.35倍。

Conclusion: VISTA以训练-free方式实现预算约束下的自适应稀疏引导，选择后每次生成复杂度与同等预算的稀疏调度相同，兼顾对齐效果与效率。

Abstract: Reward-guided discrete diffusion enables test-time alignment with downstream objectives, but applying Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) guidance at every denoising step is computationally expensive. Active guidance steps require additional reward evaluations and, for gradient-based proposals, value-gradient computations. Sparse guidance reduces this cost by guiding only a subset of timesteps, but commonly used schedules are fixed heuristics whose effectiveness can vary substantially across tasks. We propose VISTA, a training-free method that automatically selects guided timesteps under a fixed budget. VISTA formulates sparse guidance as approximation of the ideal full-guidance probability path and derives an estimable upper bound on \(\ell_1\) path deviation. Using value statistics collected from a small number of full-guidance warmup generations, VISTA optimizes the resulting empirical surrogate exactly via dynamic programming. The warmup generations can be retained as model outputs, and after schedule selection VISTA has the same per-generation guidance complexity as other sparse schedules with the same budget. Across text, biological sequence, and image generation tasks, VISTA discovers task-dependent schedules and outperforms hand-designed sparse schedules in most tested settings, while approaching or exceeding full-guidance performance on several task metrics. In toxic-text generation, for example, VISTA attains a 0.99 unique-toxic ratio compared with 1.00 for full guidance while reducing post-selection per-generation latency from 204.23 s to 38.16 s, a 5.35$\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [6] [AlphaDiana: A Measurement System for Agents in Verifiable Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20675)
*Zhanke Zhou,Zongze Li,Weikai Huang,Xuan Li,Chentao Cao,Xiao Feng,Xiangyu Lu,Jinbo Hu,Yi Xie,Nico Pelleriti,Shiyang Liu,Menghan Lu,Max Zimmer,Brando Miranda,Jiangchao Yao,Bo Liu,Sanmi Koyejo,Sebastian Pokutta,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AlphaDiana 测量系统，通过模块化接口将执行条件与结果、行为、资源使用关联，发现 harness 及组件干预（工具/技能/记忆）并不能稳定提升推理准确率，且行为与 token 使用须结合正确性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解 agent 性能需要将执行条件与其结果、行为和资源使用联系起来，但缺乏能够在受控条件下系统测量完整 agent 及各组件贡献的工具。

Method: AlphaDiana 为数据集、agent、环境、评分器提供模块化接口，保留 harness 原生控制循环，同时分别控制文件复用与记忆保留；支持对完整 agent 的宏观分析，以及在匹配条件下对工具、技能、记忆等组件的微观干预。实验覆盖 3 个开源模型、3 个开源 harness、5 个基于答案的基准，其中 2 个模型另在 2 个交互式基准上测试。

Result: harness 不能稳定提升推理准确率：对模型单独无法解答问题的增益可能被其无工具即可正确回答问题的损失所抵消；技能引导与保留历史同样不能可靠提升准确率。低 token 熵可伴随错误答案；准确率下降时，单次执行输出更少可能意味着每个正确答案消耗更多输出 token。

Conclusion: AlphaDiana 将答案正确性与执行状态关联到轨迹和资源记录，可在共享任务与资源策略下比较 agent 并诊断失败，表明 agent 评估须同时考虑正确性、行为与资源使用。

Abstract: Agents pair reasoning models with harnesses that organize model calls, execute tools, load skill instructions, and retain history in memory. Understanding agent performance requires linking execution conditions to outcomes, behavior, and resource use. We introduce AlphaDiana, a measurement system with modular interfaces for datasets, agents, environments, and scorers. It preserves harness-native control loops while controlling file reuse and memory retention separately. AlphaDiana supports macro analyses of complete agents and micro interventions on agent components such as tools, skills, and memory under matched conditions. We study three open-source models and three open-source harnesses on five answer-based benchmarks, with two models also tested on two interactive benchmarks. We find that harnesses do not consistently improve the accuracy of reasoning: gains on questions unsolved by the model alone can be outweighed by losses on questions it answers correctly without tools. This inconsistency extends to component interventions: neither skill guidance nor retained history reliably improves accuracy. Behavior and output-token use must be assessed with correctness: low token entropy can accompany wrong answers, and less output per execution can mean more output tokens per correct answer when accuracy falls. AlphaDiana links answer correctness and execution status to traces and resource records for comparing agents and diagnosing failures under shared task and resource policies.

</details>


### [7] [When Correctness Feedback Erases Evidence: Phase-Separated Completeness Auditing in Verus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20676)
*Adarsh Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文在12个Verus/Rust任务、四类模型家族上发现：无反馈独立搜索比"纠错反馈循环"（IntentShield）更能暴露"满足书面契约却违反意图"的认证见证，产量59.0% vs 27.1%；保留这些失败见证反而显著提升规格修复效果（81.3% vs 62.5%）。该效应在Dafny/Lean上未复现，界定了Verus/Rust工作流的经验边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规格完备性审计的目标是找出"通过书面契约但违背设计意图"的程序；然而常见的正确性反馈机制会抑制这类反例证据，形成"目标逆转"（objective reversal）——修复正确性与审计完备性目标相互对立。

Method: 在12个Verus/Rust任务、四个模型家族、匹配调用预算下对比：无反馈独立发现（no-feedback discovery） vs IntentShield纠错反馈循环；加入格式匹配的迭代对照以分离"反馈内容"与"独立重启"的贡献；进行解析器修复稳健性检验；另设阶段分离执行（phase-separated execution，不复用回复）；并做保留见证后的规格修复评测（Phase-Revised / IS-Revised / Direct-Revised）及Dafny、Lean的外部复现尝试。

Result: 无反馈发现认证见证产量59.0%，是纠错反馈（27.1%）的两倍以上；格式匹配对照表明差距主要来自反馈内容而非独立重启；优势在各模型家族均成立并通过解析器修复；阶段分离执行在不复用回复情况下达到相当的总产量；多数认证见证仍在留出用例上失败；规格修复成功率Phase-Revised 81.3%、IS-Revised 62.5%，Direct-Revised接近前者，且两类无反馈见证来源均显著优于弱化契约；Dafny与Lean评估未复现该效应。

Conclusion: 完备性审计与正确性修复的即时目标相反；应先保留并认证已承认的失败见证，再进行修复。该结论仅在所研究的Verus/Rust工作流中经验成立，不可外推至Dafny/Lean。

Abstract: Correctness feedback suppresses the very evidence that specification-completeness auditing is designed to uncover: programs that satisfy a written contract but violate intended behavior. We demonstrate this objective reversal on 12 Verus/Rust tasks across four model families under matched call budgets. Independent no-feedback discovery more than doubles certified-witness yield relative to IntentShield's corrective-feedback loop (59.0% versus 27.1%). A format-matched iterative control places the larger share of this gap in feedback content, not independent restarts. The advantage holds in every model family and survives parser repair. A separate phase-separated execution matches the aggregate yield without reusing responses; most certified witnesses also fail held-out cases. Preserving these witnesses delivers stronger specification repair: Phase-Revised contracts achieve 81.3% implementation success, versus 62.5% for IS-Revised, with Direct-Revised close behind. Both no-feedback witness sources substantially outperform weakened contracts. The effect does not reproduce in the available Dafny and Lean evaluations, establishing an empirical boundary around the studied Verus/Rust workflow. Completeness auditing and correctness repair have opposing immediate objectives. Preserve and certify the admitted failure before repairing it.

</details>


### [8] [Counterfactual Neighborhood Risk Fields: Evidence-Grounded Selective Safety for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20679)
*Tianshi Wang,Jingsong Wang,Fengling Li,Jingjing Li,Xin Li,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CNRF：以“上下文匹配的良性-有害反事实对”的表示差异作为风险方向，并将邻域支持度与风险分离估计，实现VLM的选择性安全干预而非整体拒答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于表示的VLM安全防御只学“有害/良性”的可分边界，但可分性本身无法解释风险分数的含义及其可信条件，且易把“不熟悉的输入”误判为“有害”，导致过度拒答、损失良性任务效用。

Method: 1) 构建上下文匹配的良性-有害反事实对，以其表示差作为与模型能力相关的风险方向；2) 将邻域支持度（输入是否处于已知分布/熟悉区域）与风险大小分别估计，避免陌生性与有害性混淆；3) 设计受害者自适应（victim-adaptive）接口以适配不同VLM架构的安全观测；4) 对被选中的请求进行同模型响应复核，或放行可接受候选，或生成有害能力受约束的替代回答。

Result: 在3个VLM与5类多模态越狱族上取得86.06–89.30%的宏平均防御成功率，同时保留可观的良性任务效用；控制实验表明，查询匹配的参照（query-matched references）与保持上下文-方向对应关系能显著改善低假阳性区间内的风险排序。

Conclusion: CNRF将基于表示的VLM安全从“学习有害性边界”重构为“为选择性风险决策构建可适用证据”，强调风险分数的可解释性与可信域，兼顾安全与效用。

Abstract: Representation-based safety defenses for vision-language models (VLMs) often learn to separate harmful from benign inputs, but separation alone does not establish what a risk score means or when it should be trusted. We introduce Counterfactual Neighborhood Risk Fields (CNRF), an evidence-grounded framework for selective VLM safety. CNRF constructs context-matched benign-harmful counterfactual pairs, uses their representation differences as capability-related risk directions, and estimates neighborhood support separately from risk. This separation prevents unfamiliarity from being conflated with harmfulness and enables selective intervention rather than blanket rejection. A victim-adaptive interface accommodates different safety observations across VLM architectures and routes selected requests for same-victim response review, which either releases acceptable candidates or produces constrained alternatives with reduced harmful capability. Across three VLMs and five multimodal jailbreak families, CNRF achieves 86.06-89.30% macro defense success rate while retaining substantial benign-task utility. Controlled experiments further show that query-matched references and preserved context-direction correspondence materially improve risk ranking in the low-false-positive regime. CNRF thus reframes representation-based VLM safety from learning harmfulness boundaries to constructing applicable evidence for selective risk decisions.

</details>


### [9] [IneqAgent: Agentic Inequality Proving in Lean with Symbolic Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20681)
*Gaolei He,Jinhui Li,Ruobing Zuo,Xia Zeng,Zhengfeng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IneqAgent：LLM 推理 + 符号计算的 Lean4 不等式证明智能体框架，以引理级 MCTS 协调引理构造、目标重写与符号后端选择，配合 SoS/Bernstein/逐次差分代换后端生成精确证书，在四个基准上最高解出 175/186（94.09%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化不等式证明既需数学推理又需精确计算，二者通常依赖中间引理与目标重写来衔接，纯 LLM 或纯符号方法都难以单独完成完整 Lean4 证明。

Method: 提出 IneqAgent 智能体框架：在 Lean4 中进行引理级蒙特卡洛树搜索，联合搜索引理构造、目标重写与符号后端选择；三个互补后端（平方和 SoS、Bernstein、逐次差分代换 SDS）产出精确证书以生成本地 Lean 证明；再由 LLM 生成步骤并与证书集成，利用 Lean 反馈迭代，最终对照原定理陈述校验完整证明。

Result: 四个基准上，IneqAgent 配 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 解出 186 题中的 175 题（94.09%），为所评估方法中总体成功率最高；在四个骨干模型上，于低推理成本阈值处成功率高于同骨干的 Direct Agent，且各方法成功运行的平均推理成本更低。另获 Dittert 猜想在 4、5 维的 Lean 验证强化版本，并证明 Holens–Djokovic (4,4) 不等式，从而推出 4 维 Merris 猜想。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 推理与符号精确计算经引理级搜索耦合，是可行且高效的形式化不等式证明路径，兼顾高成功率、低推理成本与可验证的数学新成果。

Abstract: Formal inequality proving requires mathematical reasoning and exact computation, often connected through intermediate lemmas and goal reformulations. We introduce IneqAgent, an agentic framework combining LLM reasoning with symbolic computation for inequality proving in Lean4. Lemma-level Monte Carlo Tree Search coordinates lemma construction, goal reformulation, and symbolic backend selection. Complementary sum-of-squares, Bernstein, and successive difference-substitution backends produce exact certificates for local Lean proof generation. The agent integrates these proofs with LLM-generated steps and uses Lean feedback to construct complete proofs checked against the original theorem statements. On four benchmarks, IneqAgent with DeepSeek-V4-Flash solves 175 of 186 problems (94.09%), achieving the highest overall success rate among the evaluated methods. Across four backbones, it achieves higher success rates at low LLM inference cost thresholds than Direct Agent using the same backbone, with lower mean inference costs over each method's successful runs. We also obtain Lean-verified strengthenings of Dittert's conjecture in dimensions four and five, and certify the Holens--Djokovic $(4,4)$ inequality, implying Merris's conjecture in dimension four.

</details>


### [10] [L2G-SR: Learning to Guide LLM-based Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20685)
*Letian Huang,Xiangyu Wang,Haowei Lin,Di He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出L2G-SR框架，用学习到的结构验证器（基于树编辑距离的结构监督）为LLM符号回归提供结构引导，在LLM-SRBench上取得数值拟合、方程级准确率与TED的最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归搜索能解释观测数据的方程，但仅用数值误差指导搜索时，容易偏向"数值精确但结构错误"的替代式（surrogate），而非结构正确的候选式。因此需要引入结构层面的引导信号。

Method: 1) 用树编辑距离(TED)构建结构监督信号，并考虑交换律算子与等价表达形式；2) 训练验证器：先将数值观测与公式对齐，再学习对候选式与目标公式的结构相似度打分；3) 搜索阶段隐藏目标公式，验证器与数值误差互补用于父代选择(parent selection)；4) 分析师模块基于验证器反馈与诊断工具提出修改，生成器将其转为精炼表达式。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench上，跨多个LLM骨干与多个领域，L2G-SR在数值拟合、方程级准确率(equation-level accuracy)与TED三项指标上均达到state-of-the-art。

Conclusion: 结果表明学习到的结构引导对基于LLM的符号回归具有显著价值，可弥补纯数值误差指导的不足。

Abstract: Symbolic regression searches for an equation that explains observed data. Numerical error is commonly used to guide the search, but it alone can favor an accurate surrogate over a candidate with the right structure. We propose L2G-SR, a framework for LLM-based symbolic regression that guides search with a learned structural verifier. We construct structural supervision signal using tree edit distance (TED), accounting for commutative operators and alternative expression forms. A verifier first aligns numerical observations with the formulas, then learns to score a candidate's structural similarity to the target formula. At search time, the target formula is hidden and the verifier complements numerical error in parent selection. An analyst then uses verifier feedback and diagnostic tools to propose revisions, which a generator converts into refined expressions. On LLM-SRBench, L2G-SR achieves state-of-the-art numerical fit, equation level accuracy and TED across the evaluated LLM backbones and domains. L2G-SR demonstrates the value of learned structural guidance for LLM-based symbolic regression.

</details>


### [11] [When Reality Outpaces Cognition: A New Scaling Principle for Reliable Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20764)
*Ahsan Adeel,Talha Bin Riaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"有效连贯认知速度"\(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\) 作为理论与模型无关的新基准，衡量智能系统在观测-现实差距扩大时保持行为可靠的最快现实变化速度；实验显示受细胞机制启发的 \(Co^4\) 架构显著优于 Transformer 与门控 MLP 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 范式与新兴世界模型的理解能力尚未被系统检验。作者提出一个根本性问题：现实变化快到什么程度，智能系统就无法可靠地感知、推理与行动。需要一个可操作、可比较的度量来刻画这一极限。

Method: 将问题形式化为与理论、模型均无关的挑战：系统需在逐渐扩大的观测-现实差距下为越来越远的未来状态推断可靠动作。以"有效连贯认知速度"\(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\) 作为操作化指标，即系统从基线出发、持续满足预定义行为可靠性准则的最高测试"现实速度"\(S_R\)。概念验证采用强化学习智能体：仅在基线 \(S_R\) 训练，随后不重训、固定算力，将 \(S_R\) 提升至最高 50 倍进行评估；对比 Transformer、门控 MLP 基线及受细胞机制（与意识加工和清醒思维相关）启发的 \(Co^4\) 架构。

Result: 在所有测试场景中，\(Co^4\) 取得的 \(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\) 显著高于 Transformer 与门控 MLP 基线。

Conclusion: \(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\) 可作为衡量智能系统连贯认知能力的新基准；"连贯认知的可扩展性"可作为构建可靠 AI 的设计原则。

Abstract: This paper challenges the understanding capabilities of existing AI paradigms and emerging world models by asking a fundamental question: How quickly can reality change before an intelligent system can no longer reliably perceive, reason, and act? We formulate this as a theory- and model-agnostic challenge in which systems must infer reliable actions for increasingly distant future states across a growing observation--reality gap. We operationalize this challenge through the effective Speed of Coherent Cognition (\(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\)), defined as the highest tested Speed of Reality (\(S_R\)) through which a system continuously satisfies a predefined behavioural reliability criterion from baseline. As a proof of concept, reinforcement-learning agents are trained at the baseline \(S_R\) and evaluated, without retraining, as \(S_R\) is increased by up to \(50\times\) while computational resources remain fixed. We compare Transformer and gated-MLP baselines with \(Co^4\), an architecture inspired by cellular mechanisms implicated in conscious processing and awake thought. Across the tested scenarios, \(Co^4\) achieves substantially higher \(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\) than the baselines, motivating \(S_C^{\mathrm{eff}}\) as a new benchmark and the scaling of coherent cognition as a principle for reliable AI.

</details>


### [12] [ARIA: Runtime Inspection and Amendment for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20780)
*Bolin Shen,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARIA：将执行验证过的修复转化为可复用监督数据，训练紧凑模型在多智能体系统运行过程中实时识别并修复局部错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统中局部错误会随其他智能体逐步叠加而传播，把可纠正的错误放大为任务失败；现有的"事后诊断"或"反复重执行搜索修正"方式无法提供可在执行中途介入的学习型策略。

Method: 核心思路是把修复搜索前移到数据构建与训练阶段。首先采集四大MAS框架、六个基准、八种宿主模型上的24,000余条成功/失败轨迹，构建ARIA数据集——据称首个将失败多智能体轨迹与执行验证修复配对的大规模数据集，含3,953对验证修复，每对标注"错在何处、在何处介入、后续执行如何继续"。随后提出与具体MAS无关的运行时检查与修订框架，可接入任何暴露执行轨迹并支持"续写替换"的MAS；以验证修复为训练样本，教会一个紧凑模型判断何时需要介入以及如何纠正正在进行的运行，目标是学到可迁移的修复策略而非复现单条修正。

Result: 训练后的ARIA模型在事后（post-hoc）与运行时两种设定下均表现强修复性能，并能泛化到未见的框架与宿主模型。

Conclusion: 运行时在线修复可被建模为可学习问题，执行验证的修复数据足以支撑策略学习；ARIA提供了数据集、框架与模型，为多智能体系统的在线纠错提供可复用范式。

Abstract: In LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS), a local mistake can propagate as other agents build on it, turning a correctable error into a failed task. Diagnosing completed runs or repeatedly re-executing them to search for corrections does not provide a learned policy for intervening while execution is still in progress. We present Agent Runtime Inspection and Amendment (ARIA), a framework for learning when and how to repair ongoing multi-agent executions. Our key intuition is to turn execution-verified repairs into reusable supervision, moving repair search into data construction and training so that a learned model can intervene at runtime without repeatedly testing alternatives. First, we collect over 24{,}000 successful and failed trajectories across four multi-agent frameworks, six benchmarks, and eight host models. From these trajectories, we construct the ARIA dataset, to our knowledge the first large-scale dataset pairing failed multi-agent trajectories with execution-verified repairs. It contains 3{,}953 verified repair pairs, each specifying what went wrong, where to intervene, and how execution should continue. Then, we develop the ARIA framework for MAS-agnostic runtime inspection and amendment, designed to integrate with any MAS that exposes execution trajectories and supports continuation replacement. Using verified repairs as training examples, we teach a compact model to recognize when intervention is needed and how to correct an ongoing run. The aim is to learn repair strategies that apply to related failures in new configurations, rather than merely reproduce individual corrections. Finally, the trained ARIA model demonstrates strong repair performance in both post-hoc and runtime settings, with generalization to unseen frameworks and host models. Our code is available at https://github.com/LabRAI/ARIA.

</details>


### [13] [Open-world AI: a Tale of Three Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20782)
*Jianyu Zhang,Léon Bottou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文区分"封闭世界"与"开放世界"两种设定，提出开放世界下机器必须具备三项性质：推理时学习、丰富表征、预测性解耦，并由此预测一致的内层学习器与长上下文相关任务累积的益处，并在最多8B的语言模型上做定性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 封闭世界只需专门能力，任务criterion固定、数据iid可无限采集；开放世界要求通用性——用少量样本、极少人工先验去接手机器未被设计过的任务，且数据按组呈现而非打乱，任务事先未知。因此需要重新推导机器应具备的性质，而非仅追求iid泛化。

Method: 将任务建模为共享潜在因子，分解训练目标，推导出三项要求：推理时学习、保留超出iid泛化所需信息的丰富表征、使输入输出对齐为匹配低维组的预测性解耦；分析进一步预测一致内层学习器与长上下文的益处。在最多8B参数的语言模型上做定性（是否具备性质）而非定量（胜出多少）的检验。

Result: 满足上述要求的模型，随相关任务数据累积而性能提升；不满足者性能退化，且扩大规模或层类型交错均无法修复。

Conclusion: 开放世界通用性可由潜在因子共享假设下的目标分解加以刻画，其要求具可检验性；规模与架构交错不是充分条件，表征性质与推理时学习机制才是关键。

Abstract: The closed world asks AI for specialized competence and lets it manufacture the iid data its theory assumes: a fixed criterion licenses collecting examples until the task is covered. The open world asks for versatility --- taking up tasks a machine was not built for, from few examples and with little prior from a human designer --- and offers data that is grouped, not shuffled. Its tasks are not known in advance, so a machine must do inference-time learning (1st requirement). Modeling tasks as sharing latent factors and decomposing the training objective, we derive two more: a *rich representation*, retaining more than iid generalization requires, and a predictive disentanglement that aligns inputs and outputs into matched low-dimensional groups. The analysis further predicts the need for a consistent inner learner, and the benefit of long contexts spanning related tasks. We test these claims on language models up to 8B, asking whether a model has a property (qualitative) rather than how much it wins (quantitative). Models that meet the requirements improve as data from related tasks accumulates; the rest degrade, and neither scale nor layer-type interleaving repairs this.

</details>


### [14] [EvolvingBrain: An Adaptive Skill System for Multi-modal Agents Self-Evolution in Open Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20786)
*Runxuan Liu,Jingqi Shi,Baoxin Wang,Honghong Zhao,Haoyuan Xu,Yueqing Sun,Jiafeng Liang,Tao He,Dayong Wu,Qi Gu,Shijin Wang,Ming Liu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EvolvingBrain，用自适应技能系统作为智能体自我进化的大脑，含分层技能自进化与状态感知自适应执行两部分，在开放世界任务上提升成功率并具泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体与开放世界交互已有进展，但自我进化能力有限：监督微调只能模仿、无法主动组织经验；基于结果的强化学习促进探索却未充分利用中间轨迹信息。

Method: 两大组件：(1) 分层技能自进化——从模型特定轨迹中抽取过程模式与问题模式，通过生成技能总结经验，并在与开放世界多次交互中进化，形成由领域、规划、动作/陷阱技能组成的分层技能架构，提供多粒度知识库与新的进化方式；(2) 状态感知自适应执行——将长程交互总结为结构化抽象记忆，据此引导状态感知技能融合策略，使智能体按当前状态调用知识。

Result: 在 VAB-Minecraft 与 DiscoveryWorld 上实验表明，EvolvingBrain 提升了开放世界任务的成功率，并展现出泛化能力。代码随补充材料提供，正式版将公开。

Conclusion: 以分层技能架构取代纯模仿或纯结果奖励，可有效利用中间轨迹经验实现智能体自我进化，并按状态自适应复用技能，从而提升开放世界任务表现与泛化性。

Abstract: Multi-modal agents have made significant progress in interacting with open worlds. However, their ability to self-evolve remains limited. Supervised fine-tuning enables imitation but not active experience organization, while outcome-based reinforcement learning promotes exploration yet underuses intermediate trajectory information. Therefore, we propose EvolvingBrain, which uses an adaptive skill system as the brain for agent self-evolution, consisting of two following components. Stratified Skill Self-Evolution: extracts Procedural Patterns and Problematic Patterns from model-specific trajectories, summarizes experience by generating skills, and evolves through multiple interactions with the open world, ultimately forming a Stratified Skill Architecture consisting of Domain, Plan, and Action/Trap Skills, which provides the agent with a multi-granularity knowledge repository and a novel evolution approach. State-Aware Adaptive Execution: summarizes long-horizon interactions into Structured Abstractive Memory and uses it to guide the State-Aware Skill Fusion Strategy, enabling the agent to utilize knowledge according to its current state. Experiments on VAB-Minecraft and DiscoveryWorld demonstrate that EvolvingBrain improves success rates on open-world tasks and exhibits generalization capabilities. Our code is provided in the supplementary materials and will be publicly released in the camera-ready version.

</details>


### [15] [When Reasoning Traces Conflict with Evidence: How Answer Configuration Shapes Evidence Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20805)
*Shaoang Li,Yanhang Shi,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文区分了推理轨迹（reasoning trace）在答题时的两种作用——“受益于轨迹”与“从轨迹退让（defer to it）”，并证明二者是可分离的性质；作者主张评测应同时报告两种性质及答题配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保留至答题时刻的推理轨迹既可能提升准确率，又可能在轨迹与当前证据冲突时压过证据。既有研究未区分这两种作用，且仅靠“删除轨迹”无法判断答案究竟服从了哪个信息源，因此需要解耦并量化。

Method: 将已验证的证据版本与由各版本生成的轨迹做交叉配对，在两种配置下用相同轨迹与证据作答：继续模型自身思考（continuation）与独立答案调用（separate answer call）。实验覆盖三个模型家族（gpt-oss、Gemma4、Qwen3）与 HotpotQA、MuSiQue 两个数据集，并辅以消融分析（轨迹末行价值、金标证据介入时机、干扰项影响、重新推理 vs. 验证指令）。

Result: 独立答案调用相较接续思考将“遵循证据”提升 29.9–60.6 个百分点；同样的数据对上，删除轨迹在两种配置下均使准确率下降相近幅度。然而在多数冲突情形中，接续思考会重复陈旧结论，独立调用则显著更常遵循当前证据。分析还显示：轨迹的最后一行承载了其大部分价值；金标证据在轨迹写出之前介入收益最大；干扰项会削弱独立调用下的纠错；与“验证指令”不同，“重新推理”在多数冲突中能遵循当前证据。

Conclusion: “受益于轨迹”与“退让于轨迹”是两种不同的属性。由于单独删除轨迹无法揭示答案来源，评测应同时报告这两类性质，并明确说明答题配置（continuation 或独立调用）。

Abstract: A reasoning trace retained at answer time plays two roles: it can raise accuracy, and it can compete with the current evidence when the two disagree. We disentangle these roles by crossing verified evidence versions with the traces generated from each and answering from the same traces and evidence under two configurations: continuation of the model's own thinking and a separate answer call. Across three model families (gpt-oss, Gemma4, Qwen3) on HotpotQA and MuSiQue, the separate call raises evidence-following over continuation by 29.9 to 60.6 points. On the same pairs, removing the trace lowers accuracy by similar amounts under both configurations. Yet continuation repeats the stale conclusion in most conflicts, and the separate call follows the current evidence far more often. Benefiting from a trace and deferring to it are therefore distinct properties. Further analyses show that the final line of a trace carries most of its value, gold evidence helps most before the trace is written, distractors weaken correction under a separate call, and renewed reasoning, unlike a verification instruction, follows the current evidence in most conflicts. Because trace deletion alone cannot reveal which source an answer follows, evaluations should report both properties together with the answer configuration.

</details>


### [16] [A Self-Improving Agent that Grows its Own DSL: Oracle-Free Operator Induction for Exact Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20809)
*Yun-Shiuan Chuang,Yirou Ge,Yixi Li,Yiming Bian,Zijie Pan,Jenna L. Ballard,Uma Kona,Linsey Pang,Prakhar Mehrotra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无需奖励、评委或答案键的自改进机制：冻结模型在精确DSL上运行内外两层循环，内循环靠两个独立表达式逐记录一致来作答，外循环自主发现缺失能力并发明算子，仅当第二独立实现一致且满足守恒律时接纳。内循环将可答问题集约扩大三倍，外循环可在不相交起始集上自扩展；接纳规则经验上可靠（138个错误算子变体全部拒绝），并在账本、临床问答案两个领域及跨模型家族上生效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进智能体以高奖励、说服评委模型或压缩技能库作为保留技能的依据，即使技能是错的也会被保留；但在需要答案严格正确的场景（如交易表求和）中正确性不可妥协。因此本文追问：智能体能否在没有奖励、评委和答案键的情况下扩展可计算能力且仍保证精确正确。

Method: 单个冻结模型在小型精确领域特定语言(DSL)上运行两个嵌套循环。内循环：仅当两个独立编写的表达式在每条记录上一致时才回答新问题。外循环：自主识别自身缺失的能力并为其编写算子，仅当独立第二实现一致且结果满足守恒律时接纳，全程无真值。另设无归纳对照组。

Result: 内循环将可答问题集约扩大三倍；外循环在不相交起始集上自扩展，发明了起始闭包之外的算子，而无归纳对照一个也答不出。接纳规则经验可靠：在独立参照下拒绝138/138个有缺陷算子变体（含不同模型家族编写的），而业界常用的"能跑即接纳"、自一致性、压缩信号却接纳了其中大多数。机制未经修改即在业务账本与临床问答案两个异构模式领域上自扩展，可答集分别扩大十倍以上和近二十倍。跨模型家族迁移时门控仍保持可靠，各家族自扩展程度相当，仅归纳产出与各提议者的DSL熟练度相关。

Conclusion: 在无奖励、无评委、无答案键条件下，通过双独立实现一致性与守恒律构成的双重把关，智能体可实现精确正确的可计算能力自扩展，该机制可靠、可跨领域与跨模型家族迁移，并能抵御业界常用弱信号所无法排除的算子缺陷。

Abstract: A self-improving agent has to decide which new skills to keep, and in practice it keeps whatever earns a high reward, convinces a judge model, or compresses its library, even when the skill is wrong. That is fine until the answers have to be exactly right: over a table of transactions, a total is either right or wrong. We ask a harder question: can an agent grow what it can compute with no reward, no judge, and no answer key, and still be exactly right? A single frozen model runs two nested loops over a small, exact domain-specific language (DSL). The inner loop answers new questions only when two independently written expressions agree on every record. The outer loop discovers its own missing capability and authors an operator for it, admitted only when an independent second implementation agrees and the result obeys a conservation law, all without ground truth. The inner loop roughly triples the answerable-question set, and the outer loop self-extends on disjoint starting sets, inventing operators outside the starting closure; a no-induction control answers none of the new questions. The admission rule is empirically sound: it rejects 138 of 138 buggy operator variants under an independent reference, even one written by a different model family, where the admit-if-it-runs, self-consistency, and compression signals the field relies on admit most of them. Unmodified, the same mechanism self-extends on two further domains with unrelated schemas, business-ledger and clinical-encounter question answering, growing their answerable sets more than tenfold and nearly twentyfold. The loop transfers across model families too: run with further proposers, the gate stays sound and independent families self-extend comparably, with only the induction yield tracking each proposer's DSL fluency.

</details>


### [17] [Temporal Specification Guidance for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20822)
*Avishree Khare,Hideki Okamoto,Bardh Hoxha,Georgios Fainekos,Rajeev Alur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出时序规范引导（TSG）：一种免训练、推理时的框架，用时序逻辑形式化刻画提示中的顺序、时序与持续性要求，并通过可微分定量语义的梯度构造去噪方向来引导视频生成，在220条提示基准上使规范满足率较CFG提升9.5–20个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到视频模型通常能生成提示中的视觉概念，但难以满足概念在时间上的顺序、时序与持续性要求；现有条件控制缺乏对时序约束的显式、可执行接口。

Method: 将时序需求表示为时序逻辑（文中实例化为度量时序逻辑MTL）规范；在采样过程中用可微分定量语义对视觉概念证据的轨迹打分，并利用分数梯度构造规范引导的去噪方向；实例中用视频-文本注意力轨迹作为证据、采用MTL的高效可微分评分、将梯度转化为注意力偏置，无需外部概念分类器、也无需对视频Transformer反向传播，其注意力引导前向结果用于增强无分类器引导（CFG）。

Result: 在覆盖11类时序约束（含有序物体进入、状态转换等）的220条提示基准上，于五个模型上规范满足准确率较CFG提升9.5–20个百分点，且内存开销极小。

Conclusion: 结果表明，时序规范与可微分满足度目标可作为控制视频生成的一种灵活接口。

Abstract: Text-to-video models often generate the visual concepts requested in natural language prompts but fail to satisfy their intended ordering, timing, and persistence requirements. We propose representing these requirements as specifications in a formal Temporal Logic, and introduce Temporal Specification Guidance (TSG), a training-free inference-time framework that turns temporal specifications into executable objectives for guiding video generation during sampling. TSG scores traces of visual concept evidence using differentiable quantitative semantics and uses the score's gradients to construct a specification-guided denoising direction. We instantiate this framework with video-text attention traces, efficient differentiable scores for Metric Temporal Logic, and gradient-derived attention biases. This instantiation requires neither an external concept classifier nor backpropagation through the video transformer. Its attention-steered forward pass yields a denoising direction that augments classifier-free guidance (CFG). On a benchmark of 220 prompts spanning 11 categories of temporal constraints, including ordered object entry and state transitions, this instantiation improves specification satisfaction accuracy over CFG by 9.5-20 percentage points across five models, with minimal memory overhead. These results demonstrate the potential of temporal specifications and differentiable satisfaction objectives as a flexible interface for controlling video generation.

</details>


### [18] [Confidence-Calibrated Inference Expansion for Evaluator-Guided Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20836)
*Weida Liang,Kenji Kawaguchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将评估器引导推理的每步决策建模为计算感知的三动作局部控制器（采纳/回退/扩展），给出动作差距分析与期望遗憾界，并学习校准动作级置信度，在保持效用的同时降低约16%–30%的在线计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估器引导推理（如过程评估/搜索）在每一步都面临局部控制问题：是信任当前答案、返回回退答案，还是投入更多推理计算。现有做法多将评估器视为"更强推理器"，缺乏对计算成本与决策不确定性的显式权衡与理论刻画。

Method: 构造计算感知的三动作控制器：终止类动作以预测答案成功率打分，扩展动作以基于rollout的延续成功率代理打分，并显式计入计算代价。理论层面给出局部动作差距分析（刻画打分误差何时会改变决策）与沿实际扩展路径的期望遗憾界。据此学习并校准动作特定置信度估计，并对模糊决策施加局部不确定性惩罚。

Result: 在留出的宽度扩展与修订任务上，控制器在观测效用上优于经校准选择的固定策略，同时将归一化在线计算分别降低约30%与16%。在四个Qwen3-8B随机种子上，两种设定下计算量均低于拟合Q策略，但效用差异较小且方向不一。

Conclusion: 评估器引导推理应被视为不确定性下的资源分配问题，而非普遍更强的推理器；收益主要来自计算节省而非效用提升。

Abstract: Evaluator-guided reasoning presents a local control problem at every step: trust the current answer, return a fallback, or spend more inference. We formulate this choice as a compute-aware three-action controller, scoring terminal actions by predicted answer success and expansion by a rollout-based continuation-success surrogate, with compute cost priced explicitly. A local action-gap analysis characterizes when score errors can change a decision, while an expected regret bound accounts for errors along the realized expansion path. Guided by this analysis, we learn and calibrate action-specific confidence estimates, with local uncertainty penalties for ambiguous decisions. On held-out width expansion and revision tasks, the controller improves observed utility over calibration-selected fixed policies while reducing normalized online compute by about 30% and 16%, respectively. Across four Qwen3-8B seeds, compute is lower than fitted Q in both settings, while utility differences are small and mixed. The results frame evaluator-guided reasoning as resource allocation under uncertainty, rather than a uniformly stronger reasoner.

</details>


### [19] [FDE-Gym: The Last Mile of AI Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22350)
*Dongxu Yang,Hongda Yan,Renxin Wang,Baoli Zhang,Haojia Zuo,Ying Zhao,Youmin Chen,Xiaojian Liao,Congming Gao,Tianyang Jiang,Yixiao Chen,Qing Wang,Zhiguo Yu,Xiaqing Li,Yuhao Zhang,Jiawen Sun,Renzhe Xu,De Mi,Tiancheng Wu,Xianya Fu,Xuanlu Zhao,Bo Feng,Meixuan Ren,Guanming Lin,Ziheng Zhou,Xinhua Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 FDE-Gym 基准：含 20 个客户场景、18 个行业的 95 个端到端交付任务，在带脚本化利益相关者与可修改系统的模拟客户环境中评估智能体。评分基于客户自身成功指标的归一化提升（0–1），并对交互成本（消息、试运行、等待时间）施以惩罚。13 个模型配置中最佳平均分 0.56，仅 14/95 任务达标；未明示约束的发现是主要瓶颈，但信息发现不足以解释差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准多在 issue、规范或工单界定良好的任务上评估，无法反映真实客户交付：需澄清模糊需求、在真实系统中作业并挖掘未明示约束。

Method: 构建 95 个由行业专家改写的真实交付任务，覆盖 20 个客户场景、18 个行业；在模拟环境中由脚本化利益相关者与可修改客户系统构成交互场景，智能体需与利益相关者沟通并在类生产系统上操作。指标为以客户自身成功指标衡量的归一化业务提升（0=系统不变，1=参考方案），并对沟通与试错成本扣分；另设在线平台供人类按同一标准练习。

Result: 评估 7 家厂商的 13 种模型配置：真实设定下最佳配置平均分 0.56，仅 14/95 任务达到 ≥0.8 且无事故；需无提示地发现未明示约束时性能显著下降；即便在最佳配置已从利益相关者获取全部关键事实的 23 个任务上，仅 10 个达到 ≥0.8 的归一化业务提升。

Conclusion: 解决界定良好的任务与为真实客户交付有效 AI 系统之间存在显著差距；约束发现是主要瓶颈，且仅靠信息获取仍不足以弥合差距，需提升需求澄清后转化为有效方案的端到端能力。

Abstract: Existing agent benchmarks typically evaluate agents on well-specified tasks defined by issue reports, specifications, or task tickets. Real-world customer delivery is different: forward-deployed engineers (FDEs) must clarify ambiguous requirements, work in live systems, and uncover unstated constraints. We introduce FDE-Gym, a benchmark of 95 end-to-end delivery tasks from 20 customer scenarios across 18 sectors, each adapted by industry experts from real-world delivery projects. In simulated customer environments with scripted stakeholders and modifiable customer systems, agents interact with stakeholders and operate on production-like systems to deliver working solutions. FDE-Gym scores a delivery by how much it improves the customer's own success metric, normalized between 0 (unchanged system) and 1 (reference solution), with penalties for stakeholder interactions (messages, trial runs, and waiting time). An online platform lets humans practice FDE skills under the same grading criteria. We evaluate 13 model configurations from seven providers. In the realistic setting, where unstated constraints must be discovered, the best configuration achieves a mean FDE-Gym score of 0.56 and resolves only 14 of the 95 tasks (a score of at least 0.8 with no incident). Discovering unstated constraints is the primary bottleneck: performance drops sharply when models must uncover them without explicit guidance. Yet information discovery alone does not account for the gap: even on the 23 tasks where the best configuration obtains every key fact from stakeholders, it reaches a normalized business uplift of 0.8 or above on only 10. These results reveal a substantial gap between solving well-specified tasks and delivering effective AI systems for real customers.

</details>


### [20] [Evolving Together, Failing Apart: The Skill-Policy Co-evolution Trap in Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20845)
*Yujie Feng,Xiaobin Wang,Jinluan Yang,Xiaonan Shi,Pengjun Xie,Philip S. Yu,Xiaoming Wu,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出技能-策略协同演化方法在部署后技能漂移下存在脆弱性盲点，并提出面向部署的评测协议CODA揭示该脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测仅在训练所用的最终技能库（delivered bank）上评估协同演化策略，而部署后技能可能比策略重训更频繁地被改写、增删，导致策略在漂移的技能库上运行，这一盲点未被训练目标或现有评测协议覆盖。

Method: 通过理论与实证分析证明该脆弱性源于各方法共享的"协作式接受"（cooperative acceptance）规则；提出CO-evolution Drift Audit（CODA）部署导向评测协议，模拟技能漂移，除最坏情况漂移下的成功率外，新增意义保持编辑下的一致性率与对损坏技能的抵抗率两项指标；在四种协同演化方法与五个基准（六种设置）上验证。

Result: 对技能漂移的敏感性随协同演化训练推进而增强；最坏情况成功率比各delivered pair的峰值低5-13个百分点，抹去了delivered bank相对无技能基线增益的52-93%。

Conclusion: CODA重新定义了协同演化的目标：从在协同演化技能库上的峰值性能，转向无论技能如何演化都能驾驭技能的能力，并为未来方法提供共享的审计标准。

Abstract: Skill-policy co-evolution, in which an agent's policy model and its skill bank improve each other during training, is a promising paradigm for self-improving agents. Current evaluations score the co-evolved policy on the final bank it trained with (the delivered bank). After deployment, however, skills may be rewritten, added or removed more frequently than the policy model is retrained, leaving the policy to operate on a drifted skill bank that neither the training objective nor existing evaluation protocols address. In this paper, we first establish, through theoretical and empirical analysis, that the fragility hidden by this blind spot is not incidental. The acceptance rule these methods share (cooperative acceptance) ties a policy's competence to the bank it co-evolved with, leaving it fragile once skills drift, regardless of the implementation; empirically, sensitivity to skill drift grows as co-evolution training proceeds. We therefore introduce the CO-evolution Drift Audit (CODA), a deployment-oriented evaluation protocol that simulates skill drift and, alongside success rate under worst-case drift, introduces two new metrics: a consistency rate under meaning-preserving edits and a resistance rate to corrupted skills. Across four co-evolution methods and five benchmarks (six settings), worst-case success is 5-13 points below the peak measured for each delivered pair, erasing 52-93% of the delivered bank's gain over the no-skill baseline. CODA thus reframes the target of co-evolution, from peak performance on the co-evolved bank to the ability to harness skills however they evolve, and provides a shared standard against which future methods can be audited.

</details>


### [21] [Anticipatory Commonsense with Moving Objects: An ASP-Based Framework for Predictive Reasoning with Interacting Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20867)
*Julius Monsen,Jakob Suchan,Mehul Bhatt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个声明式混合框架，用于复杂动态环境中交互运动的预期推理，在语义层面形成可解释的时空心智模型以启发式引导底层运动计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端随机轨迹预测缺乏可解释性，难以在语义层面显式表达交互运动的事件级假设，因此需要一种可解释、模块化、易扩展的预期推理框架。

Method: 混合、模块化、elaboration-tolerant 框架，包含三部分：1) 用 ASP（回答集编程）建模交互运动轨迹动力学；2) 语义引导预测，生成"分支轨迹未来"；3) 基于能量的优化模型对预期进行评价、排序与筛选。

Result: 在 Argoverse 2 基准上评估，每条预期轨迹均附带显式、可检查的事件级假设，区别于端到端随机预测。

Conclusion: 语义层面的声明式预期推理可与底层运动计算有效结合，实现可解释的事件级轨迹预测，并在标准基准上验证可行性。

Abstract: We present a general declarative framework for anticipatory reasoning about interactive motion in complex dynamic environments. Our focus is on (explainable) anticipatory space-time mental model formation at a semantic level to heuristically guide low-level motion computation. Our method is hybrid, modular and elaboration tolerant integrating: 1) An ASP model characterising the dynamics of interactive motion trajectories; 2) Semantically-guided prediction for deriving `branching trajectory futures'; and 3) An energy-based {optimisation} model to evaluate, rank and filter anticipations. We evaluate with the Argoverse 2 benchmark, where each anticipated trajectory carries an explicit, inspectable event-level hypothesis, unlike end-to-end stochastic prediction.

</details>


### [22] [A Multi-Tiered AI Accessibility Model (MAIAM): Proportionate Access and Capability Limits for Comprehensive Frontier AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20921)
*Chai K. Toh,Vasu Raj Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“多层级AI可及性模型”（MAIAM），按用途、安全与能力而非发布形态来分级开放AI系统，并与“SAFE AI运行点”结合为“能力边界＋可及性边界”的双部框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI发布争论常被简化为“API封闭 vs 权重公开”的二元对立，混淆了“系统可达到多强”与“谁能以何种条件获得该能力”两个独立决策；论文聚焦后者，指出前沿权重一旦公开即丧失发布后所有安全抓手，且基座模型评估难以代表其衍生物。

Method: 提出MAIAM三级可及性分层：第一级“AI for Learning”广泛可及但刻意限制能力；第二级“AI for Exploration”面向研发，需注册、身份与随能力提升而收紧的使用控制；第三级“Full-Scale Frontier AI”仅通过结构化、可记录、可问责的访问通道开放。并沿能力、自主性、领域后果、使用/安全四轴给出分级判据，将MAIAM与SAFE AI Operating Point整合为双部框架。

Result: 论证了公开前沿级权重的代价（发布后安全杠杆尽失），并援引安全微调可被以数百美元成本从开放权重中剥离的证据，说明基座模型评估对其衍生物的预测力有限；形成“能力边界＋可及性边界”的双部框架。

Conclusion: 可及性应与能力、安全与风险成比例；仅设能力上限不足以保障AI安全，还需由监管机构实施的分层访问制度相配合。

Abstract: Most debate about releasing capable AI systems treats the choice as binary: keep the model behind an API, or publish the weights. That framing runs together two separate decisions. One is how capable a system may become. The other is who may obtain a given capability, and on what terms. This paper is about the second decision. We propose a Multi-Tiered AI Accessibility Model (MAIAM) that sorts access by purpose, safety, and capability instead of by release artifact. Tier 1, AI for Learning, is broadly accessible and deliberately capability-bounded. Tier 2, AI for Exploration, serves research and development under registration, identity, and usage controls that tighten as capability rises. Tier 3, Full-Scale Frontier AI, is available only through structured, recorded, and accountable access. The principle behind the tiers is that accessibility should be proportional to capability, safety, and risk. We show why publishing frontier-class weights forfeits every safety lever a developer has after release, and, using evidence that safety fine-tuning can be stripped from open weights for a few hundred dollars, why an evaluation of the base model says little about its derivatives. We give criteria for assigning a system to a tier along capability, autonomy, domain-consequence, and usage/safety axes, and combine MAIAM with the SAFE AI Operating Point into a two-part framework: a Capability Boundary that fixes the safety limits not to be crossed, and an Accessibility Boundary that fixes who may obtain a capability below that limit. Capability limits alone do not make AI safe; a tiered access regime administered by regulating bodies is also needed.

</details>


### [23] [Defining and Generating Compositional Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20922)
*Eric Elmoznino,Tejas Kasetty,Blaise Agüera y Arcas,Yoshua Bengio,Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用算法信息论形式化"组合结构"：当各部分被联合建模时显现出单独建模时所不含的共享结构，即视为存在组合性；并可反向用于生成具有层级型部分-整体组织的对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合性（部件的复用与重组成新结构）是自然与人工系统中复杂性的重要来源，人类识别它似乎毫不费力，但刻画其直观含义的数学理论仍然有限。

Method: 基于算法信息论（信息与压缩的数学），定义组合结构为"部件联合考虑时揭示的、分开建模时不存在的共享结构"；跨尺度应用以定位组合结构在对象中何处、如何涌现；再用可处理的近似将该框架反演，在不指定具体内容的前提下指定所需组合结构形式，直接在字符串与图像上优化生成。

Result: 生成的字符串与图像展现出易于识别的层级式复用与部分-整体组织，表明该定义捕捉的是组合结构，而非单纯的复杂性、规则性或共享信息。

Conclusion: 该定义为组合性提供了可操作的数学刻画，并对机器学习有启示：可为神经架构中的"深度"提供新视角，也为构建支持开放式学习的组合性数据课程提供原则。

Abstract: Compositional structure is pervasive in nature and across scales: atoms combine into molecules, molecules into cells, and cells into organisms, while further compositional hierarchies appear within each level. More broadly, compositionality, the reuse and recombination of parts into novel structures, is a fundamental source of interesting complexity in both natural and artificial systems. Yet despite its importance, and despite our apparent ease in recognizing it, mathematical accounts capturing what we intuitively mean by compositional structure remain limited. Here, we fill this gap by formalizing a simple idea: compositional structure emerges when parts, considered together, reveal shared structure that is not captured when those parts are modeled separately. We express this idea using algorithmic information theory, the mathematics of information and compression, which provides a natural way to identify structure in arbitrary data without specifying in advance what that structure must look like. Applied across scales, our definition informs where and how compositional structure emerges within an object. Using tractable approximations, we then invert the framework to generate compositional objects: we specify the desired form of their compositional structure without prescribing their particular content, and optimize directly over strings and images. The resulting objects exhibit readily recognizable hierarchical reuse and part-whole organization, suggesting that the definition captures compositional structure rather than merely complexity, regularity, or shared information. Finally, we discuss implications for machine learning, including a new perspective on depth in neural architectures and principles for constructing compositional data curricula capable of supporting open-ended learning.

</details>


### [24] [SkillNEO: Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization of Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20926)
*Enze Ma,Chunyu Miao,Yankai Chen,Jie Yang,Liancheng Fang,Kening Zheng,Hanrong Zhang,Muyao Xu,Xinle Yu,Chengze Li,Wei-Chieh Huang,Ziwei Ren,Yunhao Du,Zhixiang Shen,Xue Liu,Philip S. Yu,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillNEO，一种非精英进化优化框架，将"选择使用的版本"与"继续改进的版本"分离选择，利用相关版本在同任务上的表现对比来识别应保留的步骤与待修复的失败，并可用后续证据回溯修订早期版本。在86个SkillsBench任务上，使GPT-5.4 Mini平均奖励从49.1%提升至66.4%，且生成的技能可无进一步编辑地迁移增益到更强模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能进化依赖执行反馈迭代改进，但一次改进往往需要多个相互依赖的编辑，低奖励中间版本仍具开发价值。若强制要求即时奖励提升（精英式进化），会在部分修复的收益显现前将其丢弃。因此需要一种机制，为非精英（低于先前版本）的新版本提供"保留什么、下一步修复什么"的证据支持。

Method: SkillNEO（Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization for Agent Skills）将"选用版本"与"保留继续改进的版本"分开选择。通过比较相关版本在同一任务上的执行表现，识别出可用的工作步骤（保留）与剩余失败点（修复）。支持利用后续证据回溯并修订早期版本。实验由GPT-5.4 Mini执行任务、提出修订、编辑技能，最终评估使用相同任务实例。

Result: 在覆盖软件工程、办公工作等八个领域的86个SkillsBench任务上，GPT-5.4 Mini平均奖励由49.1%升至66.4%，处于GPT-5.5与GPT-5.6 Luna的原始技能区间；增益覆盖全部八个领域。技能在不进一步编辑的情况下迁移至更强模型：在41个技能被修改的任务上，复用使Luna提升10.5个百分点，使GPT-5.5高出其已发布原始技能参考10.6个百分点。执行记录显示即使Mini仍失败于部分任务要求，技能对更强模型仍有效；搜索历史亦显示低分版本最终导向被选用的技能。

Conclusion: 技能进化可将较弱模型的部分进展转化为可复用的程序性知识，用于提升更强智能体。非精英进化配合基于同任务对比的保留/修复证据，能够避免过早丢弃有价值的部分修复，并实现跨模型的技能迁移。

Abstract: Agent skills turn procedural knowledge into reusable artifacts, allowing LLM agents to accumulate and refine expertise through experience. Building on this, skill evolution iteratively improves skills using execution feedback. However, improvement may require several interdependent edits, so low-reward versions can still be worth developing. Requiring immediate reward gains can discard useful partial repairs before their benefits emerge. Non-elitist evolution allows new versions to receive further edits despite scoring below earlier ones. For agent skills, this calls for evidence of what to preserve and repair next. We introduce SkillNEO (Non-Elitist Evolutionary Optimization for Agent Skills), which separately selects a version to use and versions to keep improving. Comparing how related versions perform the same task reveals working steps to preserve and remaining failures to fix. It can also revisit and revise earlier versions using later evidence. Across 86 SkillsBench tasks in eight domains, including software engineering and office work, SkillNEO raises GPT-5.4 Mini's average reward from 49.1% to 66.4%, within GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6 Luna's original-skill range. Mini executes tasks, proposes revisions, and edits skills; final evaluation uses the same task instances. Gains span all eight domains. The same skills improve stronger models without further editing. On the 41 tasks with modified skills, reuse improves Luna by 10.5 percentage points and places GPT-5.5 10.6 points above its published original-skill reference. Execution records show these skills can benefit stronger models even when Mini still fails some task requirements. Search histories also show lower-scoring versions leading to skills ultimately selected for use. Together, these findings show that skill evolution can turn a weaker model's partial progress into reusable procedural knowledge for improving more capable agents.

</details>


### [25] [RAC: A Relational Agent Contract for Constraint-Governed Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20935)
*Kaiyuan Zheng,Fengqing Jiang,Yichen Feng,Zubin Aaron Assadian,Yuetai Li,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Luyao Niu,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 RAC（一种与具体 harness 无关的工程智能体动作与反馈层合约），让智能体只做语义层面的关系决策，把几何、时序等可机械推导的后果交给专用工具，从而显著提升受约束工程任务的可靠性与一次通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受约束的工程任务中，产物需满足精确且相互耦合的约束。现有方案要求智能体在做出语义决策的同时，还须自行推导精确几何、时序等后果，容易出错并导致失败。

Method: 提出 RAC 合约，规定五个要素：权威观测、语义动作、基于工具的实现、独立验证、修复证据。智能体决定产物应满足哪些关系，专用工具负责推导具体后果。在 PCB 布局、布线、修复任务，作业车间调度任务，以及四类微控制器上的 141 个嵌入式代码任务上进行评估。

Result: PCB 任务中，RAC 在全部九个被测模型上均取得最高 clean-pass 率，平均 90.9%，而最强基线（仅需求委托）为 74.0%；作业车间调度中，直接关系动作达到 96.3% 质量通过率，代码中介交付在同一调度后端下仅为 59.6%；141 个嵌入式代码任务验证了动作合约的作用。

Conclusion: 动作合约本身是智能体能力的一种来源：保留语义控制权、将可机械推导的输出委托给工具，能够提升受约束产物的可靠性。

Abstract: Engineering agents must produce artifacts that satisfy exact, coupled constraints. Existing solutions often require agents to make semantic decisions and also derive consequences such as precise geometry or timing from those decisions, leading to failures in constraint-governed engineering tasks. In this paper, we introduce RAC, a harness-agnostic contract for the action and feedback layer of engineering agents. It specifies authoritative observations, semantic actions, tool-based realization, independent verification, and repair evidence. The agent decides which relations an artifact should satisfy; specialized tools derive their concrete consequences. On PCB placement, routing, and repair tasks, RAC achieves the highest clean-pass rate for all nine evaluated models, averaging 90.9%, compared with 74.0% for the strongest baseline, requirements-only delegation. In job-shop scheduling, direct relational actions achieve 96.3% quality pass versus 59.6% for code-mediated delivery with access to the same scheduling backend. Our evaluations on 141 embedded-code tasks across four microcontroller identify the action contract as a source of agent capability: preserving semantic control while delegating mechanically derivable outputs improves the reliability of constrained artifacts.

</details>


### [26] [MAI-Gym: An Open All-in-One Platform for GUI Agent Evaluation and RL Training across Mobile and Computer Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20939)
*Panrong Tong,Quyu Kong,Pengxiang Li,Tianyu Xia,Hanzhang Zhou,Lei Wang,Chenglin Cai,Long Li,Long Chen,Jianan Zhang,Zhengwen Zeng,Xu Zhang,Gongjie Zhang,Yue Wang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MAI-Gym一体化平台，统一GUI智能体的评测、数据收集与在线强化学习训练，训练出MAI-Gym-9B在10B以下开源GUI智能体中达到SOTA，并证明模拟环境中的RL可迁移到真实设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带可验证奖励的在线RL在数学、编程、工具使用上进展迅速，但迁移到GUI智能体面临两大障碍：交互环境成本高昂，且缺乏真实训练任务。

Method: 构建覆盖移动端、电脑、网页的开源平台MAI-Gym：通过共享rollout管线保证评测与训练行为一致，支持10个基准、可扩展至2万并行环境实例；并支持自动化合成多样化GUI应用与高难度任务，包括可重置的模拟应用与跨应用连贯世界，任务验证器经执行校验。基于该平台训练统一9B智能体MAI-Gym-9B。

Result: MAI-Gym-9B取得10B以下开源GUI智能体SOTA：MobileWorld 78.4%、OSWorld-Verified 76.2%；在模拟购票应用上的RL可迁移至真实对应场景，真实手机任务成功率由28.8%提升至48.1%。

Conclusion: MAI-Gym验证了可扩展模拟环境加任务合成是GUI智能体RL训练的可行路径，且模拟到真实的迁移有效；开源框架、合成技能、模拟应用、2000条已验证任务与模型权重以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards has driven rapid progress in mathematics, coding, and tool use, but scaling this success to GUI agents remains challenging due to the high cost of interactive environments and the scarcity of realistic training tasks. We introduce MAI-Gym, an open all-in-one platform that unifies GUI agent evaluation, data collection, and online RL training across mobile, computer, and web environments. Through a shared rollout pipeline, MAI-Gym ensures consistent agent behavior across evaluation and training, supports ten established benchmarks, and scales to 20,000 parallel environment instances. Beyond scalable infrastructure, MAI-Gym enables automated synthesis of diverse GUI applications and challenging tasks, including resettable mocked apps and coherent cross-app worlds with execution-validated task verifiers. Using MAI-Gym, we train MAI-Gym-9B, a unified 9B agent for mobile and computer use, achieving state-of-the-art performance among open GUI agents under 10B parameters: 78.4% on MobileWorld and 76.2% on OSWorld-Verified. Importantly, RL in a mocked train-ticketing app transfers to its real-world counterpart, improving task success on a phys- ical phone from 28.8% to 48.1%. We release the framework, synthesis skills, mocked apps, 2,000 verified tasks, and the model checkpoint to support repro- ducible research and further advances in scalable GUI agent training.

</details>


### [27] [LEV: Thinking Fast and Deep with Looped Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20951)
*Dingjie Song,Zhiling Yan,Yuxuan Zhang,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEV是一种决策模型，通过在推理时循环复用网络层（而非生成token）来“深度思考”，同一组权重可在任意深度作答，准确率随深度提升；配合小停控器按问题自适应选择深度，可在几乎不损失精度的情况下节省15%–24%计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策模型单次前向即给出各选项概率，类似双系统理论中的System 1，响应快但对难题与易题耗费相同计算，深度在训练时固定；而生成文本的推理模型虽可延长思考，却要为每个额外步骤付出解码token的代价。因此需要一种既能按需增加计算、又避免逐token解码开销的决策模型。

Method: 提出LEV：基于以循环方式预训练的语言模型，通过对各层循环复用以替代生成token来实现“更深思考”；每个循环步后读取选项，所有步联合训练，使单一checkpoint支持任意深度作答；并在推理时用小型stopper按问题设定所需深度，其阈值在无标注请求上拟合。

Result: 在三个循环骨干上，相同权重下准确率随深度上升，且提升主要来自难题：2.6B的LEV在JevBench上从深度1的63.2%升至深度4的76.2%。按需深度节省15%–24%计算，精度损失不足1个百分点，与固定深度混合方案持平或略优。在锁定测试集上，1.4B与3B模型在低于50 ms的同等延迟下，比稠密（非循环）模型及其级联方案高5.4–8.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 循环复用层可在不生成token的前提下实现可扩展的推理深度，并在同权重下随深度获益；结合无监督阈值拟合的按需深度调度，能在极小精度代价下显著降低计算，并在低延迟区间优于稠密模型与级联方案。

Abstract: Decision models answer typed questions about an input with one probability per option, computed in a single forward pass without generating text. Like the fast System 1 of dual-process theory, they answer at once, but their depth is fixed at training time, so they spend the same computation on easy and hard questions. Models that reason in generated text can think longer, but they pay for every extra step in decoded tokens. We present LEV, a decision model that thinks deeper by rerunning its layers in a loop instead of generating tokens. LEV builds on a language model pretrained as a loop, reads the options after every loop step and trains all steps jointly, so one checkpoint can answer at any depth. On three looped backbones, accuracy rises with depth on the same weights, mostly on hard questions, and a 2.6B LEV goes from 63.2% at depth one to 76.2% at depth four on JevBench. On-demand depth, set per question by a small stopper with a threshold fitted on unlabelled requests, saves 15% to 24% of the computation at a cost of under one accuracy point, level with or slightly above a fixed mix of depths. On a locked test set, the 1.4B and 3B models are 5.4 to 8.8 points more accurate than dense (non-looped) models and cascades of the same latency below 50 ms.

</details>


### [28] [StepSafe: Learning Timely Intervention with Context-Aware Safety Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20960)
*Siyi Gu,Kunhan Gao,Chao Peter Yang,Sukanta Ganguly,Rex Ying,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ASPO：面向智能体安全的步级及时干预训练框架，配合StepSafe步级推理护栏模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统安全本质上是序列化的：动作是否安全取决于先前的动作、工具输出、信息披露及智能体间交互。现有护栏虽已从轨迹级事后评估转向步级动作诊断与主动风险预测，但训练目标对"步级证据如何随时间累积、何时累积足够以支持干预"缺乏监督信号。

Method: 1) 前缀级阶段对齐（pre-fix level stage alignment）：将每个回复与同一前缀下采样的其他回复比较，奖励契合当前阶段的动作——风险未显现时继续、澄清仍可避免伤害时询问、伤害不可逆前停止；2) 自适应反馈自蒸馏：以模型自身为教师，在干预步上看到步级标注、在安全步上利用自身误报挖掘的规则，为决策背后的推理过程提供信用分配；3) 训练数据每步携带违规分类体系与干预窗口，并通过自演化多智能体红队对抗扩充。

Result: 提出StepSafe——具备及时干预能力的步级推理护栏。在五个智能体安全基准上，于安全性-效用帕累托曲线取得开源护栏最优表现，Youden's J 达53.0（4B），对比基线46.2。

Conclusion: 将及时干预建模为步级序列决策问题，通过阶段对齐与自蒸馏实现证据累积的细粒度信用分配，可在开源规模下提升安全-效用权衡。

Abstract: Safety in agentic systems is inherently sequential: whether an action is safe depends on prior actions, tool outputs, information disclosures, and interactions among agents. Recent work has shifted agent safety guardrails from post-hoc trajectory-level assessment toward step-level action diagnosis and proactive risk prediction, yet existing training objectives provide limited supervision for how step evidence accumulates over time and when it becomes sufficient to justify intervention. We propose \methodlong~(ASPO): a training framework for step-level timely intervention. Pre-fix level stage alignment allows crediting each decision at its own step: it compares each response with the other responses sampled at the same prefix and rewards the action that fits the stage, continuing before the risk is visible, asking while clarification can still avert harm, and stopping before harm is irreversible. Adaptive feedback self-distillation further credits the reasoning behind each decision, with the model itself as a teacher that sees the step annotations on intervention steps and rules mined from its own false alarms on safe steps. Its training data carry a violation taxonomy and an intervention window at every step and are extended through self-evolving multi-agent red-teaming. Finally we present \textsc{StepSafe}, a step-level reasoning guardrail with timely intervention, that achieves the state-of-the-art peformance among open-source guardrails across five agent-safety benchmarks on safety-utility pareto curve with Youden's $J$ of 53.0 (4B) against 46.2.

</details>


### [29] [MSP: Modality Self-Play for Training Multimodal LLMs Without Paired Cross-Modal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20965)
*Robert Moseley,Md Kaykobad Reza,Ameya Patil,Edward Ayrapetian,Salman Asif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Modality Self-Play (MSP)训练框架，用"代理文本描述"替代真实配对跨模态数据，实现零样本模态组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐的多模态配对数据集昂贵、难以跨模态组合扩展，成为多模态基础模型发展的根本瓶颈。

Method: 以锚定模态支撑的代理文本描述作为未见模态的语义桥：给定观测模态（如图像或音频），模型生成互补未见模态的代理描述，经对比预训练编码器编码后，通过学习到的桥接与观测模态对齐，从而在无真实配对非文本监督下实现跨模态交互。

Result: 在多个多模态基准上取得SOTA或具竞争力的性能，实现零样本模态组合；消融显示学习桥接改善对齐、代理描述支撑有效跨模态组合。

Conclusion: MSP去除了对真实配对非文本监督的需求，但仍依赖模态-文本对、预训练对比编码器与模态分布桥接校准。

Abstract: Large multimodal models have achieved remarkable progress by scaling model capacity and training on massive paired multimodal data. However, this paradigm introduces a fundamental bottleneck: aligned multimodal datasets are expensive to curate, difficult to scale across diverse modality combinations, and increasingly constrain further progress. We introduce Modality Self-Play (MSP), a training framework for multimodal foundation models based on proxy-mediated modality composition. Instead of relying on paired cross-modal data, MSP uses anchor-supporting proxy textual descriptions of unseen modalities as semantic bridges during training. Given an observed modality (e.g., image or audio), the model generates proxy descriptions for complementary unseen modalities, which are then encoded and aligned with the observed modality through learned bridges, enabling cross-modal interaction without real paired non-text supervision.
  Our central hypothesis is that proxy text, combined with contrastively trained pretrained encoders, provides sufficient semantic structure for multimodal composition to emerge. We show that MSP enables zero-shot modality composition: models trained only with a real anchor modality and accompanying proxy descriptions can integrate and reason over multiple modalities at inference time despite never observing real paired non-text data during training. Empirically, MSP achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance on multiple multimodal benchmarks. Ablation studies further demonstrate that learned bridges improve alignment, while anchor-supporting proxy descriptions enable effective cross-modal composition. MSP removes the need for real paired non-text (e.g., audio-vision) supervision, while continuing to rely on modality-text pairs, pretrained contrastive encoders, and modality-distribution bridge calibration.

</details>


### [30] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for HPC Job Scheduling: A Statistical Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21008)
*Justin M. Cheney,Clement N. Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文在统一非参数框架下，对比带/不带动作掩码的PPO、A2C、DQN与三种启发式及掩码随机控制，检验DRL作业选择是否真正优于动作掩码本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRL用于HPC作业选择时，"优于启发式"不足以证明其价值——提升可能仅来自动作掩码而非学习。需要引入掩码均匀随机控制作为对照，区分"完成调度"与"真实调度收益"。

Method: 六种DRL处理（PPO/A2C/DQN × 有/无掩码），基线为三种启发式与种子配对的掩码均匀随机控制；在两条真实Slurm负载轨迹（同构与异构）上各跑十个种子，采用统一非参数统计框架（含Nemenyi检验）。

Result: 所有掩码处理完成评估，未掩码处理被截断。同构轨迹上，掩码DRL在平均等待时间与平均/最大有界减速上均未显著优于控制。异构轨迹上MaskablePPO三项指标全面优于控制：平均等待时间在10%裕度内与三种启发式相当，最大有界减速比LCFS低22%。MaskablePPO在两条轨迹的完整处理中平均等待时间排名第一，但与MaskableA2C无显著差异。掩码变体中MaskablePPO训练时间最短，MaskableDQN决策延迟最低。

Conclusion: 学习型作业选择的收益高度依赖负载特征与基线选择，仅部分场景超越掩码随机控制；评估DRL调度价值时应纳入掩码随机控制以剥离掩码效应。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) offers learned job selection for High-Performance Computing (HPC), but performance against heuristics alone does not establish value beyond action masking. Six DRL treatments are compared: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), Advantage Actor-Critic (A2C), and Deep Q-Network (DQN), each with and without masking. Three heuristics and a seed-paired masked uniform random control provide baselines across ten seeds on two real Slurm (Simple Linux Utility for Resource Management) workload traces, one homogeneous and one heterogeneous, under a common non-parametric framework. All masked treatments complete evaluation, whereas unmasked treatments are truncated. On the homogeneous trace, no masked DRL treatment significantly improves average waiting time or average and maximum bounded slowdown over the control. On the heterogeneous trace, MaskablePPO improves all three metrics relative to the control. Its average waiting time is equivalent to that of the three heuristics within a 10% margin, and its maximum bounded slowdown is 22% lower than that of Last-Come, First-Served (LCFS). MaskablePPO has the leading average waiting-time rank among complete treatments on both traces, but Nemenyi comparisons detect no significant difference from MaskableA2C. Among masked variants, MaskablePPO has the lowest recorded mean training time, while MaskableDQN has the lowest mean decision latency. These findings distinguish completion from baseline-specific scheduling gains and support including a masked random control when assessing the value of learned selection.

</details>


### [31] [Failure Is the Mother of Success: Recovering Supervision with Grounding Hindsight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21018)
*Sheldon Yu,Tong Yu,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Zhendong Chu,Zhenwei Tang,Sungchul Kim,Lina Yao,Julian McAuley,Junda Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Replay-Selected Hindsight Distillation (RSHD)，将失败的 rollout 通过"纠错笔记 + 重解 + 重放筛选"转化为额外监督，并与 GRPO 联合训练，显著提升域内与域外基准表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 在整组 rollout 全部失败时既无成功样例可模仿，也无非零组内相对优势，导致模型在最需要学习的难题上完全得不到梯度信号。而失败推理本身仍可提供"下一步该尝试什么"的信息，因此存在被浪费的监督来源。

Method: RSHD 流程：(1) 策略在未见参考答案/解答的情况下为每个失败尝试撰写纠错笔记；(2) 将笔记放入上下文重新解题（重放）；(3) 对笔记做答案泄漏筛查（剔除直接陈述答案的笔记）；(4) 仅当归因于该笔记的重放成功率高于原始组的成功率时，才接纳该笔记；(5) 把被接纳的重放轨迹蒸馏回"纯提示"策略，与 GRPO 训练同时进行。仅训练策略模型，唯一评判器为结果验证器，部署时无需笔记或额外推理开销。

Result: 在多个模型家族与规模、两个训练领域上，RSHD 在域内基准上稳定优于 GRPO，且增益可迁移至域外评测集。方法在"全失败组"与"混合组中的失败样本"上同样适用。

Conclusion: 以"重放后新产生的成功"而非失败 rollout 本身作为接纳判据，使监督可从全失败组中恢复；RSHD 是一种即插即用、无额外推理代价的 GRPO 增强手段，有效利用失败样本提升推理能力。

Abstract: When every rollout for a problem fails, GRPO receives neither a successful exemplar nor a nonzero group-relative advantage, so it learns nothing from exactly the problems it most needs to learn. The failed reasoning can nevertheless suggest what to try next. We present Replay-Selected Hindsight Distillation (RSHD), which turns failed attempts into an additional, tested source of supervision during GRPO training. The policy writes a corrective note about each failed attempt without seeing a reference answer or solution, then re-solves the problem with the note in context. A note is admitted only if its replays succeed more often than the original group did, after screening for notes that state the answer, and an admitted replay is distilled into the plain-prompt policy. Because admission is decided by fresh successes rather than by the failed rollout itself, the procedure can recover supervision from groups in which every attempt failed, and it applies equally to failures in mixed groups. Only the policy is trained, the only judge is the outcome verifier, and deployment requires no notes or extra inference. Across model families and sizes and two training domains, RSHD consistently improves over GRPO on in-domain benchmarks, and the gains carry over to out-of-domain evaluation sets.

</details>


### [32] [Recursive Reward Improvement via Adversarial Meta-Prompt Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21040)
*Wei Dai,Qianchu Liu,Guanghui Qin,Xing Han,Tristan Naumann,Paul Pu Liang,Sheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RRI（Recursive Reward Improvement），用奖励黑客对抗者监督模型自生成评分标准，解决开放式任务自改进中评分标准被solver利用的问题，在HealthBench Professional等基准上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自改进（RSI）在数学、代码等答案可自动校验的任务上有效，但临床报告生成等开放式专家任务没有标准答案，proposer必须同时生成评分标准（rubric）。模型生成rubric的核心难点是全面性不足：solver会钻空子，只罗列预期主题而无实质内容，从而腐蚀学习过程，造成reward hacking。

Method: 构建proposer-solver循环中的rubric监督机制：在solver训练前引入奖励黑客对抗者。同一模型在未见rubric情况下写出诚实答案，同时写出尽可能无用但能骗过rubric的"作弊答案"；最多两步修复（repair）修订rubric，使诚实答案获胜。发现的漏洞还会反馈至未来任务与rubric的生成过程中。

Result: 在HealthBench Professional上，RRI相较Generation Only自改进基线，调整后得分在9B模型提升10.9分、27B提升12.4分；在无外部模型条件下提升10.3分。增益在困难任务与写作任务上最大。27B模型仅用HealthBench Professional描述训练，在未见过的PRBench Hard上提升6.6分、ProfBench提升8.0分，显示迁移能力。论文还分析了模型生成问答-rubric对的易利用点。

Conclusion: RRI通过对抗式rubric修复有效抑制开放式自博弈中的奖励黑客，提升自改进数据的质量与跨任务泛化，为开放式专家任务的自博弈研究提供了可复用框架及对rubric脆弱性的分析。

Abstract: Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI) has gained wide attention for its ability to work with limited data. In a proposer-solver loop, a proposer model writes training tasks and a solver model learns to solve them through reinforcement learning. Such loops have succeeded in mathematics and coding, where every answer can be checked automatically. Open-ended expert tasks, like clinical report generations, have no answer key, which makes simple question-answer generation not viable. Here the proposer must also write a rubric that lists the content an answer should include and the errors it should avoid. However, the unique challenge behind model-written rubric is the comprehensiveness of it. For naively generated rubric, solvers tend to exploit it, naming the expected topics without the substance behind them, which corrupts the learning process. To solve this problem, we introduce RRI, Recursive Reward Improvement, which uses a reward hacking adversary to supervise the reward. Before the solver trains on a generated task, an adversary writes an answer that games its rubric while being as useless as possible, the same model writes an honest answer without seeing the rubric, and up to two repair steps revise the rubric to make the honest answer win. The exploits found also rewrite how future tasks and rubrics are generated. On HealthBench Professional, RRI improves the adjusted score over the Generation Only self-improvement baseline by 10.9 points at 9B and 12.4 at 27B, and by 10.3 points without any external model. In particular, the gains are largest on difficult and writing tasks. We show that the 27B model trained on the HealthBench Professional description improves by 6.6 points on PRBench Hard and 8.0 on ProfBench without seeing a task from either. We further analyze the exploitable points of model-generated question-rubric pairs to facilitate future research on open-ended self-play.

</details>


### [33] [Learning When to Withdraw: Curriculum Coverage and Rule-Specific Failures in Fine-Tuned Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21045)
*Mohamed Abouzahra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NAQD-Env合成环境与规则级诊断方法，用四组Qwen2.5-3B LoRA检查点研究工具型语言智能体的"选择性撤回"能力，揭示聚合准确率会掩盖规则特定的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型语言智能体在证据变化或权限被撤销时需修订已准备的动作，即"选择性撤回"：暂停受影响动作、保留有支持的工作、在充分修复后再恢复。现有评估多用聚合精度，难以暴露规则层面的具体回归，因此需要一个具备显式依赖与确定性参考策略的受控环境来细致诊断SFT行为。

Method: 构建NAQD-Env合成环境（显式依赖、确定性参考策略）。训练4个Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct的LoRA检查点，对比原始课程与增强课程，后者在三种系统提示框架下使用相同视图与目标。在三种推理提示下，对4个检查点及3个指令微调基线进行评估，测试集包括新验证样例、留出依赖族与留出结构组合。并进行规则级诊断（如"有存活替代支持却错误撤回"、"未收窄禁止组合"、"授权保留"等）。

Result: 在naqd提示下，SFT检查点决策一致率为0.77–0.92、撤回召回率0.46–0.86，基线仅为0.45–0.63与≤0.06。规则级诊断显示：原始课程检查点在34个存在存活替代支持的动作中错误撤回26个，增强课程仅0–1个；但所有SFT检查点收窄禁止组合的频率低于7B/8B基线（该情形两个课程均未覆盖）；委托场景中撤回更多但对独立授权动作的保留较差，且四个检查点均遗漏合法事件报告。不同提示框架的效果跨指标与划分不一致，无法确立可复现优势或等价性。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于一种检测聚合准确率所掩盖的规则特定回归的方法，而非证明微调本身即可保证智能体安全；受限于每臂单次训练、课程属性联合变化、先前评估暴露及基线-SFT量化差异，因果归因有限。

Abstract: Tool-using language agents must revise prepared actions when evidence changes or authority is revoked. Selective withdrawal means suspending affected actions, preserving supported work, and resuming only after sufficient repair. We study supervised fine-tuning in NAQD-Env, a synthetic environment with explicit dependencies and a deterministic reference policy. Four LoRA checkpoints of Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct compare an original curriculum with an enriched one, the latter under three system-prompt framings with identical views and targets. Three instruction-tuned baselines and all four checkpoints are evaluated under three inference prompts on fresh validation cases, held-out dependency families, and held-out structural combinations. Under the naqd prompt, SFT checkpoints reach decision agreement of 0.77-0.92 and withdrawal recall of 0.46-0.86, versus 0.45-0.63 and at most 0.06 for the baselines. Rule-level diagnostics qualify these gains. The original-curriculum checkpoint wrongly withdraws 26 of 34 actions with surviving alternative support; enriched checkpoints withdraw 0-1. Conversely, all SFT checkpoints narrow prohibited combinations less often than the 7B and 8B baselines, a case absent from both curricula. Delegation results show more withdrawal but poorer preservation of independently authorized actions, and all four checkpoints omit valid event reports. Framing contrasts vary across metrics and splits and do not establish a reproducible advantage or equivalence. One training run per arm, jointly changed curriculum properties, prior evaluation exposure, and baseline-SFT quantization differences limit causal attribution. The contribution is a method for detecting rule-specific regressions that aggregate accuracy can conceal, not evidence that fine-tuning alone ensures agent safety.

</details>


### [34] [Athanor: An Agent-Native System for Autonomous Post-Training Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21055)
*Yufa Zhou,Yichen Wang,Jiakun Fan,Ziqi Shu,Zhichao Liu,Anru R. Zhang,Neiwen Ling,Zijun Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Athanor——一个面向真实 GPU 的智能体原生自主后训练研究（AutoPostTrain）框架。四名 Claude Opus 5.5 智能体在 104 小时内、无研究指导条件下，将 Qwen3-4B-Base 在六任务开发集上从 15.9 提升至 67.3、在隐藏留出集上从 9.2 提升至 48.2，平均达到或超过官方 Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507（66.5 / 47.9）。据称这是首次证明前沿智能体团队能自主把原始基座模型后训练为跨域通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（AI 构建更强 AI）被视为通向超级智能的路径之一，但难以直接测量。作者选择其可测量的弱化形式：让前沿智能体用公开数据后训练一个小型原始基座模型，以此检验智能体的研究潜力（engineering + research ability）。
