Method: 构建 Athanor：开放、智能体原生的自主后训练研究系统。赋予智能体完全研究自主权、低摩擦工程工具与高效起始代码；智能体自行阅读数据集与评测结果、构建训练数据、编写修改训练算法、调超参、分析结果并决定下一步。运行于沙箱内，禁止使用外部教师模型。服务端提供冻结评测、托管作业、去污染数据、持久化研究记录，以及智能体不知情的留出测试套件。

Result: 104 小时、4 个 Claude Opus 5.5 智能体（各占 8 卡节点）、无任何研究建议：开发集 15.9→67.3，留出集 9.2→48.2；对照官方 Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 为 66.5 与 47.9。完整记录显示，提升源于重组已知方法：公开推理数据 SFT、含某智能体引入 rollout 改动的 RL、智能体间权重合并、多轮自蒸馏。

Conclusion: 在"执行已被解决"的前提下，当今智能体已具备攻克该任务的工程与研究能力；这是首次展示智能体团队可自主将基座模型后训练为跨域通用模型并平均匹配/超越官方 instruct 版本。作者希望 Athanor 成为规模化自主 AI 研究的基础平台。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement, in which AI systems build stronger AI systems, is one promising route to superintelligence. We study a weaker form that can be measured today: frontier agents post-training a small raw base model with public data, as a test of their research potential. We build Athanor, an open agent-native system for autonomous post-training research (AutoPostTrain) on real GPUs. We give the agents full research autonomy, low-friction engineering tools, and efficient starter code. The agents carry out the research themselves: they read datasets and evaluation results, build their training data, write and modify training algorithms, tune hyperparameters, and analyze the results to decide what to try next. They work in a sandbox and cannot use external teacher models. Athanor's services supply frozen evaluation, managed jobs, decontaminated data, a persistent research record, and a held-out suite the agents are unaware of. In a 104-hour campaign, a team of four Claude Opus 5.5 agents, each on an eight-GPU node and given no research advice, improves Qwen3-4B-Base from 15.9 to 67.3 on a six-task development suite and from 9.2 to 48.2 on a held-out suite, against 66.5 and 47.9 for the official Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration that a team of frontier agents can autonomously post-train a raw base model into a cross-domain generalist model that matches or surpasses the official instruct version on average. The campaign's full record shows that the agents reach this level by recombining known methods and each other's models: SFT on public reasoning data, RL with a rollout change one agent introduced, weight merges across agents, and rounds of self-distillation. With execution handled, today's agents already have the engineering and research ability to conquer this task. We hope Athanor provides a foundation for scaling autonomous AI research.

</details>


### [35] [OmicsBench: a long-horizon multi-turn benchmark of AI agents on research-study-scale bioinformatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21074)
*Gideon Shaked,Siddharth Nagisetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OmicsBench——一个面向AI智能体的长程、多轮生物信息学基准：97个任务由依赖子任务构成DAG（共662个子任务），每子任务对应一轮用户交互并确定性判分；12个模型×3个框架均未接近饱和（无反馈均值0.43–0.57，有反馈0.54–0.67），并配套无监督失败模式发现流程揭示质性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算生物学家越来越多地把实质性分析委托给AI智能体，实际使用场景是长程、多轮会话；但现有生物信息学智能体基准多采用单轮短任务、一次性提示，无法反映真实工作流中的子任务依赖、反馈迭代与失败模式。

Method: 构建OmicsBench：97个任务，每个任务把相互依赖的子任务组织成有向无环图（DAG），共662个子任务；每个子任务作为一轮用户提示，依据答案键进行确定性判分，并可对错误答案提供可选的上下文内反馈。在12个模型×3个执行框架上进行评测。另提出无监督失败模式发现流程：对失败尝试进行描述、嵌入与聚类，以归纳涌现的失败模式。

Result: 最慢智能体平均每题耗时3.6小时；无任何模型-框架组合接近饱和。无上下文内反馈时平均得分0.43–0.57（满分1），有反馈时0.54–0.67。多轮评测揭示单轮提示无法发现的行为：依赖前序子任务的子任务得分更低，上下文内反馈可部分挽回该损失。失败模式流程暴露出质性缺陷，如智能体用自设统计过滤替代规定过滤、凭记忆直接写答案等。

Conclusion: 单轮短任务基准高估了智能体的生物信息学能力；长程多轮、子任务依赖显式建模的评测能更真实地刻画能力边界，确定性判分需与无监督失败模式分析互补，以同时获得量化分数与质性诊断。

Abstract: Computational biologists increasingly delegate substantial analyses to AI agents in long-horizon, multi-turn sessions, yet most benchmarks of agents' bioinformatics capabilities pose a single short task with a single-shot prompt. We present OmicsBench, a long-horizon, multi-turn bioinformatics benchmark for AI agents. Each of its 97 tasks links dependent subtasks into a directed acyclic graph, for 662 underlying subtasks in all. Each subtask is posed as one user turn and graded deterministically against an answer key, with optional in-context feedback on incorrect answers. Across 12 models on three harnesses, the slowest agent averages 3.6 hours per task, and no combination comes near saturating the benchmark, with mean scores of 0.43 to 0.57 out of a possible 1 without in-context feedback and 0.54 to 0.67 with it. Multi-turn evaluation also uncovers behavior that single-shot prompts cannot, as agents score lower on subtasks that depend on earlier subtasks, and in-context feedback helps agents recover some of that loss. Because deterministic scoring gives no qualitative account of how an agent failed, we also introduce an unsupervised failure mode discovery pipeline that describes, embeds, and clusters agents' failed attempts to surface emergent failure modes. Applied to OmicsBench, this pipeline exposes qualitative shortcomings in how agents carry out bioinformatics analyses, ranging from agents applying their own statistical filters in place of the specified ones to agents writing whole answers from memory.

</details>


### [36] [Sci$Π$vot: Benchmarking Principal-Investigator Judgment at Pivotal Moments of Scientific AutoResearch Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21082)
*Youran Sun,Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Kejia Zhang,Di Wu,Yingkang Cao,Allen Zhou,Jianping Ye,Suying Liu,Jiaxuan Guo,Jianda Du,Weicheng Ye,Kecheng Liu,Hefan Zhang,Haixin Wang,Jie Xiao,Eric Yang,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文构建 Sci$Π$vot，一个基于人类纠错记录的 248 例科学判断基准，用于评估 LLM 智能体的科研判断能力；结果显示前沿模型在给定项目记录时系统性偏好原始 LLM 方案，多数模型低于随机水平，最高准确率 63.5%，低于人类新手的 73.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体已能长时间低监督运行实验，但可靠的自主科研还需科学判断力；而科研决策开放且依赖项目语境，导致科学判断难以评估，现有基准缺乏基于真实纠错情境的评测手段。

Method: 构建 Sci$Π$vot：从六个月内 26 个项目、六个科学领域、资深研究者反复介入纠正 LLM 智能体判断的记录中抽取 248 个案例；每例要求智能体在原始 LLM 方案与人工修正方案之间二选一，仅以纠错前可得的项目记录为上下文；随后对比 LLM 与人类表现，并考察推理强度、项目记录可及性与重新考虑的影响。

Result: 在中等推理强度并可见项目记录时，模型系统性偏好原始 LLM 方案；五个前沿模型中四个在所选案例上低于随机水平；项目条件下跨模型与推理设置的最高准确率为 63.5%，低于人类新手研究者的 73.4%；中等强度下所有模型有项目记录时准确率均低于无记录时；重新考虑未带来一致提升。

Conclusion: 即使提供人工修正备选，前沿模型在所选取的科研决策上仍表现不佳，且项目记录与再思考反而可能造成负面影响；Sci$Π$vot 为系统评估 LLM 智能体的科学判断力提供了基准。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can keep scientific experiments running for months with little human supervision, but reliable autonomous research also requires scientific judgment. Evaluating scientific judgment is difficult because research decisions are open-ended and depend on project context. We construct Sci$Π$vot, a 248-case benchmark of scientific judgment based on human corrections. The benchmark draws on records from six months of research in which senior researchers ran LLM agents on 26 projects across six scientific fields and repeatedly intervened to correct the agents' research judgments. In each case, an agent chooses between the original LLM plan and the human-corrected alternative, using only the project record available before the correction as context. We compare LLM and human performance and examine the effects of reasoning effort, access to the project record, and reconsideration. At medium reasoning effort with the project record, models systematically prefer the original LLM plan over the human-corrected alternative. Four of five frontier models perform below chance on the selected cases. The highest accuracy in the project condition across the tested models and reasoning effort settings is 63.5%, compared with 73.4% for fresh researchers. At medium effort, every model achieves lower accuracy with the project record than without it, and reconsideration does not consistently improve overall accuracy. Even with a human-corrected alternative available, frontier models struggle with the selected research decisions, while Sci$Π$vot supports systematic evaluation of scientific judgment for LLM agents.

</details>


### [37] [Probe-and-Resume: When and How to Evaluate Agent Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21088)
*Jiakai Xu,Tiancheng Ge,Tujie Guan,Yusheng Li,Rohan Timmaraju,Eugene Wu,Kostis Kaffes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究终端型 computer-use 环境中的测试时搜索，发现 MCTS 配置在部分可观测环境下难以显著提升成功率；提出 Probe-and-Resume 设计——仅在代理自认完成时调用 critic，并给 critic 对状态 fork 的读写执行权限去探测隐藏状态，在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上比常规 critic 的 MCTS 提升 8.4 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时搜索（探索备选动作 + critic 剪枝）理论上可提升代理表现，但在终端型环境中，代理只能观测到大状态空间的部分视图；作者发现所评估的 MCTS 配置未能显著提升任务成功率，希望定位原因并改进 critic 的使用方式。

Method: 诊断出 critic 表现差的两个来源：(1) 观测缺失评估所需的环境状态；(2) 中间状态对"轨迹最终是否成功"提供的证据有限，critic 对后续分支的排序仅略优于随机，即便给予环境访问权限改善也有限。据此设计策略：仅在代理自认已达终态的节点调用 critic（而非部分进展阶段），并赋予 critic 对计算机当前状态与初始状态之 fork 的读、写、执行权限，使其能探测并执行潜在破坏性动作以揭示轨迹遗漏的隐藏状态。

Result: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上，Probe-and-Resume 相比采用常规 critic 的 MCTS 成功率提升 8.4 个百分点，并以不到一半的成本接近同底座模型 Codex CLI 代理的成功率。

Conclusion: 部分可观测终端环境中，critic 的调用时机与访问权限比搜索结构本身更关键；将 critic 限定在终态节点并允许其通过状态 fork 主动探测，可显著提升测试时搜索的性价比。"被拒代理从停止处恢复"的机制保证探测过程不损失已有进展。

Abstract: Test-time search can improve agent performance by exploring alternative actions and using a critic to select promising branches. We study this approach in terminal-based computer-use environments, which expose only a partial view of a large underlying state, and find that the Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) configuration we evaluate does not significantly improve task success. We identify two sources of poor critic performance: the agent's observations omit environment state needed for evaluation, and intermediate states offer limited evidence of whether a trajectory will succeed. At intermediate states, critics rank continuations only modestly better than chance, and environment access improves this little. We study when to invoke a critic and what access to give it to evaluate a node. Our findings motivate a policy that invokes the critic only at nodes the agent believes are final, rather than during partial progress. Further, the policy gives the critic read, write, and execute access to a fork of the computer's current and initial state, allowing it to probe and perform potentially destructive actions to surface any hidden state the trajectory omits. We call the resulting design Probe-and-Resume: when the agent claims to be done, the critic probes a fork of its state, and a rejected agent resumes where it stopped. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, this raises success by 8.4 percentage points over MCTS with conventional critics, and approaches the success rate of the Codex CLI agent with the same underlying model at less than half its cost.

</details>


### [38] [MemComposer: Learning to Assemble Memories for LLM Agents via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21091)
*Yixuan Xie,Xueqiang Xu,Xinyi Fan,Xinxi Lyu,Tao Feng,Xing Fan,Chenlei Guo,Jie Hao,Shaowen Wang,Jiashuo Sun,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出记忆合成器 MemComposer，用基于 GRPO 的 Experience Policy Optimization（ExPO）以任务成功反馈端到端优化"选哪些记忆、选多少、如何排序"的组装策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体可借助记忆复用过往经验而无需更新参数，但仅靠语义相似度检索并不能保证记忆有用；下游表现取决于记忆的选取与呈现方式。

Method: MemComposer 联合观察当前查询与候选记忆，联合决策记忆的取舍、数量与排序；用 ExPO（GRPO 框架）在完整智能体交互中评估组装后的记忆上下文，并依据任务成功信号优化组装策略。

Result: 在 τ²-bench 三个领域、两种冻结执行器下均优于最强基线，平均记忆 token 用量分别降低 16.9% 与 4.9%；策略可迁移到未见领域、执行器与记忆库，并能直接受益于更强模型生成的记忆而无需再训练。

Conclusion: 结果表明显式利用下游任务反馈学习查询特定的记忆组装策略具有实际价值。

Abstract: Agent memory enables large language model (LLM) agents to reuse past experiences without updating their parameters. However, semantic similarity alone does not guarantee memory usefulness: downstream performance depends on how memories are selected and presented to the agent. We propose MemComposer, a memory composer that jointly examines the current query and retrieved candidates to decide which experiences to include, how many to select, and how to order them. We train the composer using Experience Policy Optimization (ExPO), a GRPO-based framework that evaluates assembled memory contexts through complete agent interactions and uses task-success feedback to optimize the assembly policy. Across three $τ^2$-bench domains with two frozen executors, MemComposer outperforms the strongest baseline for each executor while reducing average memory-token usage by 16.9% and 4.9%, respectively. The learned policy also transfers to unseen domains, executors, and memory banks, and benefits from memories generated by stronger models without further training. These results demonstrate the value of learning query-specific memory assembly using downstream task feedback.

</details>


### [39] [Planning Commitment of LLM under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21108)
*Cassie Huang,Zhanwei Cao,Yeawon Lee,Nathalia Gomez,Zhuoyi Yang,Ian G. Harris,Tiffany Do,Jiayuan Mao,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究基于LLM的规划智能体在部分可观测环境中的“激进承诺”与“保守承诺”两种策略，系统刻画其在安全性与效率之间的权衡，并探索可调的超参数混合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM规划智能体在与部分可观测环境交互前需决定规划长度：激进者基于未观测状态的假设承诺长计划，保守者仅规划已观测部分。该行为在预训练模型中隐式出现（无论直接输出计划还是生成PDDL交由经典求解器）。激进承诺带来代价高昂的失败风险，保守承诺导致过多的LLM调用，这一安全-效率权衡此前既未被系统刻画，也未被变得可控。

Method: 在两个部分可观测领域上，考察激进与保守的LLM规划器与形式化器，先验以显式统计量或常识知识两种形式提供；测试三种LLM；并设计带可调超参数的混合策略以调节安全与效率的平衡。

Result: 两种策略达到相近的准确率：保守方法失败尝试更少（更安全）但API调用更多（效率更低）；激进方法失败更频繁，且仅当先验信息充分时才能高效重规划。分析表明LLM几乎将提供的先验当作事实，先验不可靠时易致失败。

Conclusion: 安全与效率的权衡可被系统刻画，并可通过混合策略的可调超参数进行控制；同时揭示LLM过度信任先验的隐患。

Abstract: LLM-based planning agents commit to plans of different lengths before interacting with a partially observable environment. Some aggressively commit to a long plan based on assumptions about unobserved states, while others conservatively plan only over what they have observed. Both behaviors emerge implicitly in pre-trained models, whether the LLM outputs a plan directly or generates a PDDL formulation for a classical solver. Aggressive commitment risks costly failures, while conservative commitment risks excessive LLM calls. This choice pits safety against efficiency, a trade-off that has been neither characterized systematically nor made controllable. We investigate aggressive and conservative LLM planners and formalizers on two partially observable domains, where priors are provided as either explicit statistics or common-sense knowledge. Across three LLMs, both strategies reach similar accuracy. Conservative methods achieve fewer failed attempts (safety) at the cost of more API calls (efficiency). Aggressive methods fail more often but replan efficiently only when priors are informative. Interestingly, analysis shows that LLMs treat the provided priors nearly as facts, which risks failure when priors are unreliable. We also explore hybrid strategies with tunable hyperparameters that adjust the balance between safety and efficiency.

</details>


### [40] [Kolab: Benchmarking Human-Agent Collaboration in Long-Horizon Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21109)
*Shirley Anugrah Hayati,Patricia Anugrah Setiani,Ruizi Wang,Zhouyi Li,Khanh Chi Le,Young-Jun Lee,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Kolab 数据集（115+小时、50位来自40种职业的专业人士与AI智能体协作的屏幕录制及协作/产出质量判断），并据此构建评测基准，考察智能体能否预测专家对贡献的采纳/修改/拒绝，以及能否从工作流中判断协作满意度与产出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务中人类更看重与AI智能体的持续交互（如修订计划）而非一次性完成；但现有智能体基准侧重自动化，其高分不能代表真实场景下的有效协作，缺乏研究人机长程协作的专业工作流数据与评测。

Method: 构建 Kolab 数据集：采集50位专业人士（40种职业）与智能体协作解决真实长程问题的屏幕录制，共115+小时，并收集参与者的协作判断与产出质量评价；据此将能力操作化为两项任务：(1) 预测专业人士在不同工作流中如何使用智能体贡献（采纳/修改/拒绝）；(2) 验证其对协作满意度与产出质量的判断。

Result: 分析揭示多样的协作模式，体现专家何时、如何与智能体协调的重要性；前沿模型（智能体式与非智能体式）均高估专家协作意愿，且难以区分高质量与低质量协作；更长的流程上下文并不能稳定提升这些预测。

Conclusion: 前沿智能体尚不能理解与专家的长程协作；Kolab 可作为基于真实专业工作流研究人机协作的有价值数据集。

Abstract: When humans collaborate with AI agents on long-horizon tasks, what happens to the agents' contributions? For solving long-horizon tasks, humans place greater importance on ongoing interaction with AI agents (e.g., revising plans) than on an agent's ability to complete the task in a single attempt. However, most agentic benchmarks have focused more on automation. Strong performance on such benchmarks does not imply effective real-world collaboration. Professional workflows offer a useful setting, as they often involve interdependent steps. To address this gap, we introduce Kolab, a new dataset of 115+ hours of screen recordings of 50 professionals from 40 occupations collaborating with agents on realistic, long-horizon problems. Kolab also contains participants' collaboration and output quality judgments. Our analysis reveals diverse collaboration patterns, highlighting the importance of when and how professionals coordinate with agents. A collaborative agent must do two things that automation benchmarks never test: anticipate whether experts will adopt, modify, or reject its contribution once it leaves the chat window to guide its next steps, and assess collaboration quality from the unfolding workflow without constant expert feedback. Our benchmark operationalizes both: (1) forecasting how professionals use agent contributions at diverse workflows, and (2) verifying their judgments of collaboration satisfaction and output quality. Findings show that frontier models, both agentic and non-agentic, overestimate collaboration with experts and struggle to distinguish between high- and low-quality collaboration. Longer workflow context does not consistently improve these predictions. As frontier agents cannot yet understand long-horizon collaboration with experts, we position Kolab as a valuable dataset for studying human-agent collaboration grounded in real professional workflows.

</details>


### [41] [CleanRM: Reward Modeling from Noisy Feedback with a Few Clean Feedback for RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21138)
*Licheng Pan,Haocheng Yang,Yuzhou Chen,Yichen Sun,Yuan Lu,Yunfei Teng,Jiajun Fan,Guangyi Liu,Zhixuan Chu,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CleanRM利用少量干净反馈，通过最优传输校准样本级噪声率，构建无偏的奖励模型训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好数据中的反馈噪声普遍存在（源于主观人类判断、标注错误和不完美的LLM评审），标准奖励建模会得到对干净风险的有偏估计。经典去噪方法假设样本间噪声率一致，但反馈数据的噪声本质上是样本级的，该假设失效。

Method: 提出CleanRM：使用少量干净反馈，通过最优传输校准样本级噪声率。具体做法是将从噪声反馈反推出的偏好数据的联合分布与干净反馈的分布对齐，然后用噪声校正后的替代损失训练奖励模型。

Result: 理论上证明了所得目标对干净风险是无偏的；大量实验表明CleanRM优于最先进的去噪基线，并在下游RLHF对齐中产生更有效的效果。

Conclusion: 通过最优传输进行样本级噪声率校准，可在样本级噪声场景下实现无偏奖励建模，并提升下游对齐性能。

Abstract: Feedback noise is pervasive in preference data, arising from subjective human judgments, annotation mistakes, and imperfect LLM judges. Standard reward modeling trained on such feedback yields a biased estimate of the clean risk. Although classical denoising methods are directly applicable to noisy feedback learning, they generally assume a uniform noise ratio across samples. However, this assumption fails for feedback data, where noise is intrinsically sample-wise. To address this challenge, we propose CleanRM, which uses a few clean feedback to calibrate sample-wise noise ratios via optimal transport. Specifically, CleanRM aligns the joint distribution of preferences reverted from noisy feedback with that of clean feedback and then trains the reward model using a noise-corrected surrogate loss. Theoretically, we prove that the resulting objective is unbiased with respect to the clean risk. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CleanRM outperforms state-of-the-art denoising baselines and yields more effective downstream RLHF alignment.

</details>


### [42] [All-in-One Memory: Process-Rewarded Reinforcement Learning for Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21147)
*Bingbing Wang,Jingjie Lin,Weiming Qiao,Jing Li,Ruifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AiO-Mem，将外部记忆的检索、写入与停止操作统一为共享证据状态上的转移，并在统一奖励空间内直接评估每个动作的语义质量，以过程对齐强化学习优化记忆策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在长周期历史（多轮对话、长文档）问答中依赖外部记忆，但现有方法将检索与记忆构建分开优化，缺乏统一奖励空间来评估搜索、写入与停止决策。

Method: 1) 将异构历史编译为可溯源的多视图事件，作为检索与记忆写入的共享基底；2) 统一的记忆策略 actor 在共享证据状态上展开完整交互轨迹；3) 独立的轨迹条件过程奖励模型通过证据锚定的监督微调与轨迹级 DPO 训练，为轨迹分配过程分数；4) 分数聚合为动作均衡的轨迹奖励，由 Process-Aligned DAPO（PA-DAPO）优化记忆策略。

Result: 在长上下文记忆基准上，AiO-Mem 相比静态记忆系统与经强化学习训练的记忆智能体，一致提升答案准确率。

Conclusion: 通过共享证据状态与统一奖励空间联合优化记忆的检索、写入与停止决策，并以过程奖励对齐的策略优化，可有效提升长周期记忆问答表现。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly rely on external memory to answer queries grounded in long-horizon histories such as multi-session dialogues and lengthy documents. However, existing memory methods often optimize retrieval and memory construction separately, without a shared reward space for evaluating search, writing, and stopping decisions. To address this, we propose All-in-One Memory (AiO-Mem), which organizes these operations as transitions over a shared evidence state and directly evaluates the semantic quality of each action in a unified reward space. AiO-Mem compiles heterogeneous histories into source-traceable multi-view events, providing a shared substrate for retrieval and memory writing. A unified memory policy actor then rolls out complete interaction trajectories over this shared evidence state. A separate trajectory-conditioned process reward model is trained through evidence-grounded supervised fine-tuning and trajectory-level DPO to assign process scores to the collected trajectories. These scores are aggregated into an action-balanced trajectory reward and used by Process-Aligned DAPO (PA-DAPO) to optimize the memory policy. Across long-context memory benchmarks, AiO-Mem consistently improves answer accuracy over both static memory systems and RL-trained memory agents.

</details>


### [43] [GRADS: Graph-Regulated Agentic Data Synthesis with Controllable Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21167)
*Gaocheng Yang,Ruijie Tao,Kai Ye,Senhua Zhang,Zhengqin Zhu,Ziyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GRADS：以证据-推理依赖图为核心的智能体式合成数据框架，实现显式难度控制的多跳长程任务生成，并在安全、软工、科学文档三域验证其难度分层与训练增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体需从大规模语料中检索精确信息，多跳长程任务仍是难点。现有合成流水线多通过扩充种子任务来制造"表观"深度与广度，但新引入的证据与答案耦合弱，导致意图难度与实测难度错配，扩充不等于必要推理。

Method: GRADS 维护一张刻画"推导答案所需证据与推理依赖"的图；以"种子选择—证据扩展—问答构建—验证"构成闭环，每一步都条件化于相关子图进行生成，从而保留不可或缺的推理链、避免无关上下文累积，并据此实现显式难度控制。

Result: 在安全、软件工程、科学文档三域实验中，GRADS 稳定产出可验证任务，且经独立求解器测评显示难度分布呈现经验可分性。在匹配训练预算下：SFT 将解答终止率提升至 95% 以上，同时工具调用与轮次最多减少 75%；RL 进一步将工具使用再降最多 19.5%；所得 4B 模型在留出域基准上软工任务与 Sonnet 4 持平，在科学与安全域超过之。

Conclusion: 以图结构显式建模证据与推理依赖，是控制合成数据真实难度的有效手段；高质量、难度可控的合成数据可在小参数模型上显著提升工具效率与领域表现。

Abstract: Deep research agents are increasingly deployed to retrieve precise information from large corpora, yet multi-hop, long-horizon tasks remain challenging. Synthetic data generation has emerged as a scalable solution, with existing pipelines typically expanding seed tasks to increase their apparent depth and breadth. However, this expansion does not reliably translate into necessary reasoning: newly introduced evidence may be only weakly coupled to the answer, creating a mismatch between intended and empirical difficulty. To address this problem, we introduce GRADS, a graph-guided agentic data synthesis framework with explicit difficulty control. GRADS runs a closed loop of seed selection, evidence expansion, question--answer construction, and verification while maintaining a graph of the evidence and inference dependencies required to derive each answer. At each step, it conditions generation on a relevant subgraph, preserving indispensable inference without accumulating irrelevant context. Experiments across security, software-engineering, and scientific-document domains show that GRADS consistently generates verified tasks with empirically separated difficulty distributions, as measured by independent solvers. Moreover, under matched training budgets, post-training on this data delivers consistent gains at every level: SFT raises the solution-termination rate to over 95% while cutting tool calls and turns by up to 75%, RL trims tool use by up to a further 19.5%, and on our held-out, domain-specific benchmarks, the resulting 4B model matches Sonnet~4 on software engineering and surpasses it on the scientific and security domains.

</details>


### [44] [MemScope: Evolving Agent Memory Architectures through Localized Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21170)
*Miao Su,Yucan Guo,Zixuan Li,Long Bai,Zhongni Hou,Xiaolong Jin,Jiafeng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemScope：用原始执行轨迹与运行时记忆状态作为细粒度证据，定位失败点并对记忆架构代码做有界原地修改，实现局部化自演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有自演化方法依赖评测分数或压缩摘要等粗粒度反馈，难以在长程、多组件交互的智能体系统中精确定位失败，导致缺少代码级修改目标、更新范围约束弱、常出现大范围重写。

Method: 提出 MemScope 框架：由工具使用的 Patch Agent 联合检查原始执行轨迹、运行时记忆状态与当前架构代码，识别修复目标，并在提供方身份与记忆兼容性约束下施加有界、原地（in-place）编辑。

Result: GAIA 上轨迹+记忆检查变体达 86.67% 准确率，优于仅轨迹 70.00% 与仅摘要 63.33%；xBench 上演化后架构在 DeepSeek V3.2 下由 49 提升至 55 正确，Kimi K2.7 Code 下由 50 提升至 53；ALFWorld 未见任务成功率由 50.75% 升至 71.64%；架构快照迁移的代码变更比例中位数为 4.4%。

Conclusion: 细粒度执行证据可作为局部化、原地记忆架构演化的可靠基础，在提升性能的同时保持代码改动最小。

Abstract: Agent memory architectures define how LLM agents store and reuse experience across tasks, but they are typically fixed after deployment, limiting their adaptability across task types. Recent self-evolving approaches instead adapt these memory architectures using execution feedback, typically represented as evaluation scores or compressed summaries. However, such coarse-grained feedback is insufficient for precise failure localization in current agent systems, where long-horizon task execution involves multiple interacting components. Without a precise failure location, existing approaches lack a clear code-level target for modification, leaving the update scope weakly constrained and often leading to large-scale rewrites of an otherwise capable memory architecture. We propose MemScope, a framework for evidence-grounded, localized evolution of an existing memory architecture. Its tool-using Patch Agent jointly inspects raw execution traces, runtime memory state, and current architecture code to identify repair targets and apply bounded, in-place edits under provider-identity and memory-compatibility constraints. On GAIA, the trace-and-memory inspection variant achieves 86.67% accuracy, compared with 70.00% for trace-only and 63.33% for summary-only feedback. Under xBench tasks, the evolved memory architecture improves over its original version from 49 to 55 correct answers with DeepSeek V3.2 and from 50 to 53 with Kimi K2.7 Code. On disjoint ALFWorld unseen tasks, success rises from 50.75% to 71.64%. Across archived architecture-snapshot transitions, the median code-change ratio is 4.4%. These results support fine-grained execution evidence as a basis for localized, in-place memory-architecture evolution.

</details>


### [45] [AgentEval: Evidence-Bounded Assessment of Stateful Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21177)
*Ruiqi Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentEval系统，将受合约约束的评估记录贯穿工具执行、状态与事件捕获、报告限定与用例维护，区分"智能体做了什么""证据证明了什么""结论当前是否有支持"，使评估范围、证据支持与报告复用可检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务完成与否不能判定有状态智能体是否履行了义务；已记录判定不等于其支撑证据仍有效。现有评估缺少对评估范围、证据有效性与报告可复用性的可检查机制。

Method: 构建AgentEval系统：以合约绑定评估记录，串联工具执行、状态与事件捕获、报告qualification与用例维护。开展三类研究：(1) 32次tool-world管理，由独立检查交叉验证；(2) 对48个证据视图进行受控干预以暴露绑定故障；(3) Scenario Studio将审阅用例连接至12份两轮报告，含24个独立核验答案；另在192个留出执行上做公共基准研究。

Result: 32次管理中，独立检查在全部30个完整episode上一致，可区分已满足/被违反/未决义务，并保留2次中断管理；48个证据视图干预暴露36个仅靠哈希检查遗漏的绑定故障且不改变智能体行为；12份两轮报告含24个独立核验答案；192个留出执行中揭示10个在整体任务失败内完成的返回子目标，并分离出缺失证据的影响。

Conclusion: 可执行的评估记录使评估范围、证据支持与报告复用在智能体评估工作流中可被检查。

Abstract: Task completion alone does not determine whether a stateful agent satisfied its obligations, and a recorded judgment does not guarantee that its supporting evidence remains valid. We present AgentEval, a system that carries a contract-bound assessment record through tool execution, state and event capture, report qualification, and case maintenance. It separates what the agent did, what the evidence establishes, and whether the conclusion is supported for present use. On 32 tool-world administrations, independent checks agree on all 30 complete episodes, distinguishing satisfied, violated, and unresolved obligations; two interrupted administrations are retained. Controlled interventions on 48 evidence views expose 36 binding faults missed by hash-only checks without changing agent behavior. Scenario Studio connects reviewed cases to 12 two-turn reports with 24 independently checked answers. A separate public-benchmark study on 192 held-out executions reveals ten completed return subgoals within broader task failures and isolates the effect of missing evidence. These studies show how an executable assessment record makes scope, evidential support, and report reuse inspectable across an agent evaluation workflow.

</details>


### [46] [Does Latent Chain-of-Thought Actually Reason? A Single, Unifying Taxonomy for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21182)
*Jingjie Qiu,Sheldon Yu,Xunyi Jiang,Jiajin Liu,Zihan Huang,Zhendong Chu,Tong Yu,Julian McAuley,Junda Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文系统检验"隐式思维链"(latent CoT)究竟执行了什么计算，指出高准确率与可解码的中间答案不能证明模型依赖隐式链推理；据此提出区分"链表征什么"与"模型如何使用链"的分类学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式思维链用连续隐状态替代显式推理步骤，但尚不清楚这些步骤实际承担何种计算；且高任务准确率与可解码的中间答案并不能证明模型确实依靠隐式链得到答案，已有研究报告（占位符令牌、坍缩叠加、步骤依赖的因果作用）之间看似互相矛盾。

Method: 覆盖21个模型-任务配置（3种训练配方、3类推理任务、2种骨干模型）；结合链删除（chain deletion）与内容替换（content replacement），辅以探针（probes）与轨迹分析，检验链是否影响答案、链编码了什么、状态如何演化；并引入新的分类学框架。

Result: 逻辑任务上，COCONUT常在链开始前答案已可读，状态早期即收敛，删除链几乎不改变预测；逐步监督可产生有序的推理步骤布局并令模型依赖链，但有序链仍可能仅作占位符（删除损害性能而内容替换不改变答案）。算术任务上所有测试配方均依赖链内容。

Conclusion: 可读的隐状态本身不足以作为推理证据；应区分链"表征什么"与"模型如何使用它"。占位符令牌、坍缩叠加、步骤依赖的因果角色分别对应不同任务、训练配方或测量下的不同链类型；明确研究所检验的性质（是否使用链、链持有什么、状态如何移动）后，关于隐式推理的主张才具可比性。

Abstract: Latent chain-of-thought replaces written reasoning steps with continuous hidden states, giving models additional steps to compute before answering. It remains unclear what computation these steps actually perform. High task accuracy and decodable intermediate answers do not establish that a model uses its latent chain to reach an answer. We study this question across twenty-one model-task configurations covering three training recipes, three reasoning tasks, and two backbones. We combine chain deletion and content replacement with probes and trajectory analysis to test whether the chain affects the answer, what it encodes, and how its states change. On logical tasks, COCONUT often carries an answer that is already readable before the chain begins. Its states settle early, and deleting the chain leaves predictions almost unchanged. Per-step supervision produces an ordered layout of reasoning steps and can make the model depend on the chain. Yet even an ordered chain can act as a placeholder, with deletion hurting performance while content replacement leaves answers unchanged. On arithmetic tasks, all tested recipes show dependence on chain content. These results show why readable latent states alone are insufficient evidence of reasoning. We introduce a taxonomy that separates what a chain represents from how the model uses it. Seen this way, earlier reports no longer compete: placeholder tokens, collapsed superposition and step-dependent causal roles are each consistent with a different kind of chain, each obtained on a different task, training recipe or measurement. Once a study says which property it tested, whether the model uses the chain, what the chain holds, or how its state moves, claims about latent reasoning can be compared.

</details>


### [47] [Mine, Sample, Render: Test-Time Diversity Alignment Of Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21185)
*Kostas Triaridis,Morris E. Sharp,Nebojsa Jojic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出测试时方法 MINE-SAMPLE-RENDER（MSR），将多样性从生成模型中外部化为“挖掘—采样—渲染”三步流程，在多个模态任务上提升输出多样性与语料分布对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型虽能产出高质量单体结果，但即使被明确要求，也难以保证采样的多样性。作者认为多样性不应寄望于模型隐式实现，而应被显式地建模与执行。

Method: 三步测试时流水线：(i) 从真实作品语料中挖掘可解释的类别化模式（categorical schema）并提供采样统计量；(ii) 依据语料衍生统计量采样属性配置；(iii) 将生成模型仅作为给定配置的条件渲染器（conditional renderer），不承担多样性职责。

Result: 在天气报告生成、用户模拟、网页设计、文生图、智能体生成3D资产五类任务上评估，MSR 引导的生成在输出质量与多样性上均有提升，且与语料分布的匹配度更好。

Conclusion: 将多样性外部化为语料驱动的显式采样过程是一种有效的测试时策略：生成模型专注渲染质量，多样性由可解释的统计采样保证，方法跨任务、跨模态具备通用性。

Abstract: Modern generative models produce high-quality individual outputs but struggle to sample diversely, even when explicitly instructed to do so. We introduce MINE-SAMPLE-RENDER (MSR), a test-time approach that externalizes the diversity into an explicit, corpus-derived sampling procedure. Our pipeline (i) mines an interpretable categorical schema from a corpus of real artifacts and supplies statistics for sampling; (ii) samples attribute configurations from the corpus-derived statistics; (iii) uses a generative model purely as a conditional renderer of a sampled configuration. Across weather report generation, user simulation, webpage design, text-to-image generation, and agent-generated 3D assets, we evaluate the diversity and output quality of MSR-guided generation and show that MSR improves alignment with corpus distributions and diversity of generated outputs.

</details>


### [48] [Condition-Contrastive Extrapolation for On-Policy Distillation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21195)
*Wenxuan Luo,Chenxi Li,Haoyue Liu,Yongxin Guo,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CCE-OPD，通过条件对比（掩码条件的前缀比较+有界对数概率对比）使教师监督对题目条件敏感，减少对无效续写的偏好，提升学生模型推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation（OPD）用强教师模型监督学生自生成轨迹，但当条件于学生生成的前缀时，教师可能偏好违反题目前提的无效续写，导致OPD强化错误推理步骤。

Method: CCE-OPD包含两部分：(1) 固定前缀比较：同一冻结教师同时评估完整问题与掩码掉某一条件的对照版本；(2) 有界对数概率对比：据此重加权教师分布，构造辅助蒸馏目标。

Result: 在200个诊断推理状态上，条件对比将偏好无效续写的比率从27.0%降至16.0%；在六个数学基准上，Qwen3-8B蒸馏到0.6B与1.7B学生时avg@8分别平均提升1.60和1.95个百分点；在四个迁移基准上取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 条件对比是一种有效且轻量的修正手段，可缓解OPD中的条件不敏感问题，并在同域与迁移场景下均带来稳定收益。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves a student language model by training on its own generated trajectories with supervision from a stronger teacher. However, when conditioned on student-generated prefixes, the teacher can favor invalid continuations that violate the question's premises, causing OPD to encourage erroneous reasoning steps. As a remedy, we introduce Condition-Contrastive Extrapolated On-Policy Distillation (CCE-OPD), which makes teacher supervision sensitive to question conditions. CCE-OPD incorporates (1) a fixed-prefix comparison in which the same frozen teacher evaluates the complete question and a counterpart with a selected condition masked, and (2) a bounded log-probability contrast that reweights the teacher distribution into an auxiliary distillation target. On 200 diagnostic reasoning states, condition contrast reduces the rate of preferring invalid continuations from 27.0% to 16.0%. Extensive experiments across six mathematical benchmarks show that CCE-OPD improves avg@8 over OPD by an average of 1.60 and 1.95 percentage points when distilling Qwen3-8B into 0.6B and 1.7B students, respectively. It also achieves the strongest results on four transfer benchmarks.

</details>


### [49] [Who May Release an Agent's Promise? Standing over Commitments in Multi-Party Chat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21203)
*Lan Wei,Bingyu Shen,Jiaqi Tang,Qing Lu,Kuan Huang,Meng Xu,Miles Q. Li,Bin Hu,Boyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"standing(权限资格)"概念:承诺只能由债权人或被授权的第三方解除;在受控聊天环境中区分"未授权解除"与"override(执行已解除承诺)"两类错误,并评测六个模型,发现两者相互独立、现有干预难以兼治,而"承诺台账+一段解除政策"最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 助手机器人为团队服务时会对个人(Alic 等)做出承诺,而团队中任何人都可能口头取消。作者希望明确"谁有权解除承诺"这一规范性问题,并诊断模型在此约束下的失效模式,而非笼统衡量"指令遵循"。

Method: 提出 standing 原则(仅债权人或其授权者/运营方政策指定者可释放承诺,与职级无关)。在受控聊天环境系统中改变"谁提出取消",将错误分解为 unauthorized release(服从无资格者)与 override(执行已被合法解除的承诺)。用 oracle 探针分别注入"规则陈述""债权人身份""承诺是否仍被欠"等信息以解耦两类错误,并测试 thinking 模式的影响,最后评估"自维护承诺台账"与"一段解除政策"两种机制。

Result: 六模型中:较小的开源模型倾向 override 债权人(Qwen3.5-9B 69%);较大模型会因同伴一句话放弃承诺(Qwen3.8-27B 89%,gpt-6.1-sol 83%);几乎所有模型都听从旁观者的"那就继续吧"。Oracle 实验显示两类错误可解耦:陈述规则或指定债权人可抑制未授权解除但不影响 override,后者在四个模型中三个反而上升;只有"承诺是否仍被欠"的信息能降低 override。thinking 模式使小模型表现趋近大模型。自维护承诺台账在五分之四模型中消除 override;配合一页解除政策达到或超过最佳 oracle 做法,gpt-6-luna 严格准确率由 49%(仅政策)升至 83%。

Conclusion: 承诺解除能力取决于"资格"判断而非职位或语气权威,且未授权解除与 override 是两类独立失效,需分别干预;显式维护承诺状态(台账)并配以简明解除政策是实用且高效的缓解方案。代码、提示、输出与标注均已公开。

Abstract: When an assistant serving a team makes a promise to Alice, who may cancel it? We call this standing: only the creditor (the person owed) or someone the creditor or operator policy authorizes may release a commitment, regardless of rank. In a controlled chat environment we vary who cancels and separate two errors: unauthorized release (obeying someone without standing) and override (executing a released promise). Across six models, smaller open ones override the creditor (Qwen3.5-9B: 69%); larger ones drop promises on a peer's direct word (Qwen3.8-27B: 89%; gpt-6.1-sol: 83%); nearly all act on a bystander's "go ahead after all". Oracles dissociate the errors: stating the rule or the creditor curbs unauthorized release but not override, which the latter even raises in three of four models; among them, only knowing whether the promise is still owed cuts override. Enabling thinking moves smaller models the way scale does. A self-kept commitment ledger stops override in four of five models; with a one-paragraph release policy it matches or beats the best oracle store, raising gpt-6-luna's strict accuracy from 49% (policy alone) to 83%. Code, prompts, outputs and labels are released.

</details>


### [50] [Entertain, then Cancel: How Thinking Makes Reasoning Models Read Some and Or Literally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21204)
*Zhizhuo Kou,Jian Yang,Pengfei Hao,Junyu Luo,Jingshu Peng,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究推理模型"思考时长"对日常语用推断（如"some"的隐涵义）的影响：固定权重、仅改变思考量，发现思考会让模型把逻辑词按字面逻辑解读，从而取消"some→not all"、"or→not both"等隐涵义，而语境驱动的推断反而改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型允许用户控制思考时长，但"额外思考"如何影响日常语用推断（scalar implicature 等）尚不清楚；现有评测只看准确率，不关注模型"如何解读"。

Method: 采用受控实验：固定模型权重，仅改变思考量，记录其选择的解读；并用第二件工具在推理轨迹的多个节点截断思考、强制作答，定位解读发生转变的位置。此外提出一种探索性的推理时补救方法——从早期（富化）解读出发作答。

Result: 在多数被测的开源推理模型及 GPT-5 中，思考会取消 some（not all）与 or（not both）的隐涵义；而间接回答等语境驱动推断则有所提升。推理轨迹显示模型先考虑富化解读、随后将其取消。从早期解读作答的补救方法可在不损失字面准确率的情况下恢复这些隐涵义。

Conclusion: 思考使模型把逻辑词当作纯逻辑来读，牺牲语用推断换取字面逻辑准确性；对推理模型的评测应同时衡量其"如何解读"而非仅看准确性，并可通过推理时干预恢复语用能力。

Abstract: When a speaker says ``some students passed'', listeners infer that not all of them did. Reasoning models now let users decide how long a model thinks before it answers, but we do not know what extra thinking does to such everyday inferences. We study this directly: we keep a model's weights fixed, change only how much it thinks, and record which reading it chooses. A second tool cuts the model's own reasoning at successive points and asks for an answer, showing where the reading changes. Thinking makes models read logical words as logic: it cancels the implicatures of \emph{some} (\emph{not all}) and \emph{or} (\emph{not both}) in most tested open reasoning models and in GPT-5, while context-driven inferences such as indirect answers improve. The trace shows why: models first entertain the enriched reading and cancel it later. Answering from that early reading, an exploratory inference-time remedy, recovers these implicatures without losing literal accuracy. Evaluations of reasoning models should measure how they interpret, not only how accurate they are.

</details>


### [51] [Rubric Judgments Lack Uncertainty: Conformal Weighting for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21214)
*Licheng Pan,Haocheng Yang,Haoxuan Li,Yuzhou Chen,Yichen Sun,Yuan Lu,Xiaoxi Li,Hao Wang,Zhixuan Chu,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架URW，用保形校准将评分细则各准则的二元判定转为软不确定性信号并加权聚合，提升偏好准确率与下游RL对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分细则（rubric）的LLM输出评估将多准则的二元判定等权聚合为总分，忽视了不同准则判定确定性的显著异质性；不确定的判定被无差别纳入奖励估计，可能给下游强化学习提供误导性训练信号。

Method: URW（Uncertainty-aware Rubric Weighting）为免训练框架：借助固定池的改写判定模板，从LLM验证器处引出软不确定性信号，再通过split-conformal p值将其转换为逐准则权重，并具备可证明的覆盖保证。

Result: 大量实验表明，URW一致提升成对偏好准确率，并在下游RL对齐中取得更强表现。

Conclusion: 准则级判定不确定性可被有效量化并用于准则加权，从而改善基于评分细则的奖励估计与RL训练信号质量。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is widely used to assess large language model (LLM) outputs by decomposing quality into multiple criteria and assigning each a binary judgment from an LLM verifier. However, judgment certainty varies substantially across criteria. Existing methods uniformly aggregate these judgments into a final score, implicitly treating every criterion as equally certain and overlooking this heterogeneity. As a result, uncertain judgments remain incorporated into reward estimation without distinction, potentially yielding misleading training signals for downstream reinforcement learning (RL). To address this limitation, we propose Uncertainty-aware Rubric Weighting (URW), a training-free framework that quantifies criterion-level judgment uncertainty via conformal calibration. Specifically, URW elicits a soft uncertainty signal from a fixed pool of paraphrased judgment templates and converts it into a per-criterion weight using split-conformal $p$-values with provable coverage guarantees. Extensive experiments demonstrate that URW consistently improves pairwise preference accuracy and delivers stronger downstream RL alignment.

</details>


### [52] [AutoOnboarder: Autonomously Onboarding LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21218)
*Tanay Arora,Cong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AutoOnboarder：由"Onboarder"编码智能体监督，迭代调整基座模型、提示词与 harness 源码，在少样本条件下同时提升性能并降低成本；在基因组测序预授权表单提交任务上达到或超过 Gemini 3.5 Flash 基线，成本最高低 29×，但表现方差大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体式大模型在被放入 harness（中介其观测与动作的软件）后推理能力会下降。为弥合这一性能损失，作者希望构建一个能自动将智能体适配到具体任务的系统，类比企业中培训新员工的入职流程。

Method: AutoOnboarder 将智能体置于一个智能"Onboarder"编码智能体监督下，迭代地定制其基座模型、提示词和 harness 源代码，以同时优化性能与成本。类似真实入职流程，Onboarder 仅获数十个示例；所有评估数据隔离在独立虚拟机上以防泄漏。作者在基因组测序预授权表单提交任务上构建并测试该系统。

Result: 自主完成 onboarding 的智能体可达到或超过 Gemini 3.5 Flash 基线的性能，成本最高降低 29×。但这些智能体仍不完美，且 onboarding 过程的最终性能波动很大。

Conclusion: 未来 onboarding 系统需要为 Onboarder 提供稳健的环境，包括结构化的思维接口、（受控的）互联网访问以及更宽的模型搜索空间。

Abstract: Agentic large language models have been documented to lose reasoning capability when placed inside a harness, the software that mediates their observations and actions. To reconcile these losses, we introduce AutoOnboarder, an autonomous system that adapts an agent to a task the way a trainer onboards a new employee. It places the agent under the supervision of an intelligent ``Onboarder'' coding agent, which iteratively tailors its foundation model, prompt, and harness source code to simultaneously improve performance and decrease cost. Like a real onboarding process, the Onboarder is only given tens of examples, and all evaluation data is isolated on a separate virtual machine to prevent leakage. We report the lessons learned from building and testing AutoOnboarder on genomic-sequencing prior authorization form submission. Autonomously onboarded agents are able to match or exceed the performance of a Gemini 3.5 Flash baseline at up to 29$\times$ lower costs. However, these agents remain imperfect, and the Onboarding process varies greatly in terms of final performance. We conclude that future Onboarding systems require a robust environment for the Onboarder, including a structured thought interface, (controlled) internet access, and a wide model search space.

</details>


### [53] [Auditing Escalation Reliability in Healthcare LLM Agents: A Perturbation-Controlled Benchmark of Workflow-State Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21270)
*Zihao Zheng,Junyi Yao,Baichuan Li,Jiayu Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出基于400个合成医疗工作流episode的episode级评测协议，暴露开源权重模型在证据缺失/冲突/滞后等动态条件下对"升级求助"的严重漏报，并用透明的工作流感知控制器提升效用、降低漏报，但指出规则检测器对未见解说改写完全失效，主张工作流状态评测与路由控制自身的泛化检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可信生物医学AI的核心不仅是静态输入下的答案准确性，更是在证据缺失、过期、冲突或延迟时做出可靠的动作选择（作答/澄清/弃答/升级）。现有评测多停留在静态问答准确率，未在episode层面考察包含成本权衡的动作决策，因此作者构建可控的合成工作流评测协议来量化这一能力缺口。

Method: 构建400个合成医疗工作流episode：40个手工撰写的语义种子，覆盖4类任务，配2种表层参数化与5种受控变体。动作空间为ANSWER/ASK CLARIFICATION/ABSTAIN/ESCALATE，按预先声明的5:1漏报与不必要升级成本比评分。参照动作为单一作者按研究策略标注（非临床医师裁定金标准）。评测5个1.7B–9.7B开源权重配置；分析verbalized confidence的校准（ECE、失败AUROC）；提出透明的工作流感知控制器，并与仅基于置信度的开发集调优策略对比；以30概念词汇压力测试检验规则检测器的泛化；配套种子聚类统计、消融、跨任务迁移及原始输出与manifest以便审计。

Result: 干净条件下决策准确率达0.97，但经不确定性提示的模型漏报32–97%的必要升级。更强的9.7B配置在冲突条件下从0.97降至0.78，缩小但未消除差距。言语化置信度不一致（ECE 0.10–0.54，失败AUROC 0.48–0.77），仅用置信度调优的策略无任何效用增益。工作流感知控制器对每个模型均提升5:1效用，漏报降至0.12–0.24，特异度0.83–0.93。然而30概念词汇压力测试中，各规则检测器召回率从触发短语上的1.00降至未见改写上的0.00。

Conclusion: 结果支持将工作流状态纳入显式评测，说明当前模型的高静态准确率不能迁移为可靠的动作选择；同时表明看似有效的路由/控制器需要单独立项的泛化测试，控制器的效果声明仅限本benchmark。作者主张将episode级、含成本权衡的动作评测与控制器泛化审计作为可信生物医学AI的标准实践。

Abstract: Trustworthy biomedical AI requires reliable action selection when evidence is missing, stale, conflicting, or late, not only accurate answers on static inputs. We instantiate an episode-level evaluation protocol as 400 synthetic healthcare workflow episodes: 40 hand-authored semantic seeds across four task classes, two surface parameterizations, and five controlled variants, scored on ANSWER/ASK CLARIFICATION/ABSTAIN/ESCALATE under a pre-declared 5:1 missed-versus-unnecessary escalation cost. Reference actions are single-author study-policy labels, not clinician-adjudicated ground truth. Across five open-weight configurations (1.7B--9.7B), clean decision accuracy reaches 0.97, yet uncertainty-prompted models miss 32--97% of required escalations. The stronger 9.7B configuration narrows but does not remove the gap, falling from 0.97 clean accuracy to 0.78 under conflict. Verbalized confidence is inconsistent (ECE 0.10--0.54; failure AUROC 0.48--0.77), and a development-tuned confidence-only policy yields no utility gain. A transparent workflow-aware controller improves 5:1 utility for every model and reduces missed escalations to 0.12--0.24, at specificity 0.83--0.93. However, a 30-concept lexical stress test reduces each rule detector's recall from 1.00 on trigger phrases to 0.00 on unseen paraphrases, limiting the controller claim to this benchmark. Seed-clustered statistics, ablations, cross-task transfer, raw outputs, and manifests are packaged for audit. The results motivate explicit workflow-state evaluation while showing that apparently effective routing controls need their own generalization tests.

</details>


### [54] [Diviner: Agentic RCA with Human-Governed Adaptation for Large-Scale Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21281)
*Pradipta Ghosh,Gaurav Mitra,Shripad Nadgowda,Akshil Shah,Junyu Liu,Canturk Isci,Syamla Bandla,Xinyao Hu,Onkar Deshpande,Anik Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向大规模分布式系统的代理式根因分析系统 Diviner，以“编码—执行—评估—适配”四阶段可靠性生命周期组织，结合评审过的历史事件、服务方专家知识、类型化工具与事件知识图谱，并用自适应 Lead 与上下文隔离的 worker 做时点证据推理，再以轨迹比对和回放审批实现可验证与持续更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式系统的根因分析困难在于单一症状会跨越服务、数据、配置、算力与组织边界，响应者需拼凑不完整证据并协调掌握不同因果链片段的专家。通用语言模型代理仅靠工具接入无法解决：可能使用过期或时点错配的上下文、把查询失败误判为无症状、报告无依据的系统状态，并给按人类节奏设计的可观测性服务带来过载。

Method: 系统在 Meta 部署，围绕四阶段可靠性生命周期：Encode 将大量已评审事件与服务水平专家经验编码为有界、版本化的技能、工作流、类型化工具和事件知识图谱；Execute 采用自适应 Lead 与上下文隔离的 worker，将相关知识应用于时点证据并维护与证据关联的假设；Evaluate 将保存的调查轨迹与响应者复核结论对比，度量精确根因、有用方向和 Groundedness（有据性）；Adapt 依据反复出现的缺口与值班反馈生成聚焦的运维知识更新，仅在回放、质量检查与责任人批准后才准入。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅说明系统已在 Meta 投入运行，并通过评估与适配机制实现从轨迹到知识更新的闭环验证（专家评审、回放与质量检查为准入条件）。

Conclusion: 代理式根因分析需以领域知识编码、证据可追溯的隔离式执行和可验证的评估—更新闭环为可靠性基础，而非单纯依赖模型与工具调用；Diviner 提供了这一工程化生命周期的参考范式。

Abstract: Root-cause analysis (RCA) in large-scale distributed systems is challenging because a single symptom can cross service, data, configuration, compute, and organizational boundaries. Responders must assemble incomplete evidence and coordinate experts who hold different parts of the causal path. Generic language-model agents do not solve this problem through tool access alone: they can use stale or mistimed context, interpret failed queries as absent symptoms, report unsupported system state, and overload observability services designed for human-paced use.
  We present Diviner (Domain-Informed Verifiable Incident Navigation through Evidence and Review), an agentic RCA system operating at Meta and organized around a four-stage reliability lifecycle. Encode combines a large corpus of reviewed incidents with service-owner expertise to produce scoped, versioned skills, workflows, typed tools, and an incident knowledge graph. Execute uses an adaptive Lead and context-isolated workers to apply relevant knowledge to point-in-time evidence and maintain evidence-linked hypotheses. Evaluate compares saved investigation trajectories with responder-reviewed outcomes to measure exact cause, useful direction, and Groundedness. Adapt uses recurring gaps and on-call feedback to derive focused operational- knowledge updates, admitting them only after replay, quality checks, and owner approval.

</details>


### [55] [Mallea: Building, Steering, and Memorizing Worlds with Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21283)
*Xuanyi Liu,Ruiqi Li,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Mallea 系统，在"代码世界模型"框架下以同一套对象为中心，统一物体表示、行为修订与视觉记忆，实现从文本构建、操控并记忆可执行的世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码世界模型通过显式状态与可执行规则表达世界动态，但如何在其内部协调物体表示、行为修订与视觉记忆尚不清楚，本文即研究这一问题。

Method: 编码智能体从提示中推断物体交互需求，联合规划物体结构、属性与规则，偏好满足这些需求的紧凑表示；世界在规则下演化，可选用户动作进行操控，文本请求可编辑属性或扩展能力且保留未受影响状态；物体纹理记忆仅对白盒定义的对象保留可复用外观并随对象变化自适应；程序推进世界状态，生成式渲染器将当前结构与记忆外观结合。

Result: 以同一对象组织构建、干预与记忆，使后续观测以最新状态和兼容外观为条件，从而在统一框架内实现可编辑、可操控、带视觉记忆的世界模型（摘要未给出定量指标）。

Conclusion: Mallea 表明围绕共享对象组织代码世界模型的构建、干预与记忆是可行的协调方案，为文本驱动的可执行世界建模提供了一种一体化设计。

Abstract: Code world models represent world dynamics through explicit state and executable rules. We investigate how to coordinate object representations, behavioral revisions, and visual memory within this formulation. We present Mallea, a system for building, steering, and memorizing worlds from text. A coding agent infers object interaction requirements from the prompt and jointly plans object structure, properties, and rules, favoring compact representations that support those requirements. The world evolves under the planned rules; optional user actions steer it, while text requests can edit properties or extend capabilities with unaffected state preserved. Object texture memory retains reusable appearance only for whitebox-defined objects and adapts it as those objects change. The program advances world state, while a generative renderer combines current structure with remembered appearance. By organizing construction, intervention, and memory around the same objects, Mallea conditions subsequent observations on the latest state and compatible appearance. Project page: https://lxycopper.github.io/Mallea.

</details>


### [56] [The Density of Thought: Predicting and Steering Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21286)
*Tanush Kalhan,Anupam Chattopadhyay,Sriram Ranga,Amritendu Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究CoT各推理步骤信息密度是否预测答案正确性及能否作为可控的推理时目标，发现密度行为更多由训练范式决定，直接密度操控有害而预生成引导与熵门控密度路由可提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究跨推理步骤的信息密度差异能否预测答案正确性，并作为推理阶段的可控目标，以理解自然密度模式在推理中的功能作用。

Method: 对冻结预训练模型以CoT解题，用两种独立指标度量每步密度：LP方差（各步平均per-token负对数似然方差，衡量信息分布不均程度）与Caveman压缩比；并设计合成实验检验复杂度偏好，进而测试密度与正确性的相关性并施加密度操控。覆盖6种模型规模、2种训练范式、60道GSM8K题目。提出Entropy-Gated Density Routing（EGDR），用token级Shannon熵自监督地门控成比例的KV-cache调整。

Result: 四项主要发现：(1) 较低LP方差在SFT模型中预测正确，但随规模增大关系减弱、并在RL蒸馏模型中反转，训练范式比模型规模更具影响；(2) 合成实验显示内在复杂度偏好，均匀数据训练的模型学到的偏好复杂度分布相比偏好简单分布准确率高2.0–2.6倍；(3) 推理时直接密度操控使准确率从60.0%降至8.3%；(4) 预生成引导（提示注入+对比KV-cache引导）提升13.3个百分点，两指标一致；EGDR无需外部引导提升10.0个百分点。

Conclusion: CoT步骤信息密度与正确性相关且受训练范式主导；自然密度模式对推理具有功能必要性，粗暴操控有害，而预生成引导与自监督EGDR是可有效提升准确率的可控干预手段。

Abstract: We investigate whether variation in information density across chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning steps predicts answer correctness and can serve as a controllable inference-time target. We prompt frozen pretrained models to solve problems via CoT and measure the density of each reasoning step with two independent metrics: LP variance (how unevenly information is distributed across steps, computed as the variance in mean per-token negative log-likelihood) and the Caveman compression ratio. We then test whether density correlates with, and can be steered to improve, answer correctness. Experiments across six model scales, two training paradigms, and 60 GSM8K problems yield four main findings. First, lower LP variance predicts correctness in supervised fine-tuned (SFT) models, but this relationship weakens with scale and reverses in reinforcement-learning (RL) distilled models. Training paradigm therefore appears more influential than model size in determining density behaviour. Second, synthetic experiments reveal an intrinsic complexity preference: models trained on uniformly distributed data learn complexity-favouring distributions 2.0 to 2.6 times more accurately than simplicity-favouring ones. Third, direct inference-time density steering reduces accuracy from 60.0% to 8.3%, indicating that natural density patterns are functionally important to reasoning. Fourth, pre-generation nudging combining hint injection with contrastive KV-cache steering improves accuracy by 13.3 percentage points, consistent across both metrics. Finally, we introduce Entropy-Gated Density Routing (EGDR), a self-supervised method using token-level Shannon entropy to gate proportional KVcache adjustments during generation, improving accuracy by 10.0 percentage points without any external guidance.

</details>


### [57] [Bias Control and Injection for Multi-Agent Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21290)
*Sungjae Lee,Soheil Abbasloo,Jabin Koo,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无需验证数据与模型强弱先验的测试时聚合框架：通过"偏差控制"（每个不同答案仅呈现一条风格归一化推理轨迹）改善初始答案选择，通过"偏差注入"（把聚合代理首选答案置于少数派）诱发决策不稳定性作为可靠性信号，从而解决多智能体LLM推理中的分歧问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM推理中，代理出现分歧时"该信任谁的决策"缺乏可靠依据；代理决策易受多数压力、回复风格偏好等非语义偏差干扰，掩盖了底层推理质量，且现有聚合方法常依赖验证数据或模型相对强弱的先验知识。

Method: 测试时聚合框架，包含两个机制：(1) 偏差控制——对每个不同答案仅呈现一条风格归一化的推理轨迹，抑制风格与多数压力偏差以改进初始答案选择；(2) 偏差注入——将每个聚合代理初始选中的答案置于少数派位置，诱发决策不稳定性，并将该不稳定性作为可靠性信号。框架既不需要验证数据，也不需要预先知道各模型的相对强弱。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准、四种异构LLM组合上，相较非oracle聚合方法取得更高的平均准确率与更好的token效率；应用于多智能体辩论与Mixture-of-Agents时，在不修改其交互或合成过程的前提下提升了最终答案准确率。

Conclusion: 偏差既可以被抑制也可以被利用：偏差控制提升初始选择，偏差注入把决策不稳定性转化为可靠性信号；二者结合构成一种通用、无需oracle的测试时聚合方案，可即插即用地增强现有多智能体推理流程。

Abstract: Multi-agent reasoning with large language models (LLMs) raises a central question for collective decision-making: when agents disagree, whose decision should be trusted? Resolving such disagreements is difficult because agents' decisions are susceptible to non-semantic biases such as majority pressure and response-style preferences, which can obscure the quality of the underlying reasoning. Our analysis shows how bias can be both mitigated and exploited: bias control improves initial answer selection, while bias injection elicits decision instability that serves as a reliability signal. Building on these findings, we introduce a test-time aggregation framework that requires neither validation data nor prior knowledge of relative model strength. The framework implements bias control by presenting one style-normalized reasoning trace per distinct answer, and bias injection by placing each aggregating agent's initially selected answer in the minority. Overall, our framework achieves higher average accuracy and better token efficiency than competing non-oracle aggregation methods across three mathematical reasoning benchmarks and four heterogeneous LLM combinations. It also improves final-answer accuracy when applied to multi-agent debate and Mixture-of-Agents, without modifying their interaction or synthesis processes.

</details>


### [58] [Money-Mule Detection as a Relational Learning Problem: A Study on the RBIH NFPC Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21314)
*Owais Ahmad,Anirudh Deodhar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出直接将账户而非交易作为评分对象的检测器：把银行关系型模式视为异构图（行为节点、外键为边），用逐列编码器、相对时间编码与HeteroGraphSAGE聚合多跳邻域，并将邻域限制在账户打标截止时点之前的行，避免时序泄漏。在RBIH五年数据集（4亿交易、94,063个有标签账户，2.6%为确认mule）上，以83%精确率和0.5%误报率召回九成留出mule，比最强树模型多找出115个mule。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洗钱mule账户的识别信号不在单笔转账中，而在于其共享的交易对手、地址及时间维度上的活动形态；生产系统多为规则驱动、看不到这些关系，而多数研究以交易为分类单位、事后聚合才得到账户风险。作者希望直接对账户打分，并解决"模型看到打标之后才产生的证据"这一时序泄漏问题。

Method: 将银行关系型schema直接读作异构图（行=节点，外键=边）；每个账户以其多跳邻域为输入，采用逐列编码器（per-column encoders）、相对时间编码和HeteroGraphSAGE消息传递；邻域仅保留早于该账户pre-flag cutoff的行。与RelGNN、XGBoost、LightGBM、CatBoost在相同标签、划分与cutoff下对比；因图即schema，可直接部署于现有数据库，无需自建特征图。

Result: 在400万至4亿交易的RBIH零售银行数据（94,063个有标签账户，2.6%确认mule）上，检测器在0.5%误报率下以83%精确率召回约90%的留出mule，相同证据下比最强树集成多识别115个mule。在调优阈值下差距主要体现在召回：XGBoost与LightGBM在队列前100个告警中领先，但只有图模型能在队列深处（长尾）维持精确率。

Conclusion: 账户级图建模在长尾告警队列上优于树模型，且因图结构即数据库schema、每个分数按账户标注日期，方案可在银行现有数据库上直接落地，无需专门的特征图构建流程。

Abstract: Money-mule networks move criminal funds through many small transfers, each of which looks ordinary on its own. What marks an account as a mule lies outside any single transfer: in the counterparties and addresses its network shares, and in the shape of its activity over time. Production systems remain largely rule-based and see neither, while most research classifies transactions rather than accounts, recovering account-level risk only by aggregating afterwards. We instead score the account directly, reading the bank's relational schema as a heterogeneous graph in which rows are nodes and foreign keys are edges. Each account is scored from a multi-hop neighbourhood using per-column encoders, a relative-time encoding and HeteroGraphSAGE message passing, with that neighbourhood restricted to rows predating the account's own pre-flag cutoff so that no model sees evidence recorded only after the account was flagged. On a five-year Reserve Bank Innovation Hub (RBIH) retail-banking dataset of 400 million transactions and 94{,}063 labelled accounts, 2.6\% of them confirmed mules, we compare against RelGNN, XGBoost, LightGBM and CatBoost trained on identical labels, splits and cutoffs. Our detector recovers nine in ten held-out mules at 83\% precision and a 0.5\% false-alarm rate, finding 115 more mules than the strongest tree ensemble given the same evidence. At the tuned thresholds the gap is mostly in recall: XGBoost and LightGBM lead across the first hundred alerts, but only the graph models hold their precision deep into the queue. Since the graph is the schema itself and every score is dated per account, the approach deploys on a bank's existing database without a custom feature graph build.

</details>


### [59] [Constrained Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning in Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21319)
*Raz Shmueli,Shahaf S. Shperberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CA-MAMBA——一种带代价（cost）预测的多智能体世界模型，把大部分约束策略更新迁移到"想象"中进行，从而在多智能体协作任务中同时提升交互效率与奖励表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习中约束策略更新昂贵、样本效率低；作者希望利用世界模型预测代价（cost），从而减少真实环境交互，并检验模型代价估计精度是否为性能瓶颈。

Method: 构建多智能体世界模型并加入代价预测，使得约束策略更新主要在世界模型生成的想象轨迹中完成；在 SMAC 与 MAMuJoCo 两个基准上，与强 model-free 基线在相同预算下比较交互量与回报，并设计单任务受控实验，将模型代价优势按"平价"（parity）定价以做消融对照。

Result: 在 SMAC 中位数配置上，最强 model-free 基线所需交互量是 CA-MAMBA 的 10–20 倍；在等预算下 CA-MAMBA 的 MAMuJoCo 回报为基线的 2.8–12 倍。受控实验中，将模型代价优势按平价定价，使 cost-blind 学习者的代价下降超过一半，并满足一个严格连续控制限制，而学习得到的乘子未能达标。

Conclusion: 基于世界模型代价预测的想象式约束更新显著提升样本效率与回报；受控实验表明模型的代价估计并非性能瓶颈，瓶颈更可能在于如何利用代价信息进行策略更新。

Abstract: Predicting cost in a multi-agent world model moves most constrained policy updates into imagination: the stronger model-free baseline needs $10$ to $20$ times CA-MAMBA's interactions on the median SMAC configuration, and CA-MAMBA earns $2.8$ to $12$ times the baselines' MAMuJoCo reward at equal budget. In controlled tests on one task per benchmark, pricing the model's cost advantages at parity more than halves a cost-blind learner's cost and meets a tight continuous-control limit the learned multiplier misses, suggesting the model's cost estimates are not the bottleneck.

</details>


### [60] [DocPilot: Interactive Agent Orchestration and Training for Long Visual Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21327)
*Zhuowen Liang,Xiaotian Lin,Yuyu Luo,Haixun Wang,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 DocPilot，一个支持表达性编排的智能体文档框架，通过页面级导航与细粒度证据定位，结合视觉检查、代码数值推理与跨模态推理，并用课程式智能体训练将能力迁移到小语言模型（4B 版本媲美更大模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态长文档问答需要跨页、跨模态检索稀疏证据并推理；先进 VLM 单次推理易遗漏稀疏证据、跨模态推理不足；现有文档智能体依赖预定义流程或粗粒度动作，灵活性与推理粒度受限。

Method: DocPilot 框架：页面级导航 + 细粒度证据定位的证据检索，集成局部视觉检查、基于代码的数值推理和跨模态推理；并提出课程式智能体训练，从单能力学习逐步过渡到自适应编排，监督信号来自数据接地的推理轨迹与可执行交互轨迹。

Result: 在 MMLongBench-Doc、DocBench、FinRAGBench-V、MADQA 上，配合前沿 VLM 主干取得显著提升；DocPilot-4B 与更大规模模型相比具竞争力，体现有效性与泛化性。

Conclusion: 表达性编排 + 细粒度交互 + 课程式训练可有效提升多模态长文档推理，并使小模型可学习复杂文档求解能力，具备跨场景泛化性。

Abstract: Multimodal long-document question answering requires models to seek sparse evidence across pages and modalities, understand heterogeneous document content, and reason over the collected evidence. Even advanced vision-language models (VLMs) can overlook sparse evidence and struggle with cross-modal reasoning in a single pass. Existing document agents further introduce multi-step interaction, but some still rely on predefined workflows or coarse-grained actions, limiting the flexibility and granularity of document reasoning. We introduce DocPilot, an agentic document framework that supports expressive orchestration and makes complex document solving learnable by small language models (SLMs). DocPilot orchestrates evidence seeking through page-level navigation and fine-grained evidence localization, and integrates local visual inspection, code-based numerical reasoning, and cross-modal reasoning for multimodal understanding. To transfer these capabilities to small models, we propose curriculum-based agentic training that progresses from single-ability learning to adaptive orchestration through supervision from data-grounded reasoning traces and executable interaction trajectories. Across MMLongBench-Doc, DocBench, FinRAGBench-V, and MADQA, DocPilot achieves strong gains with frontier VLM backbones, while DocPilot-4B delivers competitive performance against substantially larger models, demonstrating strong effectiveness and generalization across diverse long-document reasoning settings. The code is available at https://github.com/HKUSTDial/DocPilot.

</details>


### [61] [Quantum Versus Classical Machine Learning for Neuropsychiatric Disorders: A Feature-Matched Multi-Cohort Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21344)
*Utsav Poudel,Anuj Nepal,Vyas Viswanath,Subramaniyaswamy Vairavasundaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在4个公开队列（4,308名被试）上，用同一套相关特征对6类量子模型与匹配的经典模型进行特征对齐比较；多数量子模型与经典模型持平，但没有一种量子模型能持续超越经典模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习模型已被用于功能连接数据的分类，但少有研究在留站点验证（leave-site-out）下评估，或与特征匹配的经典模型进行对照，缺乏公平、可复现的基准证据。

Method: 使用相同的相关性衍生功能连接特征，将6类量子模型家族与对应经典模型逐一匹配，共构成284组匹配比较。ABIDE I、ADHD-200、REST-meta-MDD采用留站点验证，UCLA-CNP采用分层10折交叉验证。分析包含量子核方法、混合量子卷积网络、可训练量子核，并进一步考察基于保真度的图构建，以及通过有效维度和特征谱解释量子-经典持平现象。

Result: 量子核与匹配经典核在4个队列上统计不可区分，平均准确率差异在95%置信度下小于1.4个百分点。ABIDE I上混合量子卷积网络准确率与经典对应模型相近（0.645 vs 0.641），可训练量子核的AUC比经典对应模型高0.064；量子卷积网络在该队列AUC更高，且在全部4个队列上训练-测试差距更小，但准确率无提升。

Conclusion: 多数量子模型与经典模型表现相当，无一能持续占优；本研究是首批在留站点验证下评估ABIDE I的量子研究之一，提供了284组匹配比较的特征对齐基准，并从有效维度与特征谱角度解释了量子-经典持平现象。

Abstract: Resting-state functional connectivity is widely used in machine learning-based classification of neuropsychiatric disorders. Quantum machine learning models have been proposed for functional connectivity data, but few studies have evaluated them under leave-site-out validation or against matched classical models. We compared six quantum model families with classical ones using identical correlation-derived features across four public cohorts (4,308 subjects). Leave-site-out validation was used for the Autism Brain Imaging Data Exchange (ABIDE I), ADHD-200 (attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder), and REST-meta-MDD (major depressive disorder) cohorts, while the UCLA Consortium for Neuropsychiatric Phenomics (UCLA-CNP) was evaluated using stratified 10-fold cross-validation. The quantum kernel was statistically indistinguishable from a matched classical kernel across all four cohorts, with the average accuracy difference within 1.4 percentage points at 95% confidence. On ABIDE I, a hybrid quantum convolutional network achieved similar accuracy to its classical counterpart (0.645 vs. 0.641), while a trainable quantum kernel exceeded its classical counterpart by 0.064 in area under the curve (AUC). Quantum convolutional networks also achieved higher AUC than their matched classical networks on this cohort and showed smaller train-test gaps on all four cohorts, without an accuracy gain. Overall, most quantum models matched their classical counterparts, but none consistently outperformed them. This study, among the first quantum evaluations of ABIDE I under leave-site-out validation, provides a feature-matched benchmark of quantum and classical models comprising 284 matched comparisons. We further analyse fidelity-based graph construction and account for the observed quantum-classical parity through effective-dimension and eigenspectrum analyses.

</details>


### [62] [ARGUS: An Agenda-Driven Framework for Reliable User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21346)
*Hang Zheng,Nan Jiang,Zichen Zhu,Zixu Yang,Zhiyang Tang,Shuai Fan,Lu Chen,Xiaobao Wu,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出神经符号框架ARGUS，用动态议程栈以符号操作维护用户目标状态，并通过"评估器引导SFT+在线RL"蒸馏为小模型，同时引入CBS指标评估行为保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的用户模拟器存在可靠性问题：目标遗漏（goal omission）与策略不遵循（policy non-compliance），影响交互环境中的智能体评估与训练。

Method: ARGUS（Agenda-dRiven Goal-oriented User Simulator）：将状态跟踪与自然语言生成严格解耦，用由符号操作维护的动态议程栈（agenda stack）表征用户目标状态，实现严格的目标推进与约束对齐交互；部署上通过统一流程蒸馏为紧凑模型——先评估器引导的SFT，再进行带稠密步级奖励的在线RL；并提出基于评分量表（rubric）的复合行为分CBS作为行为保真度指标。

Result: 在τ³-Bench与MultiWOZ上的实验表明，ARGUS架构显著优于基线，且所提训练流程有效。

Conclusion: 神经符号解耦设计可提升LLM用户模拟器的目标推进与约束遵循可靠性，配合SFT+RL蒸馏流程可实现低成本部署；代码与数据将开源。

Abstract: High-fidelity user simulators are essential for evaluating and training agents in interactive environments. However, current LLM-based simulators struggle with reliability issues, such as goal omission and policy non-compliance. We propose ARGUS (Agenda-dRiven Goal-oriented User Simulator), a neuro-symbolic framework that maintains the user's goal state using a dynamic agenda stack manipulated via symbolic operations. By rigorously decoupling state tracking from natural language generation, ARGUS facilitates strict goal progression and constraint-aligned interactions. For cost-efficient deployment, we distill ARGUS into compact models using a unified pipeline: evaluator-guided SFT followed by online RL with dense step-level rewards. To rigorously evaluate behavioral fidelity, we introduce a rubric-based metric Composite Behavioral Score (CBS). Evaluations on $τ^3$-Bench and MultiWOZ demonstrate the significant superiority of the ARGUS architecture and the efficacy of our training pipeline. Code and data will be released soon.

</details>


### [63] [FedSkillGit: Federated Skill Evolution via Version Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21348)
*Boxun Lan,Jiamei Li,Weijian Li,Chengyu Yang,Zhuang Qi,Chengcheng Wan,Xiaohong Chen,Ming Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出联邦技能演化框架 FedSkillGit，用"提交式补丁（commits）"替代整篇技能文档的聚合，各客户端提交有界补丁与本地经验摘要，经两阶段隐私过滤后由服务器去重、合并并向客户端验证打分，性能提升最多 22% 且接近集中式演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体可从执行经验中演化可复用的自然语言技能，但真实场景中经验分散在无法共享原始数据的多方，需要联邦式技能演化。最直观的"每客户端演化整篇技能文档—上传—服务器聚合"方案存在三处失配：一是演化后的文档仍可能残留由私有轨迹蒸馏出的客户端特定内容（隐私泄露）；二是反复传输几乎未变的文档，通信开销浪费在群体已知的知识上；三是单个客户端的贡献被淹没在整篇文档中，服务器无法进行有意义的归因。

Method: FedSkillGit 以补丁级提交取代全文档更新：①每个参与客户端针对具名规则提交有界数量的补丁，并附上概括本轮所学的本地经验，二者在离开客户端前通过两阶段隐私过滤；②服务器对提交的补丁与所有参与者本地经验分别去重，更新仅优化器可读的全局经验文档，并把存活的补丁合并为候选技能；③候选技能下发至客户端，各客户端在自身验证集上打分并回传结果，仅在分数优于当前版本时才正式发布。

Result: 在三个数据集与三个语言模型族上的实验表明，FedSkillGit 相较当前最优基线最高提升 22%，并在不共享原始客户端数据的前提下接近集中式（centralized）技能演化的效果。

Conclusion: 以补丁/提交为粒度的联邦技能演化在隐私、通信与贡献归因上优于整文档聚合方案，能够在联邦约束下获得接近集中式演化的技能质量，为分布式经验驱动的智能体技能演化提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Large language model agents can improve their capabilities by evolving reusable natural-language skills from execution experience. In real-world deployments, this experience is distributed across data owners who cannot pool their raw data, which motivates federated skill evolution. The natural first design lets each client evolve the whole skill document and upload the result for server-side aggregation. That design is poorly matched to skill evolution for three reasons. An evolved document can still carry client-specific content distilled from private traces, repeated transmission of a nearly unchanged document spends communication on knowledge that the population already holds and the contribution of a single client is buried in a document that the server cannot meaningfully attribute. We propose FedSkillGit, a federated skill evolution framework that replaces full-document updates with commits. Each participating client submits a bounded set of patches over named rules together with its local experience, which summarizes what the round taught it, and the pair passes a two-stage privacy filter before it leaves the client. The server deduplicates the submitted patches and the local experience of all participants, updates a global experience document that only the optimizers read, and merges the surviving patches into a candidate skill. The candidate is distributed to the clients, which score it on their own validation sets and report the results, and it is published only when the reported scores improve on the current version. Experiments on three datasets and three language model families show that FedSkillGit outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 22% and approaches centralized evolution without sharing raw client data.

</details>


### [64] [Hierarchical Graph Convolutional Network Approach for Detecting Low-Quality Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21350)
*Jaeyoung Lee,Joonwon Jang,Misuk Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出分层图卷积网络(HGCN)，自动检测文档内部不一致及标题-正文不符，并构建Inconsistency数据集；准确率91.20%，在公开数据集上达92.00%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨媒体海量信息中存在大量低质量文档，表现为内部缺乏一致性或标题与内容完全无关，导致读者疲劳并损害媒体可信度，因此亟需基于自然语言处理的一致性自动检测研究。

Method: 构建分层图卷积网络(HGCN)，同时建模文档内部不一致与标题-正文不一致；利用已发表新闻数据及其元数据构建Inconsistency数据集用于模型训练。

Result: 在自建Inconsistency数据集上准确率91.20%，优于对比模型；在公开的incongruent相关数据集上达到92.00%，验证泛化性；消融实验证实元数据使用对性能提升有显著贡献。

Conclusion: HGCN可有效检测文档一致性问题并具通用性，有望在真实场景中用于低质量文档的检测与过滤。

Abstract: Consistency within a document is a crucial feature indicative of its quality. Recently, within the vast amount of information produced across various media, there exists a significant number of low-quality documents that either lack internal consistency or contain content utterly unrelated to their headlines. Such low-quality documents induce fatigue in readers and undermine the credibility of the media source that provided them. Consequently, research to automatically detect these low-quality documents based on natural language processing is imperative. In this study, we introduce a hierarchical graph convolutional network (HGCN) that can detect internal inconsistencies within a document and incongruences between the title and body. Moreover, we constructed the Inconsistency Dataset, leveraging published news data and its meta-data, to train our model to detect document inconsistencies. Experimental results demonstrated that the HGCN achieved superior performance with an accuracy of 91.20% on our constructedInconsistency Dataset, outperforming other comparative models. Additionally, on the publicly available incongruent-related dataset, the proposed methodology demonstrated a performance of 92.00%, validating its general applicability. Finally, an ablation study further confirmed the significant impact of meta-data utilization on performance enhancement. We anticipate that our model can be universally applied to detect and filter low-quality documents in the real world.

</details>


### [65] [Learning to Reason with Selective Internal Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21392)
*Xun Lu,Shumeng Yang,Xinrui Wang,Songjun Tu,Linjing Li,Daniel Dajun Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SIF强化学习方法，通过比较式奖励构建与选择性策略更新，缓解自置信度与正确性偏离问题，在12个数学与科学推理基准上超过Intuitor和Vigor。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无需真实答案或外部验证器的内部反馈强化学习可提升语言模型，但训练中置信度与正确性会发散，持续优化平均自置信度会推高置信度却损害推理准确率。

Method: SIF结合比较式奖励构建与选择性策略更新：在多个窗口与同伴间比较排序后的token级自置信度曲线，仅当两个层级的投票均提供足够支持时赋予正奖励；并采用样本级与组级过滤决定哪些回答参与策略优化。

Result: 在3个骨干模型、12个数学与科学推理基准上，SIF最终检查点的平均准确率高于Intuitor与Vigor的最佳验证检查点；在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B上较Intuitor提升4.71个百分点。分析显示持续优化过程中准确率保持更好，测试时训练实验表明每提示仅需四次rollout即可获得持续提升。

Conclusion: 比较式奖励与选择性更新可有效抑制自置信度与正确性的偏离，实现更稳健的无监督内部反馈强化学习，并具备低采样成本下的持续改进能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from internal feedback can improve language models without ground-truth answers or external verifiers. However, confidence and correctness can diverge during training, and continued optimization of average self-certainty can increase confidence while degrading reasoning accuracy. We introduce SIF, a reinforcement learning method that combines comparative reward construction with selective policy updates. SIF compares ranked token-level self-certainty profiles across multiple windows and peers, assigning positive rewards only when both levels of voting provide sufficient support. Sample and group level filtering determines which responses contribute to policy optimization. Experiments on three backbones across 12 mathematical and scientific reasoning benchmarks show that SIF's final checkpoints achieve higher average accuracy than the best-validation checkpoints of Intuitor and Vigor, with a gain of 4.71 percentage points over Intuitor on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B. Further analyses show better retention of accuracy during continued optimization, while test-time training experiments demonstrate sustained improvements with as few as four rollouts per prompt.

</details>


### [66] [Self-Guided LLM Reasoning for Error Diagnosis in Vehicle Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21393)
*Ni Zhang,Jianan Zhou,Yue-Jiao Gong,Zhiguang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型（LLM）对车辆路径问题（VRP）实例中错误的检测与纠正能力，并提出自引导推理框架以提升其错误诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的VRP求解方法普遍假设输入实例无错误，忽视了真实数据中常见的故障；而LLM在处理复杂实例或显著增加推理负担的错误时存在明显局限。

Method: 系统性评估LLM检测与纠正VRP实例错误的能力，并在此基础上提出自引导推理框架：由LLM自主生成代码，产出结构化洞察以辅助错误诊断。

Result: 在多种问题设置与多种LLM上的实验表明，该框架显著提升错误检测性能，达到近乎完美的准确率。

Conclusion: 将代码生成作为结构化推理辅助手段可有效缓解LLM在复杂VRP实例错误诊断中的推理负担，为其在含噪真实数据场景下的应用提供可行路径。

Abstract: Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are fundamental to many real-world applications. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have spurred progress in solving these NP-hard problems. However, most existing approaches assume that input instances are error-free, neglecting the presence of faults commonly encountered in real-world data. In this work, we systematically investigate the capability of LLMs to detect (and correct) errors in VRP instances, and identify key limitations, particularly in handling complex instances or errors that substantially increase the LLM's reasoning burden. To overcome these challenges, we propose a self-guided reasoning framework in which LLMs autonomously generate code that provides structured insights to aid in error diagnosis. Experiments across diverse problem settings and LLMs demonstrate that our framework significantly improves error detection performance, achieving near-perfect accuracy.

</details>


### [67] [Watts If? A Benchmark for Counterfactual Reasoning About the AI Data Center Buildout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21397)
*Pankaj Chouhan,Rajarshi Das,Aditya Dubey,Manminder Tomar,Sreekar Reddy,Samta Sharma,Satyapragnya Kar,PR Panigrahi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DCBench-440基准，用生成式STRIPS题与多选题检验LLM能否追踪AI数据中心早期承诺所排除的后续选项；模型在"列出闸口全部可行动作"这一技能上全面失败（精确匹配≤4.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI数据中心的供电、冷却、选址等少数离散选择在投运前多年即锁定资本、水耗与碳路径，LLM已被用于筛选此类承诺，但缺乏测试其是否真正理解每个承诺"排除了什么"的基准。

Method: 构建DCBench-440，双臂分别报告：(1) 200道基于STRIPS世界模型的生成式题目，覆盖五类美国投资原型，金标准经搜索验证且任何题型上未发现超过12%的捷径；(2) 240道多选题，以引用的场址记录、排放方程和给定情景为依据。

Result: 开源权重模型准确率：生成式12.5–71.5%，多选题58.3–92.5%。共同短板为"列出决策闸口所有可行动作"，无一模型精确匹配超过4.0%；最强模型能列出72–85%会改变项目状态的行动，却遗漏多数不改变状态的允许动作；即便豁免后者，最佳精确匹配也仅24%。

Conclusion: 现有LLM对承诺所引出的完整动作空间缺乏系统把握，尤其对"状态保持型"允许动作严重欠枚举，难以可靠支撑数据中心早期承诺的筛选。

Abstract: An AI data center is committed years before it draws a watt: a few discrete choices (grid or on-site gas, evaporative or dry cooling, which county to file in) fix its capital, water draw, and carbon path, and LLMs are now asked to help screen them with no test of whether they track what each commitment rules out. We introduce DCBench-440, with two arms reported separately: 200 generative questions over STRIPS worlds for five US deal archetypes, with search-verified gold answers and no tested shortcut above 12% on any question type, and 240 multiple-choice questions grounded in quoted site records, emission equations, and stated scenarios. Across open-weight deployments, accuracy spans 12.5-71.5% (generative) and 58.3-92.5% (multiple choice). One skill fails everywhere: asked to list every action available at a decision gate, no model exceeds 4.0% exact match. The strongest list 72-85% of the actions that would change the project's state but omit most permitted actions that change nothing; with those excused, the best exact match is still 24%. Code, data, and other materials are available upon request.

</details>


### [68] [MemCut: Every Secret Leaves a Trail in the Long-Term Memory of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21405)
*Cheng Zhang,Xuanning Cao,Zhicheng Zheng,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MemCut 基准，用于评测 LLM 智能体长期记忆系统的"用户请求式遗忘"能力，并指出候选覆盖不足是现有删除方法的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆研究多聚焦检索的准确与高效，但可靠的记忆系统还需在用户请求时遗忘敏感数据；缺少系统化评测手段。

Method: 构建含 456 条人工设计秘密的长程对话数据，并为每条秘密标注完整证据链（含未直接陈述秘密的消息）；提出原生恢复率与残留恢复率两项指标，建立三个参考删除基线，在五种记忆设计与四种 LLM 上评测，并进行子集扫描实验。

Result: 删除披露消息对原生恢复率的抑制显著强于残留恢复率；在 20 个检索候选中由 LLM 引导额外删除 10 轮对话后，残留恢复率仍达 39%–76%；子集扫描显示固定预算下扩大候选池比删除全部原始候选更能降低残留恢复率。

Conclusion: 现有检索器与写入模型对遗忘的支持有限，候选覆盖是参考基线的关键瓶颈，记忆删除需从检索层面系统性改进。

Abstract: Long-term memory has become a crucial component of large language model (LLM) agents, with most research focusing on retrieving relevant information accurately and efficiently. However, a dependable memory system must also forget sensitive user data upon request. To evaluate this capability, we introduce MemCut, a systematic benchmark for user-requested forgetting. Its 456 hand-designed secrets in long-horizon conversations have fully annotated evidence trails to test recovery after deletion, including from messages that never state the secrets directly. We propose two recovery metrics. Native recovery measures reader success through the system's retrieval, whereas residual recovery measures the same reader's success when directly given surviving objects traced to annotated disclosures or evidence. We establish three reference deletion baselines to test how far existing retrievers and write models support forgetting across five memory designs and four LLMs. Disclosure removal reduces native recovery far more than residual recovery. LLM-guided deletion of ten additional turns from twenty retrieval candidates leaves 39-76% residual recovery. Subset sweeps (144 facts, three systems, two models) show that widening the pool at a fixed budget reduces residual recovery more than deleting all original candidates. These sweeps identify candidate coverage as a bottleneck in the reference baselines.

</details>


### [69] [Jev-LiteMAS: Separating Decisions from Generation in Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21411)
*Kaixiang Wang,Zhaijiacheng Zhou,Jiong Lou,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Jev-LiteMAS 用轻量决策模型 Jev 承担路由与初审，配合生成式执行与反馈，在四个基准、两个骨干上相较 LAS 减少 12.7–54.6% 生成式 token，精度损失控制在 1.34 个百分点内，八个设置中有七个延迟下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体 LLM 系统（MAS）中专家执行、工作流规划与产物审查都依赖生成式模型，但路由与评审等协调操作本质上只需有界决策。三款代表性 MAS 框架的剖析显示，路由与审查占生成式 LLM token 的 31.7–43.5%，存在明显浪费。然而直接用低成本决策模型替换会带来两个接口缺口：生成式规划器还承担制定执行计划与交接指令的职责（规划缺口），审查器还需给出可操作的修复反馈（审查缺口），这些是有界决策无法提供的。

Method: 提出 Jev-LiteMAS 框架，将轻量决策模型 Jev 与生成式执行、反馈相结合。针对规划缺口：不预生成执行轨迹，Jev 依据任务描述与既有专家输出动态选择下一位专家，用累积上下文为后续执行提供依据。针对审查缺口：Jev 先依据任务标准对中间产物做筛查，通过者直接进入下一步，被标记者转交 LLM 复审，决定接受或给出纠正性反馈。

Result: 在四个基准与两个骨干模型上，Jev-LiteMAS 相较 LAS 减少 12.7–54.6% 的生成式 LLM token，精度保持在 1.34 个百分点以内，八个设置中有七个延迟降低。将路由建立在实质性专家输出之上，可避免高达 18.0 个百分点的精度损失。
