Method: 提出分数幂次量化FPoT：(i) 采用基-√2（半八度）分数PoT权重网格，配合均匀整数激活；(ii) 在校准阶段进行网格对齐精修，并将其吸收进静态逐通道缩放因子；(iii) 使用轻量单加法器√2近似，其误差通过近似网格量化在权重校准中吸收，使处理单元（PE）门数显著少于4-bit整数乘法器。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B上采用4-bit权重与激活量化，FPoT相对PoT基线的零样本常识推理性能提升8.72%（相对值），与均匀整数基线相比仅有0.4个百分点的可忽略退化；结论在覆盖7个模型、3种比特宽度的多种配置下成立。

Conclusion: FPoT在保持无乘法器移位-加法硬件效率的同时弥合了PoT与均匀整数量化间的精度差距，并可与现有先进LLM PTQ方法结合，具备框架无关的通用性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demand efficient inference, where the multiply-accumulate (MAC) operations in linear projections dominate compute and energy cost. Post-training quantization (PTQ) reduces this cost by mapping weights and activations to low-bit integers. Power-of-Two (PoT) weight quantization promises efficient multiplier-free LLM inference via shift-add logic, but suffers significant accuracy degradation at low bitwidths due to its coarse base-2 quantization grid. We introduce Fractional PoT (FPoT), a base-$\sqrt{2}$ (half-octave) fractional PoT grid that achieves practical 4-bit weight quantization while preserving a fully integer shift-add datapath. The method combines (i) the base-$\sqrt{2}$ weight grid with uniform integer activations, (ii) a calibration-time grid-alignment refinement absorbed into the static per-channel scale, and (iii) a lightweight 1-adder $\sqrt{2}$ approximation whose error is absorbed during weight calibration via approximate-grid quantization, yielding a processing element (PE) with significantly fewer gates than a 4-bit integer multiplier. On Llama-3-8B with 4-bit weight and activation quantization, FPoT improves zero-shot commonsense reasoning performance by 8.72% relative to the PoT baseline, while maintaining performance close to the uniform integer baseline, with only a negligible 0.4%p degradation. These results hold across various configurations spanning seven models and three bit-widths. Furthermore, FPoT can be integrated with state-of-the-art LLM PTQ methods, demonstrating framework-agnostic applicability.

</details>


### [204] [Behavioral Compositionality of Sparse Autoencoder Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21261)
*Haotian Ma,Ziye Mei,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 SAE Additivity Residuals (SAR)，量化在给定交互阶数之上未被解释的行为变异，并据此区分"SAE 表示本身的可组合性"与"下游计算诱导的可组合性"，发现 SAE 坐标一阶残差低但并非完全可加，成对交互项对忠实解释与干预仍必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE 用稀疏线性解码特征表示模型激活，但特征在下游的行为效应未必按加法组合；需要一种度量来量化超出指定交互阶数、无法由低阶近似解释的行为变异，并厘清可组合性究竟来源于 SAE 表示还是下游计算。

Method: 提出 SAR 指标：在独立特征子集采样下，总体 SAR 恰等于高于该阶数的交互能量的归一化值，并为最优低阶近似的 coalition 排序误差提供上界。通过将学习到的 SAE 坐标与同一被选子空间的"和保持随机旋转"进行对比来分离表示效应；在 18 个模型与行为设置上评估一阶、成对（及随机选择的参数匹配三阶项）模型的留出 coalition 排序表现，并进行组合式 steering 实验。

Result: 在所有 18 个模型与行为设置中，学习坐标的一阶残差均低于随机旋转坐标；但坐标并非完全可加：成对项在全部设置中提升留出 coalition 排序，并优于使用随机三阶项的参数匹配模型；组合 steering 实验中成对建模将 rank fidelity 从 0.5874 提升至 0.7790，排序误差从 0.1444 降至 0.0606。

Conclusion: SAE 坐标支持相对更具可组合性的行为描述（优于随机旋转基线），但忠实的联合解释与干预仍需超越单个特征效应的交互项；一阶特征效应不足以完全刻画下游行为。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) represent model activations with sparse, linearly decoded features, but the downstream effects of those features need not compose additively. We introduce SAE Additivity Residuals (SAR), which measure the behavioral variation left unexplained above a chosen interaction order. Under independent feature subset sampling, population SAR is exactly the normalized energy of interactions above that order and bounds coalition ordering error for the optimal low order approximation. To distinguish compositionality associated with the SAE representation from that induced by downstream computation, we compare learned SAE coordinates with sum preserving random rotations of the same selected subspace and find lower first order residuals for the learned coordinates across all 18 model and behavior settings. The coordinates are nevertheless not fully additive: pairwise terms improve held out coalition ordering in every setting and outperform parameter matched models using randomly selected third order terms. In a combined steering experiment, pairwise modeling raises rank fidelity from 0.5874 to 0.7790 and reduces ordering error from 0.1444 to 0.0606. Together, these results show that SAE coordinates support comparatively compositional behavioral descriptions while faithful joint explanations and interventions still require interactions beyond individual feature effects. Code is available at https://github.com/OpenSAE/SAE-Behavioral-Compositionality.

</details>


### [205] [Rethinking Learning from Label Proportions via Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21262)
*Tianhao Ma,Wei Wang,Yivan Zhang,Dong-Dong Wu,Takashi Ishida,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLP-MM方法，在多阶阶乘矩匹配框架下解决非随机装袋（non-random bag construction）场景的学习标签比例问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLP方法普遍假设实例-标签对i.i.d.采样并随机装入bag，该假设在现实中常不成立；实验显示此类方法在非随机装袋下表现次优。作者转向更实际的假设：bag i.i.d.采样，实例标签在给定实例条件下条件独立。

Method: 在该假设下证明bag内标签计数服从Poisson多项分布（Poisson multinomial distribution），据此以多阶阶乘矩（multi-order factorial moments）作为训练目标，使分类器预测的矩与观测bag标签比例的矩相匹配，从而更充分利用数据的潜在统计结构。

Result: 在多个基准数据集与多种bag构造策略下的实验表明该方法有效，同时保持较高计算效率。

Conclusion: LLP-MM以简洁有效的矩匹配思路突破了随机装袋假设的局限，适用于更现实的LLP设定，性能与效率兼备。

Abstract: Learning from label proportions (LLP) is a weakly supervised setting where training data are grouped into bags with only bag-level label proportions observed, and the goal is to learn a classifier that predicts labels for individual instances. Recently, a large body of methods has emerged; however, most of them rely on the assumption that instance-label pairs are sampled i.i.d. and randomly assembled into bags, an assumption often violated in real-world scenarios. Our experimental results indicate that these methods perform suboptimally under non-random settings. In this work, we adopt a more realistic assumption: bags are sampled i.i.d., and instance labels are conditionally independent given the instances. Under this setting, we show that the label counts follow a Poisson multinomial distribution. Motivated by this observation, we propose LLP via moment matching (LLP-MM), a simple yet effective approach which leverages multi-order factorial moments as training objectives, encouraging the classifier predictions to match these moments and thereby more fully exploit the underlying statistical structure of the data. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets under various bag construction strategies demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach while maintaining high computational efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/TianhaoMa5/LLP-MM.

</details>


### [206] [Regret Lower Bound for Online Learning of Quantum States with Logarithmic Loss, with Applications to Quantum State Tomography and Confidence Sequences for Matrix Means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21273)
*Yen-Huan Li,Wei-Fu Tseng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究对数损失下量子态的在线学习问题，建立了regret下界，证明Exponential Weights与Tseng等人(2025)算法在维度依赖上是多项式最优的；由此导出样本量意义下（至polylog因子）样本最优的量子态层析算法，以及矩阵值随机变量均值的置信序列构造算法，并在数值实验中与现有方法表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线量子态学习是online portfolio selection的量子推广，可用于量子态层析（由测量结果估计未知量子态）。现有算法（Exponential Weights、Tseng et al. 2025）的regret对维度的依赖是否最优尚不清楚，缺乏下界刻画。

Method: 首先构造regret下界分析，刻画regret率对维度的最优多项式依赖；将下界证明技术迁移，导出量子态层析算法；进一步开发矩阵值随机变量均值的置信序列构造算法；最后进行数值实验验证。

Result: 证明Exponential Weights与Tseng et al. (2025)算法在维度依赖上达到最优（多项式阶）；得到样本量至polylog因子最优的量子态层析算法；提出矩阵均值置信序列算法，数值实验显示其与现有方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 对数损失下量子态在线学习的regret对维度依赖已近紧；下界技术可转化为样本最优的量子态层析与矩阵均值置信序列工具，兼具理论与实用价值。

Abstract: We study the problem of online learning of quantum states with logarithmic loss, which is a quantum generalization of online portfolio selection and has applications to quantum state tomography, the task of estimating an unknown quantum state from measurement outcomes. We first establish a regret lower bound, showing that the regret rates of Exponential Weights and the algorithm of Tseng et al. (2025) have optimal polynomial dependence on the dimension. Our analysis leads to two further results. First, our lower-bound proof leads to a quantum state tomography algorithm that is sample optimal up to polylogarithmic factors in the sample size. Second, we develop an algorithm for constructing confidence sequences for the mean of a matrix-valued random variable. A numerical experiment shows that the latter algorithm is competitive with existing methods.

</details>


### [207] [TimeCap: A Scalable Open-Source Pipeline for Captioning Wearable Health Time-Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21274)
*Max Rosenblattl,Milana Gurbanova,Karl Aurel Deck,Narayan Schuetz,Thomas Kaar,Manuel Milling,Nicolas Zumarraga,Ning Wang,Kelly W. Zhang,Anders Johnson,Robert Jakob,Björn W. Schuller,Elgar Fleisch,Daniel Seung Kim,Allan Lawrie,Patrick Langer,Maxwell A. Xu,Euan Ashley,Paul Schmiedmayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出开源确定性时间序列描述生成流水线 TimeCap（无大模型参与、可复现、可扩展），将其应用于 MyHeart Counts 可穿戴数据集生成 MHCap（19 个 HealthKit 通道、908,944 人日、7,005,763 条描述），并基于此训练 OpenTSLM-Cap，在 8 项健康结局预测任务中 32 个逐用户二分类评测的 29 个上优于仅标签基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序语言模型（TSLM）的可扩展解释能力受限于缺乏"信号接地、可复现、跨模态与模式可扩展"的文本-时序配对数据：现有配对语料要么闭源，要么规模与模态覆盖有限；用大语言模型扩增标注虽可扩大规模，但输出无法精确重现，导致他人无法重新推导、扩展或审计语料。

Method: 发布 TimeCap：开源确定性描述生成流水线，含三个可复用抽取器层级与一个跨通道合成层，直接作用于原始信号、闭环中不引入语言模型；仅需编写通道定义即可扩展至新模式。将 TimeCap 应用于 OpenMHC（MyHeart Counts 可穿戴数据集）构建 MHCap 语料，并以其训练时序语言模型 OpenTSLM，在健康结局预测上进行基准评测。

Result: MHCap 覆盖 19 个 Apple HealthKit 通道、908,944 人日、7,005,763 条描述，为多模态可穿戴时序数据最大的公开描述层。描述增强训练（OpenTSLM-Cap）在 8 项任务、32 个逐用户二分类评测中的 29 个上优于仅标签基线。

Conclusion: TimeCap 被定位为面向未来数据集生成的可复现机制而非一次性发布；确定性、无 LLM 的设计保证了语料可重现、可审计、可扩展，且描述增强确实提升健康结局预测性能，验证了可扩展配对语料对 TSLM 的价值。

Abstract: Time-series data generated continuously across industrial, environmental, and especially health domains produce multimodal signals whose scale and heterogeneity make their processing and interpretation particularly challenging. Time-Series Language Models (TSLMs) promise scalable interpretation of these signals, yet remain bottlenecked by a fundamental lack of text-annotated time series pairs that are signal-grounded, reproducible, and extensible across modalities and schemas. Existing paired corpora are either closed or limited in scale and modality coverage. Annotation with large language models could scale them up, but its outputs cannot be regenerated exactly, which prevents others from re-deriving, extending, or auditing the resulting corpus. To address this, we release TimeCap, an open deterministic captioning pipeline with three reusable extractor tiers and a cross-channel synthesis layer that operates directly on the signal with no language model in the loop, extensible to new schemas by writing channel definitions alone. This positions TimeCap as a reproducible mechanism for future dataset generation rather than a one-off release. Applying TimeCap to OpenMHC, the MyHeart Counts wearable dataset, yields MHCap, the largest open caption layer for multimodal wearable time-series data. MHCap spans 19 Apple HealthKit channels and 7,005,763 captions over 908,944 person-days. To showcase the impact of the corpus, we train OpenTSLM, a time-series language model, on MHCap and benchmark on health-outcome prediction. Caption-augmented training (OpenTSLM-Cap) outperforms a label-only baseline on 29 of 32 per-user binary evaluations across 8 tasks.

</details>


### [208] [Can Capability Enhancement be Localized?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21294)
*Anh Tuan Nguyen,Anna Bair,Zico Kolter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"能力增强定位"(CEL)现象：给定模型与任务，存在稀疏的注意力头组合，其移除反而能提升任务性能；并给出训练动力学层面的理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作只关注"重要头"（移除后性能骤降）与"冗余头"（可安全移除），但忽视了互补问题：是否存在一小部分头，移除后能正向提升任务表现。作者希望从经验与理论两方面填补这一空白。

Method: 实证上，跨多个模型家族，采用两阶段流程（先筛选候选头，再评估其联合消融）搜索稀疏头组合，无需微调；理论上，构造简化的多头softmax注意力模型与两个含冲突特征的任务，分析从小随机初始化出发的梯度下降动力学。

Result: 实验中确实找到稀疏头组合，其联合消融可在不微调的情况下提升特定任务性能；理论上证明梯度下降会将冲突特征集中到特定注意力头，且即便联合目标收敛到全局最优，消融这些头仍会提升一个任务而损害另一个任务。

Conclusion: CEL现象普遍存在且可被训练动力学自然解释：冲突特征在训练中被隔离到特定头，形成"任务间干扰"的可移除来源，为头级模型编辑与任务特化提供依据。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models have shown strong capabilities across many tasks and domains. However, prior work shows that, for a given task, not all attention heads matter equally. Specifically, they suggest that many \textit{redundant} heads can be safely removed with little-to-no effect, while a small set of \textit{important} heads, when ablated, severely damages the model's performance on the given task. In this work, we investigate a complementary aspect, called \textit{Capability Enhancement Localization} (CEL): \textit{given a model and a task, does there exist a small set of heads whose removal improves the task performance?} We first investigate this question \textit{empirically} across models from different families. Using a simple two-stage routine that screens candidate heads and evaluates their joint ablations, we identify sparse head combinations whose removal improves task-specific performance without further fine-tuning. Motivated by these observations, we ask \textit{theoretically whether such a phenomenon can naturally arise from training dynamics, and under what conditions}. To this end, we construct a simplified multi-head softmax attention model and two tasks with conflicting features, and show that gradient descent from small random initialization provably concentrates the conflicting features into particular heads. We further show that ablating such heads improves performance on one task while harming the other, even though training converges to a global optimum of the joint objective. This establishes a possible training-induced theoretical explanation for CEL.

</details>


### [209] [Personalized Projected Ascent for Federated Stackelberg Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21295)
*M Yashwanth,Sharannya Ghosh,Saikiran Bulusu,Anirban Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦极小极大问题中最大化变量按客户端个性化保留的设定，分析投影上升的收敛性，并指出非凸-PL 情形下的噪声地板，进而提出 VR-SGDA 消除该地板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦极小极大问题（如分布鲁棒优化、对抗训练）中，各客户端的最大化变量 $\mathbf{y}_i$ 在含义与维度上均不同，标准方法对其跨客户端平均是不适定的，因此需要研究 $\mathbf{x}$ 共享聚合、$\mathbf{y}_i$ 个性化的联邦极小极大设定。

Method: 直接分析约束在紧凸集上的投影上升（projected ascent）个性化方案；针对非凸-PL 情形下随机 $\mathbf{y}_i$ 更新导致的步长不变的噪声地板，提出 VR-SGDA：引入方差缩减的上升步，每个客户端维护私有变量快照，在同一采样上评估当前与快照梯度，将常数方差替换为两时间尺度下的消失漂移。

Result: 在非凸-凹情形下，投影个性化方案达到标准次线性收敛率；在非凸-PL 情形下，该方案出现步长不变的噪声地板；VR-SGDA 消除噪声地板，在保持个性化与投影的同时取得次线性主项收敛率。在子群体鲁棒性与 NLP 对抗训练实验中验证了最差组鲁棒性收益及两时间尺度跟踪机制。

Conclusion: 个性化最大化变量的联邦极小极大设定是必要且可行的；投影上升在非凸-凹下有效，但在非凸-PL 下受随机性限制，需借助方差缩减与两时间尺度设计（VR-SGDA）才能消除噪声地板并保持个性化。

Abstract: Federated minimax problems, such as distributionally robust optimization and adversarial training, include an inner maximization variable built from each client's local data. These variables differ in meaning and dimension from one client to the next, so averaging them across clients, as standard federated minimax methods do, is ill-posed. We therefore study a federated minimax problem where the minimization variable $\mathbf{x}$ is shared and aggregated, while each client retains the maximization variable $\mathbf{y}_i$ (personalized). Because these applications constrain $\mathbf{y}_i$ to a compact convex set, we analyze projected ascent directly. We show that, in the nonconvex-concave regime, this projected personalized scheme attains the standard sublinear rate. In the nonconvex-PL regime, however, it hits a stepsize-invariant noise floor due to stochastic $\mathbf{y}_i$ updates. To mitigate this, we propose VR-SGDA, incorporating a variance-reduced ascent step where each client maintains a private variable snapshot. By evaluating current and snapshot gradients on the same sample, the constant variance is replaced by a vanishing drift under a two-timescale regime. VR-SGDA eliminates the noise floor, attaining sublinear leading rates while preserving personalization and projection. Experiments on subpopulation-robustness and NLP adversarial training confirm both the worst-group robustness benefits and the two-timescale tracking mechanism.

</details>


### [210] [PocketTSLM: Deploying an On-Device Soft-Prompt Time-Series Language Model for Privacy-Preserving Smartphone Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21308)
*Max Rosenblattl,Nikhil Krishnaswamy,Leon Nissen,Manuel Milling,Nicolas Zumarraga,Ning Wang,Thomas Kaar,Robert Jakob,Björn W. Schuller,Elgar Fleisch,Patrick Langer,Paul Schmiedmayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PocketTSLM 将 OpenTSLM SoftPrompt 时间序列语言模型完整部署到消费级智能手机，通过将编码后的时间序列窗口以嵌入方式注入而非文本序列化，在保持时序结构的同时大幅降低上下文与内存开销，实现端侧隐私保护推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手机与可穿戴设备持续采集生理时序数据，但现有移动系统多将其压缩为统计摘要或文本，破坏了时序结构；云端时序语言模型(SLM)虽规避设备算力限制，却需反复上传敏感生理数据，带来隐私、延迟与单次查询算力成本；而设备端替代方案(统计特征聚合、原始样本文本序列化)要么压缩时序结构，要么耗尽小模型上下文与内存预算。

Method: 提出 PocketTSLM，即 OpenTSLM SoftPrompt 的移动原生部署。核心是将编码后的时间序列窗口作为嵌入向量注入模型，而非将时间序列 token 化作文本。采用数值一致性协议，使用 Apple MLX 框架验证 Swift 移植版与 Python 参考实现之间贪心解码 token 序列完全一致。并在设备端开展上下文经济性基准测试，在同一骨干网络上对比 soft-prompt 路径与文本序列化基线。

Result: 数值一致性：Swift 移植版与 Python 参考实现的贪心解码 token 序列完全一致；答案与 PyTorch 参考也匹配，仅理由措辞可能不同。基准中 soft-prompt 使用的时序 token 数约为文本序列化的 1/12，并降低 prompt 预填充成本。实测处理 Sleep-EDF 的 30 秒 EEG 片段(睡眠分期)与 ECG-QA 的 1000 采样点(10 s)12 导联 ECG 片段；而文本序列化基线在较长时序窗口下超出手机上下文或内存预算。

Conclusion: PocketTSLM 证明了将时序语言模型以嵌入注入方式全端侧部署在消费级手机上可行，兼顾时序结构保真、低上下文/内存开销与隐私保护(推理全程无健康数据传输)。

Abstract: Consumer phones and wearables increasingly collect physiological time-series data, yet most mobile systems treat these streams as summaries or text rather than as temporal evidence. This representation is poorly matched to personal health systems that need to retrieve, route, or summarize sensor evidence while preserving temporal structure. Cloud-hosted time-series language models avoid smartphone limits, but they repeatedly move sensitive physiology off the device and add latency and per-query compute cost. Mobile model alternatives that aggregate time-series data into statistical features or serialize raw samples as text either compress temporal structure or exhaust the context and memory budgets of small on-device models. We present PocketTSLM, a mobile-native deployment of OpenTSLM SoftPrompt, the soft-prompt variant of the OpenTSLM time-series language model. PocketTSLM runs fully on a consumer smartphone by injecting encoded time-series windows as embeddings instead of tokenizing time series as text. A numerical parity protocol verifies identical greedy-decoded token sequences between the Swift port and a Python reference using Apple's MLX machine-learning framework. Answers also match the PyTorch reference, although rationale wording can differ. An on-device context-economics benchmark compares the soft-prompt path with a text-serialized baseline on the same backbone. In our benchmark format, soft-prompting uses approximately 12 times fewer time-series tokens and reduces prompt-prefill cost. PocketTSLM processes 30-second Sleep-EDF electroencephalography clips for sleep staging and a 1,000-sample (10 s) 12-lead electrocardiogram clip from ECG-QA. In comparison, text-serialized baselines exceed the phone's context or memory budget at longer time-series windows. All inference runs entirely on the device, with no health data transmitted during inference.

</details>


### [211] [Spectrum Is Not Enough: A Random-Matrix Account of Data-Free Preconditioning for Differentially Private Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21312)
*Shilin Zhang,Kangning Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将DP-KFC的探针验证判据转化为随机矩阵理论，给出"G因子与A的架构bulk可无数据重构、仅A的数据诱导尖峰不可重构"的二分法，并以BBP阈值刻画失效边界、以白化体积律解释特征基的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无数据预条件用合成探针替代私有数据构造KFAC的Kronecker因子，但缺乏理论刻画"探针究竟能重构什么"；DP-KFC仅以特征值衰减匹配作验证判据，其有效性边界与真正起作用的量未被阐明。

Method: 在A⊗G分解下做随机矩阵分析：证明G的数据不敏感性并给出误差能量界；用Baik-Ben Arous-Péché阈值定位bulk-only探针的失效边界与损伤量s²/(1-s²)；固定探针谱、仅扰动特征基以检验谱形匹配的必要性；推导闭式白化体积律；考察逐层调优的下游迁移及隐私噪声下的排序变化。

Result: G部分在四种模态与自然图像基准的完整训练轨迹上零违规；注入尖峰实验中尖峰超过(d/n)^{1/4}后不可见；谱形近乎匹配（对数比离散度0.02）但特征向量随机的探针仍使残差膨胀2–90倍；白化体积律预测谱内临界体积排序但未预测跨数据集幅度；逐层调优不迁移到精度，提升空间仅为"oracle减白化"的小差距；隐私噪声下源排序先保持后反转。

Conclusion: 可重构性由"架构/误差信号 vs 数据诱导结构"二分决定，谱形匹配既非充分条件也非操作对象；特征基（白化体积）才是关键控制量，为无数据预条件与隐私机制设计给出可检验的定量准则。

Abstract: Data-free preconditioning builds the Kronecker factors of KFAC from synthetic probes instead of private data. DP-KFC validates a probe by matching the eigenvalue decay of the private factors; we turn this criterion into a random-matrix account of what a probe can reconstruct. A single dichotomy organizes it: in the factorization $ A \otimes G $, the error-signal factor $ G $ and the architecture-determined bulk of the activation factor $ A $ are reconstructible without data, while only the data-induced spikes of $ A $ are not. We prove the $ G $ half is data-insensitive up to an explicit error-energy bound, with zero violations over a full training trajectory across four modalities and a natural-image benchmark. We locate the failure boundary at a Baik-Ben Arous-Péché threshold: a bulk-only probe suffers an exact damage $s^2/(1-s^2)$ from a data-induced spike, which becomes invisible once it exceeds $(d/n)^{1/4}$, confirmed on an injected-spike surrogate. Holding the probe spectrum fixed and varying only its eigenbasis, we show that spectral-shape matching is neither sufficient nor the operative object: a probe with a near-exact matched spectrum (log-ratio dispersion $0.02$) still inflates the residual $2 \times $-$90 \times $ when its eigenvectors are random. A closed-form whitened-volume law explains this and predicts the within-spectrum ordering of the critical volume, though not its cross-dataset magnitude. Downstream, per-layer tuning does not transfer to accuracy and the headroom is a small oracle-minus-white gap. Under a privacy mechanism the source ordering survives while learning does, then inverts at a noise cross-over where the flat probe overtakes the matched ones.

</details>


### [212] [Ranking and Partitioning of Attention Heads for Sparse and Continual Reinforcement Learning Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21336)
*Ruizhe Shi,Zaiwen Yang,Qiubo Wang,Banghua Zhu,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过按梯度范数对注意力头排序，实现语言模型的稀疏微调；并通过对注意力头跨任务划分来缓解持续学习中的跨任务干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型微调成本高，且持续学习易出现灾难性遗忘与跨任务干扰。作者希望仅训练少量关键注意力头以降低开销，并通过头划分减少任务间冲突、保留已学能力。

Method: 稀疏学习：计算各注意力头的梯度范数并排序，选取排名靠前的小子集进行训练。持续学习：将注意力头分配给不同任务，实现更新分离。理论部分建立梯度范数与训练有效性之间的关系，并分析稀疏、分离的头更新为何保持稳定。实验覆盖数学、指令跟随与代码任务。

Result: 在各任务上，调优高排名头持续优于低排名头，且常可媲美更密集的基线方法；头划分在保持对新任务适应性的同时提升了旧任务保留率。

Conclusion: 注意力头的梯度范数可作为训练有效性的有效指标；稀疏、分离的头级更新既能高效微调，又能缓解持续学习中的遗忘，为参数高效且稳定的语言模型适配提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We study sparse and continual reinforcement learning fine-tuning of language models through attention-head ranking and partitioning. For sparse learning, we rank attention heads by gradient norm to identify a small subset of heads that can be trained effectively. For continual learning, we partition heads across tasks to reduce cross-task interference and preserve previously learned capabilities. Theoretically, we relate gradient norm to training effectiveness, and further characterize why sparse, separated head updates can remain stable. Empirically, across math, instruction-following, and coding tasks, tuning higher-ranked heads consistently outperforms tuning lower-ranked ones while often matching denser baselines, and head partitioning improves retention while preserving adaptation to new tasks.

</details>


### [213] [Does On-Policy Distillation Need to Complete Every Rollout?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21337)
*Zhengxin Zhang,Maosen Tang,Ning Wang,Changhao Li,Jingren Zhou,Bo Dai,Sainyam Galhotra,Alex Townsend,Claire Cardie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机截断在线策略蒸馏STOPD，通过随机截断学生rollout并用分块逆概率加权(IPW)纠正采样偏差，减少25-48%生成token，实现1.3-2.2倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏(OPD)需对学生生成的完整rollout的每个token计算教师反馈，计算开销高昂。

Method: 每一步训练前为每条rollout采样停止位置，学生生成至该位置或自然终止；对已生成token获取教师反馈，采用分块逆概率加权补偿后段token被保留概率较低带来的偏差并控制权重。理论上证明该加权在期望上保持Vanilla OPD的梯度求和，并推导SGD、Adam、Muon的单步更新误差界。

Result: 在多种师生模型对、任务领域与优化器下评测，STOPD减少25-48%生成token，精度与Vanilla OPD相当，训练加速1.3-2.2倍。

Conclusion: STOPD在不损失精度的前提下显著降低OPD的计算成本，是一种有效的OPD加速方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) effectively transfers capabilities from a teacher to a student through feedback on student-generated rollouts. However, generating complete student rollouts and computing teacher feedback for every token make OPD computationally expensive. We introduce Stochastic Truncated On-Policy Distillation (STOPD), which accelerates OPD by randomly truncating student rollouts and correcting the resulting sampling bias with blockwise inverse-probability weighting (IPW). At each training step, we sample a stopping position for each rollout before generation and let the student generate until that position or natural termination. We obtain teacher feedback on every generated token and use blockwise IPW to compensate for the lower probability of retaining later tokens while controlling the weights. We prove that this weighting preserves Vanilla OPD's gradient sum in expectation and derive one-step update-error bounds for SGD, Adam, and Muon. We evaluate STOPD across multiple teacher-student model pairs, task domains, and optimizers. STOPD reduces generated tokens by 25-48% while maintaining accuracy comparable to Vanilla OPD, achieving 1.3-2.2x training speedups.

</details>


### [214] [Online Quantile Omniprediction for Proper Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21342)
*Hyunsuk Kim,Isaac Gibbs,Michael W. Mahoney,Ryan J. Tibshirani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究分位数预测形式下的 omniprediction，提出在线、样本高效算法，可在任意离散概率水平集合上同时对所有满足温和正则条件的 proper loss 保持准确，并在动态环境中同时兼顾多个损失，示例应用于 COVID-19 住院人数专家分位数预测的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 omniprediction 工作多集中于二分类，面向多分类与连续结果的方法误差收敛率较慢；实际应用需要同时在多个评价指标下准确、且能提供丰富风险信息并可在线适应动态环境的预测器。

Method: 将预测表示为任意离散概率水平集合上的一组分位数；基于 proper loss（由真实分位数最小化）构造在线、样本高效的算法，在温和正则条件下对全部此类损失同时保证准确性，并支持多损失联合优化与动态环境运行。

Result: 算法在样本效率上优于现有多分类/连续结果方法，能同时优化多个 proper loss 并在线适应动态分布；在疫情期间专家 COVID-19 住院人数分位数预测的集成任务中展示了实用性。

Conclusion: 分位数形式的 omniprediction 为多指标同时准确的预测提供了样本高效、可在线运行且支持多损失融合的框架，可为多样化终端用户提供风险评估信息并支撑实际决策。

Abstract: The task of constructing a predictor that is simultaneously accurate under multiple evaluation metrics has been popularized recently under the name omniprediction. Prior work in this area has largely focused on binary prediction tasks, and existing results for multiclass and continuous outcomes typically suffer from slow error rates. In this paper, we consider forecasts expressed as a set of quantiles over a discrete but otherwise arbitrary set of probability levels. We develop sample-efficient algorithms for constructing forecasts that are accurate simultaneously across all proper losses that satisfy mild regularity conditions, where proper losses are those minimized by predicting the true quantiles. Our algorithms are online and can operate in a dynamic environment, while simultaneously taking multiple losses into account, and their forecasts offer the potential to provide diverse end-users with rich information for evaluating risk and informing a range of practical decision-making tasks. As an example, we demonstrate how our procedure can be used to ensemble quantile forecasts of COVID-19 hospitalizations made by experts during the pandemic.

</details>


### [215] [Honesty is the Best Policy ... or is it? Demand Management for Probabilistic Service Options](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21351)
*Daniela Sailer,Robert Klein,Claudius Steinhardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究服务提供商在预订时向顾客披露"概率性服务水平"信息时的策略性操纵问题：将真实概率递归定义，引入透明度约束限制信息失真，并用强化学习近似真实概率、以基于采样的混合整数规划优化信息披露，揭示个体层面透明度与总体顾客效用之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为帮助顾客做出知情选择并调节各服务选项的需求，提供商可在预订时展示预测服务水平的概率。但在许多服务场景中，真实概率内生于系统状态而非静态产品属性，顾客依赖该信息决策却难以验证其准确性，因此提供商有动机操纵所显示的概率以引导顾客选择，形成一种新的需求管理手段。

Method: 将问题建模为序贯决策过程，递归定义真实概率，并提出限制概率失真的透明度约束；由于真实概率在现实规模下难以精确计算，采用适配的强化学习算法进行近似；进一步提出基于采样的混合整数规划，优化提供商在各状态下所沟通的信息。

Result: 框架可帮助提供商评估在预订时动态披露概率信息的收益，并揭示操纵激励；以户外配送（投递至包裹柜或商店）为背景的计算研究表明，个体层面透明度与总体顾客效用之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: 概率性服务信息披露是有效的需求管理杠杆，但存在操纵风险；通过透明度约束与优化框架可在可控失真下评估披露价值，且需在个体透明度与总体效用之间做出权衡。

Abstract: To enable well-informed customer choices and redistribute demand across service options, providers can display probabilities regarding predicted service levels at the time of booking, effectively offering probabilistic service options. In many service settings, the underlying truthful probabilities endogenously depend on the system state, rather than being static product attributes controlled by the provider. While customers rely on this information when making decisions, they cannot readily verify its accuracy. Consequently, providers may be tempted to manipulate the displayed probabilities to guide customers' choices in their favor, creating a novel lever for demand management. In this work, we model the resulting problem as a sequential decision process, define truthful probabilities recursively, and introduce transparency constraints that limit their distortion. Because the exact computation of truthful probabilities is intractable for realistic settings, we develop an adapted reinforcement learning algorithm to approximate them. Furthermore, we propose a sampling-based mixed-integer program to optimize the information communicated by the provider in each state. Our framework enables providers to gauge the benefits of dynamically disclosing probabilistic information at the time of booking and sheds light on incentives for manipulation. Our computational study is motivated by out-of-home delivery, i.e., the delivery to parcel lockers or shops, and reveals a trade-off between individual-level transparency and aggregate customer utilities.

</details>


### [216] [Same Target, Different Origins: Aligning Latent Rollout for Consistent PDE Emulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21370)
*Xinying Li,Jing Xu,Wenjun Wang,Limei Han,Fenglei Cao,Ruoxi Jiang,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Align Latent Rollout (ALR)正则化方法，通过一致性约束缓释神经PDE求解器自回归rollout中误差随起点累积传播的问题，提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归rollout递归调用学习到的演化算子并将预测反馈为输入，近似误差沿rollout传播，导致同一目标时刻的预测依赖于其时间起点与rollout深度；而对良定确定性PDE流，沿同一物理轨迹的不同时刻状态在精确演化下应到达同一目标状态。

Method: ALR在求解器内部隐表示层面施加跨rollout起点的一致性正则，不改动主干架构。基于隐差异传播的二阶Taylor分析导出两个目标：零阶隐状态一致性（对齐共享目标时刻的表示）与一阶隐动力学一致性（对齐对共享扰动的局部演化响应）。

Result: 在多个PDE基准与多种神经算子主干上的实验表明，ALR提升预测精度，同时降低不同rollout起点在同一目标时刻的差异。

Conclusion: 隐空间rollout一致性正则是一种与主干无关、可插拔的通用手段，可用于改善神经PDE求解器长程rollout的稳定性与精度。

Abstract: Neural PDE solvers commonly generate multi-step predictions through autoregressive rollout, recursively applying a learned evolution operator and feeding each prediction back as input for the next step. Approximation errors can therefore propagate through the rollout, causing predictions at a shared target time to depend on their temporal origins and rollout depths. For a well-posed deterministic PDE flow, however, states at different times along the same physical trajectory should, under exact evolution, reach the same target state. To this end, we propose Align Latent Rollout (ALR), a regularization approach that promotes consistency across rollout origins through the neural PDE solver's internal representations. A second-order Taylor analysis of latent discrepancy propagation motivates two objectives: zeroth-order latent state consistency, which aligns representations at a shared target time, and first-order latent dynamics consistency, which aligns local evolution responses to shared perturbations. ALR can be integrated into different neural PDE solvers by regularizing suitable internal representations, without modifying their backbone architectures. Experiments across multiple PDE benchmarks and neural-operator backbones show that ALR improves prediction accuracy while reducing shared-target discrepancies across rollout origins.

</details>


### [217] [Memorilla: Latent Semantic Memory for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21375)
*Adibvafa Fallahpour,Parsa Idehpour,Vignesh Kothapalli,Nikita Mounier,Shirley Wu,Shayan Pardis,Jure Leskovec*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memorilla 是一种用于冻结大语言模型的记忆系统，将整个文档集合的信息压缩进固定数量的查询相关“记忆 token”（仅 16 个），在 8 个基准中的 5 个上优于 Mem0、MemoRAG 等基线，并在智能体持续学习场景中提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的 LLM 只能利用记忆系统放入上下文窗口的信息，而现有系统难以在保持上下文简短的同时，为每个查询收集并组合相关信息，因此需要一种高效、可扩展的记忆机制。

Method: Memorilla 先将文档（用户对话、书籍章节等）一次性编码为可复用嵌入，然后学习在 LLM 输入嵌入空间中检索并放置与查询相关的信息，把整个文档集合的信息汇聚为固定数量的、查询相关的记忆 token，输入冻结 LLM。

Result: 仅用 16 个记忆 token，Memorilla 在个性化、长文档与知识密集型问答等 8 个基准中的 5 个上取得最佳表现，优于 Mem0 与 MemoRAG；在 FactKG 上准确率达 84.33%，超过全上下文推理的 80.43%，且表示源文档所用 token 数减少 108 倍。在 TextWorld 中，其减少智能体在已探索房间间的徘徊，平均成功率由全历史上下文学习的 18.6% 提升至 24.2%。

Conclusion: Memorilla 帮助冻结 LLM 在长上下文交互中有效积累与使用知识，是迈向持续学习型 AI 系统的一步。

Abstract: Memory enables large language models (LLMs) to use information beyond their parameters, such as past user conversations, long documents, or an agent's earlier actions. For a frozen LLM, access to this accumulated knowledge is limited to what a memory system places in its context window. However, gathering and combining relevant information needed for each query while keeping the model's context short remains challenging for existing systems. Here we introduce Memorilla, a memory system that gathers information from an entire document collection into a fixed number of query-dependent 'memory tokens' for a frozen LLM. Memorilla encodes documents (such as user chats, book chapters) once into reusable embeddings, then learns to retrieve and place query-specific information in the LLM's input embedding space. Using only 16 memory tokens, Memorilla achieves the best performance against baselines including Mem0 and MemoRAG on five of eight benchmarks spanning personalization, long-document and knowledge-intensive question answering. Notably, on FactKG, it reaches 84.33% accuracy versus 80.43% with full-context inference, while using 108x fewer tokens to represent the source documents. Furthermore, Memorilla can facilitate continual learning in agentic systems where the document count (the agent's past turns) increases throughout the trajectory. In a TextWorld environment, we show that Memorilla reduces the agent's tendency to wander between rooms it has already explored, and improves its average success rate from 18.6% with full-history in-context learning to 24.2%. Overall, Memorilla aids frozen LLMs in accumulating and using knowledge effectively across long-context interactions and takes a step towards building continually learning AI systems.

</details>


### [218] [Does Compression Imply Generalization? A Minimum Description Length Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21376)
*Lincen Yang,Jiayang Shi,Nan Pu,Daniël M. Pelt,Matthijs van Leeuwen,Zhong Li,Shujian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从MDL视角提出"预序遗憾"(prequential regret)作为仅依赖训练轨迹即可计算的可压缩性信号，并在插值regime下证明其可上界算法诱导分布下参数描述长度，进而导出PAC-MDL泛化界；同时将IB的编码器复杂度修正与MDL参数描述长度统一，实验上在CIFAR-10/100上其与泛化误差的Pearson相关超过0.9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代学习理论的核心问题是：何种压缩概念能解释过参数化神经网络的泛化？现有回答在权重、表示等不同层级度量复杂度，但相关量要么原则上需要留出数据，要么依赖困难（超高维）的互信息估计，难以实际计算。

Method: 以最小描述长度(MDL)原理为框架，提出prequential regret作为可仅由训练轨迹计算的补充信号；在(近)插值regime下理论证明期望prequential regret上界算法诱导分布下训练参数的描述长度，并将该描述长度转化为PAC-MDL设定下的高概率泛化界；同时证明算法级信息瓶颈(IB)中新增的编码器复杂度修正被某个MDL参数描述长度所上界，从而连接MDL与IB两种压缩视角；在CIFAR-10/100上做经验验证。

Result: 理论上建立了prequential regret—描述长度—泛化界之间的链条，并给出IB编码器复杂度修正与MDL描述长度的上界关系；实验中prequential regret与泛化误差的Pearson相关系数超过0.9（CIFAR-10与CIFAR-100）。

Conclusion: prequential regret是一种无需留出数据或高维互信息估计、可直接从训练轨迹计算的实用压缩/复杂度信号，可作为过参数化网络泛化的解释性度量，并在MDL与IB两种压缩观之间架起桥梁。

Abstract: A central question in modern learning theory is what notion of compression, if any, explains why over-parameterized neural networks generalize. Existing answers measure complexity at different levels, ranging from weights to representations; however, estimating the associated quantities either requires held-out data in principle or relies on the challenging (ultra) high-dimensional mutual information estimation. To overcome these challenges, we approach the question through the minimum description length (MDL) principle, and specifically, we propose the prequential regret as a complementary signal that is computable from the training trajectory alone. We establish that, in the (nearly) interpolating regime, the expected prequential regret upper-bounds the description length of the trained parameter under the algorithm-induced distribution, and that this description length translates into a high-probability generalization bound in the PAC-MDL setting. Along the way, we show that the encoder-complexity correction recently added to the algorithm-level information bottleneck (IB) is upper-bounded by an MDL parameter description length, \new{bringing together} the two views of compression from MDL and IB. Empirically, the prequential regret tracks the generalization error on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, with Pearson correlations exceeding 0.9.

</details>


### [219] [RDL-Bench: A Benchmark for Task-Aware Question Answering over Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21382)
*Yao Cheng,Siqiang Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出RDL-Bench，一个面向真实关系数据库（RDB）的任务感知问答基准，将用户问题分为"推理型"（答案由当前数据库状态决定）与"预测型"（答案依赖时间约束下的未来结果），填补了现有基准孤立处理两类目标的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系数据库仍是结构化数据管理的核心基础设施，自然语言接口旨在让用户无需编写SQL即可提问。然而真实用户需求超出SQL式查询：部分问题需对可观测数据库状态进行推理，部分问题需在时间约束下依据历史关系记录做预测推断。现有基准对这两类目标分别孤立研究，缺乏统一评测平台，故需构建新基准。

Method: 构建RDL-Bench：将多样化用户问题组织为推理型与预测型两类；每个实例包含自然语言问题、任务元数据、模式信息、时间上下文、可执行证据或目标构造逻辑，以及指称级（denotation-level）真值。支持两种评测设置：(1) 原生求解器在其既定能力下求解；(2) 任务感知的模块化系统，由路由器将每个问题分派至相应执行模块。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数值或性能结果，主要贡献为基准的设计、问题分类体系、实例构成与双评测设置的提出。

Conclusion: RDL-Bench统一了关系数据库上推理型与预测型问答的评测，兼顾原生求解器与模块化任务感知系统的评估，为超越SQL式查询的真实NL2RDB需求提供了系统化基准。

Abstract: Relational databases (RDBs) remain the primary infrastructure for storing and managing structured data across enterprise and scientific domains. Natural-language (NL) interfaces to RDBs aim to let users ask questions without writing SQL, but real user needs go beyond SQL-style lookup: some questions require reasoning over the observable database state, while others require predictive inference from historical relational records under temporal constraints. Existing benchmarks address these goals in isolation. To address this gap, we introduce RDL-Bench, a benchmark for task-aware question answering (QA) over real RDBs. RDL-Bench organizes diverse user questions into reasoning-oriented questions, whose answers are determined by the current database state, and prediction-oriented questions, whose answers depend on future outcomes under temporal constraints. Each instance contains an NL question, task metadata, schema information, temporal context, either executable evidence or target-construction logic, and denotation-level ground truth. RDL-Bench supports two evaluation settings: native solvers under their intended capabilities, and task-aware modular systems where a router dispatches each question to an appropriate execution module.

</details>


### [220] [Fault Detection and Bit-Error Rate Diagnostics in Optical Wireless Links Using Edge-AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21402)
*Sarthak Bhattarai,Sheshadri Shikhar Bandyopadhyay,Suprabh Dhakal,Jitendra Kumar Manandhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种集成物理层信号遥测与TinyML分类引擎的自主自诊断光无线接收节点，基于ESP32-S3实现1000 bit/s OOK数据恢复与信道特征实时提取，并部署3.32 kB的INT8量化TFLite Micro模型完成四类工况的实时故障分类（总体准确率74.68%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由空间光链路对视线遮挡、光衰减、环境光饱和及收发端失配极为敏感；传统信道诊断依赖外部计算节点或云端处理，导致诊断时延大、边缘节点自主性差。

Method: 以ESP32-S3为核心构建接收机，采用5样本多数表决机制恢复1000 bit/s OOK载荷，同时实时提取BER、接收信号电压、SNR(dB)与环境光基线四类信道特征；在固件端部署后训练INT8量化的TensorFlow Lite Micro模型，并与随机森林及浮点神经网络基线对比。

Result: 随机森林基线准确率77.22%，浮点神经网络78.48%；INT8量化模型仅占3.32 kB SRAM，在正常传输、滤光片衰减、环境光干扰、严重遮挡/失配四种工况下取得74.68%总体准确率，片上测试验证了实时、确定性的故障分类。

Conclusion: 该工作表明在资源受限的光无线接收端直接进行片上信号遥测与TinyML诊断是可行的，为具备自感知能力的光学IoT节点提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Optical Wireless Communication (OWC) systems provide high-bandwidth, spatially secure, and RF-interference-free digital transmission. However, direct free-space optical links suffer from extreme sensitivity to line-of-sight disruptions, optical attenuation, high ambient light saturation, and transmitter-receiver misalignment. Traditional channel diagnostic frameworks rely on external computing nodes or cloud-based processing, which introduces severe diagnostic latency and reduces edge node autonomy. This paper presents an autonomous, self-diagnosing optical wireless receiver node that integrates physical-layer signal telemetry extraction with an on-device TinyML classification engine. Built around an ESP32-S3 microcontroller, the receiver recovers 1000 bit/s On-Off Keying (OOK) digital payloads using a 5-sample majority voting mechanism while concurrently extracting real-time channel features: Bit Error Rate (BER), received signal voltage (Vsignal), Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNRdB), and ambient light baseline (Lambient). Evaluated against a Random Forest baseline (77.22% accuracy) and a floating-point neural network (78.48% accuracy), a post-training INT8 quantized TensorFlow Lite Micro model occupying only 3.32 kB of SRAM runs directly on the receiver firmware. The quantized model achieves 74.68% overall accuracy across four distinct operational conditions: normal transmission, optical filter attenuation, ambient light interference, and severe obstruction/misalignment. On-device deployment tests confirm real-time, deterministic fault classification, establishing a practical approach for self-aware optical IoT nodes.

</details>


### [221] [Multi-Reward Hacking: From Aggregation-Induced Drift to Trust-Anchored Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21404)
*Yushu Li,Jiin Woo,Xiaoxiao Li,Youngsuk Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示多奖励聚合中的"聚合诱导失败"：对称聚合会让总体奖励上升但主任务性能下降，从理论上刻画了多奖励hacking的成因，并提出Trust-Anchored Gating (TAG) 通过锚定主通道来结构性消除该失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM后训练常将主奖励（如正确性）与辅助奖励（如简洁性、效率）结合以在提升任务表现的同时引导理想响应属性。但奖励的聚合方式会显著影响训练结果；作者发现对称聚合（平等对待各奖励通道）可能导致总体奖励改善而主任务性能退化的失败，亟需理解其机理并加以解决。

Method: 1）通过一步多奖励决策建模，刻画聚合诱导失败发生及构成多奖励hacking的条件；2）理论证明在对称聚合下，确定性条件均值GRPO在辅助奖励更易获得时会收敛到偏向辅助的行为，且与初始化无关；3）提出TAG：先基于各通道单独优化引起的策略漂移选出锚定通道，再以锚定通道满足度为门控条件来分配辅助信用，从而在结构上消除仅辅助成功带来的信用，同时保留联合成功的额外信用。

Result: 在同一分析框架下，当主通道被锚定时，TAG可证明地反转该选择，从任意初始化收敛到偏向主任务的行为。在Qwen与Llama上的数学与程序合成实验表明，对称、约束及归一化聚合均出现显著性能退化，而TAG可缓解该失败；以简洁奖励为例，Qwen3-4B上TAG的pass@4为0.581，加性聚合为0.220，同时相比仅正确性训练响应缩短44%。

Conclusion: 多奖励聚合方式本身会导致主任务性能退化，对称聚合下的失败可用多奖励hacking刻画并有理论保证。TAG通过对锚定通道的信用门控，既在理论上消除偏向辅助的收敛，又在实验中显著优于静态重加权等对校准敏感的方法，兼顾主任务性能与辅助属性优化。

Abstract: To improve task performance while encouraging desirable response properties, LLM post-training often combines a primary reward (e.g., correctness) with auxiliary rewards (e.g., brevity or efficiency). However, how these rewards are aggregated can critically affect training outcomes. We show that symmetric aggregation, which treats reward channels equally, can induce a failure where aggregate reward improves while primary-task performance degrades, even when individual rewards are meaningful. We investigate this aggregation-induced failure through a one-step multi-reward decision formulation, characterizing when it constitutes multi-reward hacking. We prove that, under symmetric aggregation, deterministic conditional-mean GRPO converges to auxiliary-favoring behavior when auxiliary rewards are more reliably obtained, regardless of initialization. We propose Trust-Anchored Gating (TAG), which first elects an anchor channel based on the policy drift induced by optimizing each channel alone, then gates auxiliary credit on anchor satisfaction. Unlike calibration-sensitive static reweighting, TAG structurally eliminates credit for auxiliary-only outcomes while preserving additional credit for joint success. In the same analytical setting, TAG provably reverses this selection, converging to the primary-favoring action from any initialization when the primary channel is anchored. Experiments across Qwen and Llama models on mathematics and program synthesis reveal substantial degradation under symmetric, constrained, and normalized aggregation, whereas TAG mitigates this failure. On Qwen3-4B with a brevity reward, TAG achieves 0.581 pass@4 versus 0.220 for additive aggregation, while shortening responses by 44% relative to correctness-only training.

</details>


### [222] [Activation-Adjacent Layer Normalization: Effects on Approximation Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21527)
*Yunhao Ni,Yuxin Guo,Jie Luo,Wenjun Wu,Lei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带归一化（RMSNorm/LayerNorm）的浅层网络在宽度受限时能否精确或近似地表示给定的一隐藏层MLP，给出宽度N+1/N+2的构造与下界，并做数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LN与RMSNorm依赖输入且推理时仍非线性，置于激活旁会改变网络所表示的函数；需澄清归一化架构的表达能力是否因非线性归一化而被削弱，以及宽度代价有多大。

Method: 将RMSNorm视为输入相关的缩放，通过宽度增广在紧特征集上局部线性化归一化；分别构造激活后归一化（AN）和激活前归一化（PN）网络；PN借助仿射参数或一次正齐次性，一般连续激活用宽度N+r并以O_f(r^{-1})误差逼近；LN构造使用中心化坐标对，宽度为N+2与N+2r；并证明下界与通用逼近定理的迁移。

Result: AN在无界激活范围下宽度N+1，任意连续非常数激活宽度N+r误差O_f(r^{-1})；PN在仿射参数或一次正齐次下宽度N+1，一般连续激活宽度N+r误差趋零；LN相应为N+2与N+2r；证明RMSNorm的N+1、LN的N+2为最坏情形下界；数值实验在五种设定下复现预测的逼近率，并展示shifted-ReLU的有限宽度障碍。

Conclusion: 归一化并不必然降低表达能力，但需付出有限宽度代价：RMSNorm至少N+1、LN至少N+2，且这些界是紧的；普通MLP的通用逼近定理可迁移至这些归一化架构。

Abstract: Layer normalization (LN) and root mean square normalization (RMSNorm) depend on their inputs and remain nonlinear at inference. Inserting either next to an activation may therefore change the function a network represents. We study when shallow activation-normalized (AN) and preactivation-normalized (PN) networks can approximate a prescribed width-$N$ one-hidden-layer MLP. Viewing RMSNorm as input-dependent scaling, we use width augmentation to locally linearize normalization on compact feature sets. For AN networks, an unbounded activation range allows width $N+1$, while every continuous nonconstant activation admits width $N+r$ with error $O_f(r^{-1})$. For PN networks, affine parameters or, without them, degree-one positive homogeneity recover width $N+1$; general continuous activations admit width $N+r$ with error tending to zero. The corresponding LN constructions use centered coordinate pairs, yielding widths $N+2$ and $N+2r$, respectively. We prove that $N+1$ for RMSNorm and $N+2$ for LN are also the worst-case bound for approximations, and show that applicable universal-approximation theorems for ordinary MLPs transfer to these normalized architectures. Numerical experiments recover the predicted approximation rates for both normalizations across all five settings and illustrate the shifted-ReLU finite-width obstruction.

</details>


### [223] [Federated Art Chase: Privacy Leakage and Data Poisoning in Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21547)
*Vasileios Perifanis,Nikolaos Pavlidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以可复现流水线研究「中心微调+联邦适配」范式的隐私与完整性：用ImageNet预训练MobileNetV4在小样本Quick, Draw!上中心微调后做head-only FedAvg，并系统评估梯度反演、标签计数恢复、后门与标签翻转攻击，以及FLTrust的防御效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FL已从"从零训练"转向"适配已中心微调的预训练模型"，但该场景下的隐私泄露与投毒风险缺乏系统、可复现的实证评估。

Method: 中心微调MobileNetV4-Conv-Small于10类Quick, Draw!（97.50%测试精度），再在10个非IID客户端上做head-only FedAvg；设置四个对抗场景：(1) 单客户端单步更新的honest-but-curious服务器梯度反演；(2) 单轮FedAvg聚合的标签计数恢复；(3) 30%/50%/100%恶意客户端下的后门与标签翻转投毒；(4) 保留中心微调root set的FLTrust防御；并提供基于Indigma FedMaestro的现场演示与Colab代码。

Result: (1) 单步更新可恢复完整私有标签集，但重建图像趋近类原型（平均SSIM=0.130）；(2) 标签计数恢复准确率随客户端数由87.5%(1个)降至75.0%(5/10个)，低于无信息均匀猜测的80.0%；(3) FedAvg下后门ASR为21.9%/31.3%/54.7%，干净精度损失≤0.8个百分点，标签翻转仅在多数源类数据被改标时有效；(4) FLTrust将30%/50%恶意比例下后门ASR降至1.8%/4.0%，标签翻转ASR由12.6%降至0.8%且无明显干净精度损失，但全员恶意时无防护；客户端训练精度由90.34%升至91.49%且全局精度保持。

Conclusion: head-only FedAvg适配预训练模型在保持全局精度的同时提升客户端精度；其隐私泄露主要表现为标签级信息而非像素级重建，投毒存在非平凡但中等强度的威胁，基于可信root set的FLTrust显著降低低比例恶意场景的攻击成功率，但对全恶意客户端无效。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is increasingly used to adapt a pretrained, centrally fine-tuned model to data that remain on user devices, beyond training a model from scratch. In this report, prepared for the 4th Federated Machine Learning International Summer School, we study the privacy and integrity of this approach with a reproducible pipeline. An ImageNet-pretrained MobileNetV4-Conv-Small model is fine-tuned centrally on ten Quick, Draw! classes (97.50% test accuracy) and then adapted with head-only FedAvg over ten non-IID clients, which raises client training accuracy from 90.34% to 91.49% while preserving global accuracy. We evaluate four adversarial scenarios. First, from a single one-step client update, an honest-but-curious server recovers the complete private label set, but its reconstructions follow class prototypes rather than the private drawings (mean SSIM 0.130). Second, from a one-round FedAvg aggregate, label-count recovery falls from 87.5% with one client to 75.0% with five and ten clients, below the 80.0% of an uninformed uniform guess. Third, under FedAvg, a backdoor injected by 30%, 50%, and 100% of the clients reaches an attack success rate (ASR) of 21.9%, 31.3%, and 54.7% at a cost of at most 0.8 percentage points of clean accuracy, whereas label flipping is effective only when most of the source-class data are relabeled. Fourth, FLTrust with a root set retained from centralized fine-tuning lowers the backdoor ASR to 1.8% and 4.0% at 30% and 50% malicious clients and the label-flipping ASR from 12.6% to 0.8% without measurable clean-accuracy loss, but provides no protection when every client is malicious. The report closes with a live session in which participants draw, fine-tune, and poison the model through Indigma's FedMaestro orchestrator. Code and executed notebooks, runnable on Google Colab, are at https://github.com/Indigma-Innovations/fl-summer-school.

</details>


### [224] [Finite-Time Guarantees for Constrained Reinforcement Learning under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21548)
*Valentina Zaccaria,Philip Jordan,Tingting Ni,Gian Antonio Susto,Maryam Kamgarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究约束部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（CPOMDP）的强化学习，用有限历史窗口近似将CPOMDP转化为约束MDP，提出原始-对偶算法，首次给出同时约束平均奖励次优性与约束违反的非渐近保证，误差随窗口长度几何衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPOMDP中状态不可完全观测，最优策略可能依赖任意长的观测-动作历史，使规划与学习极其困难；现有方法缺乏对原始CPOMDP中奖励与约束违反的联合非渐近理论保证。

Method: 采用有限记忆近似：将有限长度历史窗口作为约束MDP的状态，在此基础上设计原始-对偶算法求解；在滤波稳定性（filter-stability）假设下，推导有限状态模型与原CPOMDP之间最优性与可行性的关系界。

Result: 给出首个联合界定原始CPOMDP平均奖励次优性与约束违反的非渐近保证；近似误差随窗口长度几何（指数）衰减；在部分可观测gridworld实验中验证方法有效性。

Conclusion: 有限历史窗口近似结合原始-对偶学习为CPOMDP提供了可证明有效的求解框架，其理论误差可控且随窗口增长迅速收敛，实验亦支持其实际效果。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning in constrained partially observable Markov decision processes (CPOMDPs), where an agent maximizes reward subject to a cost constraint using only incomplete information about the underlying state. The lack of state observability may require optimal policies to depend on arbitrarily long observation-action histories, making planning and learning challenging. We leverage recent advances in finite-memory approximations of POMDPs and represent finite history windows as states of a constrained MDP. We then develop a primal--dual algorithm that provides the first non-asymptotic guarantees that jointly bound average reward suboptimality and constraint violation in the original CPOMDP. A key challenge is that this finite-window approximation affects both reward values and the set of feasible policies. Under a filter-stability assumption, we derive bounds relating optimality and feasibility in the finite-state model to those in the original CPOMDP, with approximation errors that decay geometrically with the window length. Experiments in partially observable gridworlds demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [225] [Uncertainty Quantification in Pairwise Difference Learning for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21554)
*Mohamed Karim Belaid,Maximilian Rabus,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向分类任务的成对差分学习（PDL）：学习一个以两个样本为输入、预测其结果差的函数，查询样本的预测由与所有训练样本的差分预测平均得到；并提出相应的元学习技术，同时将总不确定性分解为偶然性与认知性两部分，把不确定性量化扩展到 PDL 框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准学习范式直接学习"实例→结果"的映射；PDL 则通过对实例间的结果差分建模来间接预测，可能带来不同归纳偏置。已有工作缺少分类场景下的 PDL 及其元学习方案，也缺少对 PDL 预测不确定性的忠实表示与量化。

Method: 1) 构建用于分类的 PDL 分类器：基学习器接收成对样本输出结果差，查询预测通过对所有训练样本的差分预测进行平均获得；2) 设计适配该结构的元学习技术以训练该差分函数；3) 借鉴总不确定性分解为偶然性（aleatoric）与认知性（epistemic）的近期工作，依据基学习器对不确定性的表示方式与程度，提出多种将相应度量推广到 PDL 的途径。

Result: 在大规模实证研究中，同时评估了该 PDL 分类器的预测准确率与不确定性感知能力（uncertainty awareness）。

Conclusion: PDL 可作为分类的一种可行替代范式，且通过合理的元学习与不确定性分解推广，能够以忠实方式表示和量化其预测不确定性；实验从准确率与不确定性感知两方面对其进行了系统评估。

Abstract: Instead of learning a mapping from instances to outcomes in the standard way, the key idea of pairwise difference learning (PDL) is to learn a function that takes two instances as input and predicts the difference between the respective outcomes. Given a function of this kind, predictions for a query instance are derived from every training example and then averaged. We propose a version of PDL for the task of classification and propose a suitable meta-learning technique for this purpose. Moreover, we address the problem of uncertainty quantification, i.e., of representing and quantifying the uncertainty of a PDL classifier's predictions in a faithful manner. Building on recent work on the decomposition of total uncertainty into aleatoric and epistemic components, we propose different ways of extending corresponding measures to the setting of PDL, depending on how and to what extent uncertainty is represented by the underlying base learner. In large-scale empirical studies, we analyze both the predictive accuracy and the uncertainty awareness of our PDL classifier.

</details>


### [226] [When Does Muon Actually Win? A Paired Data-, Time-, and Tuning-Budget Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21555)
*Mushir Akhtar,Hazem N. Nounou,Majdi Mansouri,Mohamed N. Nounou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在显式数据/时间/调参预算下于单张A100对比Muon与AdamW：等数据量时Muon降低验证损失AUC 3.25%–22.44%，但优化器调用开销占训练时间56.1%–69.6%，使时间归一化后视觉任务领先、语言任务落后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在数据效率上的优势已受关注，但其在显式数据、时间与调参预算下的实际表现缺乏系统评估，尤其缺少把优化器计算开销计入墙钟时间的公平对比。

Method: 单张A100 GPU上，将参考单设备Muon实现与AdamW对比；模型包括17.7M和33.6M参数的因果语言模型及2.70M参数的视觉Transformer。采用统一的23候选开发预算选配置，并以5个配对随机种子评估，记录完整验证轨迹、优化器单步耗时，并做冻结的一次性测试。

Result: 等数据量下Muon将验证损失AUC降低3.25%–22.44%，并在全部20组主对比中降低测试损失。计入时间后：视觉任务上Muon的时间归一化损失面积改善19.68%–22.31%，语言任务上AdamW反而优3.38%–6.96%。完整Muon优化器调用（含辅助AdamW更新）占训练时间56.1%–69.6%，AdamW基线仅6.1%–9.1%。在受限候选池下Muon的期望遗憾更高。

Conclusion: 结论仅刻画小模型与参考内核实现；墙钟时间排序不能外推为更大模型或优化实现下的优劣排名。

Abstract: Muon's gains in data efficiency motivate evaluating its performance under explicit data, time, and tuning budgets. On a single A100 GPU, we compare a reference single-device Muon implementation with AdamW across 17.7M- and 33.6M-parameter causal language models and 2.70M-parameter vision transformers. A common 23-candidate development budget selects configurations evaluated with five matched seeds, full validation trajectories, optimizer-step timing, and a frozen one-shot test. Muon reduces validation-loss area under the curve by 3.25--22.44\% at equal data and lowers test loss in all 20 primary pairs. In this configuration, Muon improves time-normalized loss area} by 19.68--22.31\% on vision, while AdamW is better by 3.38--6.96\% on language. The full Muon optimizer call, including auxiliary AdamW updates, consumes 56.1--69.6\% of training time, compared with 6.1--9.1\% for the AdamW baseline. Muon also has higher expected regret under restricted access to these candidate pools. These results characterize small models with reference kernels; their wall-clock ordering does not establish a ranking for larger models or optimized implementations.

</details>


### [227] [A Quantum Machine Learning Approach to Content-Based Media Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21562)
*Mina Abbaszadeh,Ouissal Moumou,Anna Pearson,Bob Coecke,Rob Cooper,Alessandro Piscopo,Mehrnoosh Sadrzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文基于量子自然语言处理(QNLP)框架构建内容型媒体推荐系统，将节目简介映射为变分量子电路并训练，在BBC iPlayer数据上以极少参数达到与BiLSTM相当的性能，并在Quantinuum H2真实硬件上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索变分量子电路在推荐任务中的潜力，寻求参数高效的文本表示替代方案，并推动量子机器学习向真实硬件部署迈进。

Method: 基于QNLP框架，利用节目简介的语义结构将文本映射为变分量子电路；通过监督式成对相似度学习任务训练电路参数；与同数据同目标训练的经典BiLSTM基线对比；并采用基于SWAP-test的协议在Quantinuum System Model H2及其仿真器上执行训练后电路。

Result: 两模型在相似度学习任务上性能相当，但量子模型参数显著更少(47k vs. 17.8M)；将BiLSTM限制到同等参数预算后其性能下降3.63个百分点(MAP)；硬件实验表明在真实含噪条件下相似度信号得以保留。

Conclusion: 结构化量子表示在此场景下是高度参数高效的替代方案，变分量子电路在推荐任务中具有应用潜力，并代表了迈向实际部署的务实一步。

Abstract: We use quantum machine learning techniques to develop a content-based media recommender system and evaluate its performance on TV programmes using textual descriptions from one week of BBC iPlayer data. Grounded in the Quantum Natural Language Processing framework (QNLP), programme synopses are mapped to variational quantum circuits using their semantic structure. The parameters of these circuits are trained via a supervised pairwise similarity learning task.
  We benchmark the quantum recommender against a classical BiLSTM baseline trained on the same dataset and objective. While both models achieve comparable performance on the similarity learning task, the quantum model requires significantly fewer parameters (47k vs. 17.8M). When the BiLSTM is constrained to a matching parameter budget, its performance degrades substantially (by 3.63 percentage points in Mean Average Precision (MAP)), indicating that the structured quantum representation provides a highly parameter-efficient alternative in this setting. We demonstrate the feasibility of executing the trained models on quantum hardware by deploying the circuits on Quantinuum's System Model H2 (and accompanying emulator) using a SWAP-test-based protocol. The results confirm that the similarity signal is preserved under realistic, noisy conditions. Taken together, these findings highlight the potential of variational quantum circuits for recommendation tasks and represent a practical step toward their real-world deployment.

</details>


### [228] [Sharp and Stable Label-Free Calibration Certificates from Logical Coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21567)
*Takahiro Kato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文针对"逐题回答的逻辑相关查询型概率预测器"在标签缺失下无法检验校准的问题，提出以赌徒下注策略受限方式为参数的极小极大理论：对每种约束给出仅凭答案分布即可得到的尖锐无标签下界（校准残差），并给出可实际计算的证书（离线固定节点与在线 anytime-valid 两类）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询条件下概率预测器一次回答一个逻辑相关问题，检验其校准所需的标签常常缺失，因此需要不依赖标签就能给出校准误差下界（"认证"）的方法；同时需要刻画不同下注/约束下界的结构性质（可否分离、是否连续稳定），以判断哪些证书可靠、何时不可归因于单个问题。

Method: （1）对每类下注约束（mean、marginal、joint 等阶段）建立极小极大定理，在有限支撑的答案律上给出尖锐无标签下界；（2）分析下界在事件束上的严格分离性与可归因性；（3）讨论 marginal 界对答案的不连续性及 Wasserstein 连续性下界不可达常数因子；（4）引入 Lipschitz 下注得到稳定界，并证明其对平滑校准残差尖锐、与 marginal 界同号；（5）i.i.d. 流上用固定节点的投影在线梯度上升给出固定时域证书（误差由网格、学习、统计三项界定），并用可预测下注给出 anytime-valid 证书；（6）在 4 个预测器×2 个数据集上做预注册实证研究（8 对中 7 对通过试点并给出阳性证书）。

Result: 极小极大下界在相应约束类中尖锐；mean、marginal、joint 阶段可严格分离，束上的界在缺乏其他问题假设时无法归因于单个问题；尖锐 marginal 界不连续，且任何 Wasserstein 连续的无标签下界都无法在其常数倍内逼近；Lipschitz 下注给出稳定且对平滑残差尖锐的界，且对任意正 Lipschitz 常数其正性与尖锐 marginal 界正性等价；离线证书短缺陷于网格+学习+统计三项，在线证书 anytime-valid；实证中 7 个确认性证书全部为正。

Conclusion: 该框架把"无标签校准认证"形式化为受限下注下的极小极大问题，给出尖锐、可算且有稳定性/连续性保障的下界，并配套离线与在线两类证书；需强调证书只说明校准至少差到该程度，取零仅表示"未被反驳"而非校准良好，因此适用性受下注约束选择与问题间依赖假设的限制。

Abstract: Query-conditioned probabilistic predictors answer logically related questions one at a time, and labels to check their calibration are often missing. For each restriction on what a bettor's stakes may use, a minimax theorem identifies, for finitely supported laws of the answers, the sharp label-free lower bound on the corresponding calibration residual. On bundles of different events its mean, marginal and joint stages can separate strictly, and a bundle's bound cannot be attributed to one question without assumptions on the others. The sharp marginal bound is discontinuous in the answers, and no label-free lower bound continuous in the Wasserstein distance stays within a constant factor of it. Lipschitz stakes give a stable bound, sharp for a smooth calibration residual and, for every positive Lipschitz constant, positive exactly when the sharp marginal bound is. On i.i.d. streams, projected online gradient ascent at fixed nodes gives fixed-horizon certificates whose shortfall is bounded by grid, learning and statistical terms, and predictable betting gives anytime-valid ones. In a preregistered study of four predictors on two data sets, all seven confirmatory certificates were positive (the eighth pair did not pass the pilot). A certificate shows only that calibration is at least this bad; zero means "not refuted".

</details>


### [229] [From Perception to Prediction and Intervention: Benchmarking Proactive Video Assistants in Real-Life Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21568)
*Boyu Cui,Junhao Luo,Zhihao Wang,Linghan Chen,Zejun Li,Jiwen Zhang,Siyuan Wang,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 P³IBench——面向真实流式场景的主动式视频理解基准，评估日常感知、风险预测与干预三项能力，测试12个视频语言模型并训练 P³I-Assistant-4B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准只考察模型能否解读已观察到的视觉内容，而真实助手还需预判需要何种帮助并决定何时介入，此能力缺口尚未被评测覆盖。

Method: 构建 P³IBench 基准，覆盖日常感知、风险预测、干预三类互补能力，聚焦真实生活流式场景；对12个代表性开源与闭源视频语言模型（含流式系统及改造的非流式系统）进行评测；并构造额外主动式训练数据训练 P³I-Assistant-4B。

Result: 现有模型难以做出良好校准的响应决策；经训练得到的 P³I-Assistant-4B 在若干任务上有所提升，但各能力间增益不均衡。

Conclusion: 有效的主动式辅助不仅依赖视觉理解，还需精准的响应时机把握与恰当的克制；当前模型在这两方面仍显著不足。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models have advanced video understanding, but existing benchmarks mainly evaluate whether models can interpret observed visual content. In real-world scenarios, assistants must additionally anticipate what assistance is needed and decide when to provide it. To address this gap, we introduce \textsc{P$^3$IBench}, a benchmark for proactive video understanding in real-life streaming scenarios. It evaluates three complementary capabilities: daily perception, risk prediction and intervention. We benchmark 12 representative open-source and proprietary video-language models, including streaming and adapted non-streaming systems. Our evaluation shows that current models struggle to make well-calibrated response decisions. Finally, we construct additional proactive training data and train \textsc{P$^3$I-Assistant-4B}, achieving improvements on several tasks, though gains remain uneven across capabilities. Together, these findings show that effective proactive assistance requires not only visual understanding, but also precise response timing and appropriate restraint.

</details>


### [230] [Learning to Search for Executable Patternmaking from Certified Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21579)
*Zhuotao Xu,Chi Zeng,Liying Yang,Xinxing Yu,Yanyan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SemDraft：首个从成对"改前/改后"缝纫纸样中学习搜索可执行制版程序的框架，无需记录编辑步骤；通过带三重校验的有界搜索恢复编辑动作序列，并用认证程序训练动作排序器，使认证案例数由≤53提升至63–64（1000次搜索内）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法从图像或文本生成缝纫纸样，仅产出最终纸样，不建模"如何由一个纸样演化为另一个纸样"的编辑过程。实际制版中新品样式多来自对已有纸样的编辑（加长衣身、移动省道、更换袖型），而纸样档案只留存结果、丢失步骤。

Method: 将步骤恢复建模为对编辑动作的搜索：有界搜索在有向动作空间中求解，使一个纸样变换为另一个；确定性编译器保证步骤可检查。认证需通过三重校验：(1) 从零重新编译；(2) 动作两两分类为有序或可交换；(3) 独立重放得到同一纸样。认证所得程序用于训练一个排序模型，在同一搜索内对候选动作打分排序。

Result: 在两份冻结的80个顺序敏感案例（order-sensitive）上，固定其余搜索组件，学习型排序器在1000次搜索执行内分别认证63与64例；非学习型排序器最多分别为53与52例。

Conclusion: 编辑步骤可由搜索恢复且可被确定性编译验证；用认证程序监督学习动作排序能显著提升搜索效率与成功率，为从无标注纸样档案中挖掘可执行制版知识提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent models generate sewing patterns from images or text, and program-based representations make these patterns executable. These systems produce a finished pattern and do not model how a patternmaker turns one pattern into another. In practice a new style is made by editing an existing pattern: lengthening the bodice, moving a dart, swapping a sleeve. Pattern archives record the results of such edits and none of the steps. We observe that the steps can be recovered by searching over editing actions until one pattern becomes the other. A deterministic compiler makes the recovered steps checkable. To this end, we present SemDraft, the first framework that learns to search for executable patternmaking programs from paired before-and-after sewing patterns without recorded editing steps. Specifically, a bounded search recovers a program of editing actions and certifies it only after three checks: it recompiles from scratch, every pair of its actions is classified as ordered or interchangeable, and an independent replay reaches the same pattern. The certified programs then train a model that ranks candidate actions inside the same search. On two frozen draws of 80 order-sensitive cases, with every other search component fixed, the learned ranker certifies 63 and 64 cases within 1,000 search executions, against at most 53 and 52 for non-learned rankers.

</details>


### [231] [Transfer Learning in Bandits from Unknown Adaptive Policies: Problem-Dependent Lower and Upper Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21582)
*Adrien Prevost,Timothee Mathieu,Waris Radji,Odalric-Ambrym Maillard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究历史数据由未知策略的自适应决策过程生成时的迁移学习随机多臂老虎机问题，提出KL-UCB-Transfer算法，在源-目标均值差有界时取得更优的对数遗憾常数，并在单边松弛与模型误设下刻画了三类遗憾区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迁移学习老虎机工作通常假设先验数据为固定离线数据集或来自已知源，而实际历史观测往往来自策略未知的既往自适应决策过程，样本量N'_k本身可能随机且依赖数据，这一更现实的情形缺乏理论与算法分析。

Method: 设定有界奖励[0,1]，对每个臂k获得N'_k个来自未知分布ν'_k的i.i.d.观测，N'_k可为随机；假设|μ_k-μ'_k|<L_k（已知常数）。推导渐近问题依赖下界，并提出KL-UCB-Transfer，将在线数据与历史数据融合用于置信上界构造；进一步将假设松弛为单边条件μ_k-μ'_k<L_k以分析误设下的稳健性。

Result: 得到渐近问题依赖下界；KL-UCB-Transfer在μ'_a<μ_1-L_a时具有改进的首项常数且保持对数遗憾，否则退化为经典KL-UCB的保证。在单边条件与误设下，刻画了从性能提升、对数界退化到保证丧失的三类区间；仿真验证了利用过程生成历史数据的实际收益。

Conclusion: 在历史数据由未知自适应策略生成、样本量随机的现实设定下，恰当融合历史与在线数据仍可获得严格的理论收益；但收益依赖于源-目标差异的幅度与方向，误设会连续地削弱乃至破坏对数遗憾保证，为迁移老虎机的稳健设计提供了理论刻画。

Abstract: Transfer learning in stochastic multi-armed bandits is typically studied assuming that prior data forms a fixed offline dataset or is generated by a known source. In practice, historical observations come from a previous adaptive decision process whose policy is unknown. We study this setting for bounded rewards in $[0,1]$.
  Before the online game starts, the learner receives, for each arm $k$, $N'_k$ observations drawn i.i.d.\ from an unknown distribution $ν'_k$, collected by an arbitrary and possibly data-dependent strategy, so the sample sizes $N'_k$ may be random. Source and target means satisfy $|μ_k-μ'_k|<L_k$ for known constants $(L_k)_k$.
  We derive an asymptotic problem-dependent lower bound and propose KL-UCB-Transfer, which combines online and historical data and achieves logarithmic regret with an improved leading constant whenever $μ'_a<μ_1-L_a$, and recovers the classical KL-UCB guarantee otherwise. Since bounding the source-target discrepancy is often unrealistic, we relax the assumption to the one-sided condition $μ_k-μ'_k<L_k$ and show that logarithmic regret can survive under misspecification, characterizing three regimes ranging from improvement to a degraded logarithmic bound to a loss of guarantee. Simulations illustrate the practical benefits of exploiting process-generated historical data.

</details>


### [232] [Waypoint-OPD: Transferring RL-Induced Policy Shifts via Gated On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21601)
*Bingxi Hou,Guochao Jiang,Guofeng Quan,Weiqing Li,Wenfeng Feng,Guohua Liu,Yuewei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Waypoint-OPD：利用同一 RL 运行中的中间教师检查点，比较早期与晚期策略偏移在相对动作偏好上的方向一致性，据此对端点偏移进行门控，抑制方向相反的偏移，实现更可靠的选择性蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略偏移蒸馏只利用教师 RL 前后对数概率差（端点偏移）作为学生轨迹上的监督，但端点偏移无法反映教师训练过程中局部动作偏好是否一致演化；相同的端点偏移可能由早期与晚期偏移相互强化或部分抵消所致，因而监督信号含噪。

Method: Waypoint-OPD 取同一 RL 运行中的中间教师检查点，在学生访问到的前缀处比较早期偏移与晚期偏移在相对动作偏好上的方向；按二者的方向一致程度对端点偏移进行门控，方向相反时抑制该信号。相比仅端点迁移，仅增加一次冻结教师打分前向。

Result: 以 RL 训练的 Qwen3-4B 为教师，Qwen3-8B/14B/30B-A3B 为学生，在数学与代码推理上评估：全部六个领域—学生组合中，Waypoint-OPD 在对比的蒸馏方法里取得最高领域平均分；Qwen3-8B 消融显示移除或打乱门控会降低数学推理性能。

Conclusion: 早期与晚期教师策略偏移之间的方向一致性可作为端点偏移的加权依据，用于学生训练中的选择性迁移，且代价仅为一次额外教师评分。

Abstract: Policy-shift distillation reuses the difference between a teacher's log probabilities before and after reinforcement learning (RL) as supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, this endpoint policy shift cannot reveal whether local action preferences evolved consistently during teacher training. The same endpoint shift can result from early and late shifts that reinforce or partially cancel each other. We investigate whether agreement between early and late teacher policy shifts can guide selective transfer. We introduce Waypoint-guided On-Policy Distillation (Waypoint-OPD), which uses an intermediate teacher checkpoint from the same RL run to compare early and late shifts in relative action preferences at student-visited prefixes. We gate the endpoint shift according to the directional agreement between the early and late shifts, suppressing it when they point in opposing directions. Compared with endpoint-only transfer, our method requires only one additional frozen-teacher scoring pass. Using RL-trained Qwen3-4B teachers, we evaluate our method with Qwen3-8B, Qwen3-14B, and Qwen3-30B-A3B as student models in mathematical and code reasoning. In all six domain--student settings, Waypoint-OPD achieves the highest domain-average score among the compared distillation methods. Ablations with the Qwen3-8B student show that removing or shuffling gates reduces mathematical reasoning performance. These results support the use of directional agreement between early and late teacher policy shifts to weight the endpoint shift during student training.

</details>


### [233] [Market Incentives for AI Safety Investment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21607)
*Nikita Tsoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建上游LLM提供者与多个竞争性下游企业组成的AI供应链博弈模型，比较利润最大化驱动的安全投入与市场福利最优、行业利润最优水平，并分析监管干预的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统带来毒性内容、恶意使用与虚假信息等社会风险，其缓解不仅取决于技术可行性，还取决于开发与部署企业的激励，因此亟需理解利润最大化如何塑造供应链中的安全投入。

Method: 建立博弈论模型：一个上游LLM提供者向多个相互竞争的下游企业供货，推导市场驱动的安全投入水平，并与最大化市场福利、最大化行业利润的基准进行比较；进而分析不同监管干预对参与者激励、利润及模型安全水平的影响。

Result: 下游竞争通常使下游企业的安全投入高于行业利润最优水平；相反，上游提供者因无法充分获取安全投入带来的盈余，可能相对于基准出现投入不足。

Conclusion: 市场驱动的安全投入在供应链上下游呈现方向相反的扭曲（下游过度投入、上游投入不足），不同监管干预会改变各方激励与利润，从而影响最终模型安全水平。

Abstract: Despite their practical significance, modern AI systems pose significant societal risks, including toxicity, malicious use, and misinformation. Their mitigation depends not only on technical feasibility but also on the incentives of firms that develop and deploy these systems. We show how profit maximization shapes safety investment in an AI supply chain with an upstream LLM provider and several competing downstream firms. In our game-theoretic model, we derive the market-driven level of safety investment and compare it with the levels that maximize market welfare and industry profit. We find that downstream competition generally induces downstream firms to invest more in safety than the industry-profit-optimal level. By contrast, the upstream provider may underinvest relative to these benchmarks because it is not fully compensated for the surplus from safety investments. Finally, we analyze how different regulatory interventions affect the participants' incentives and profits, and the resulting levels of model safety.

</details>


### [234] [Local Temporal Contrasts on Human Motion Data Induce a Latent Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21630)
*Lourenço A. Rodrigues,Felix Putze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SemTAC：用时序邻域对比损失训练编码器，无需预测式目标即可从表示空间中恢复序列的几何轨迹结构，在截断序列上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流基于注意力的编码器配合序列级池化，使单向量、逐点比较成为默认推理方式，丢弃了序列在表示空间中划出的轨迹；而多时间尺度结构需在表示空间中保留以供下游使用。

Method: 以时序邻域对比损失训练编码器（SemTAC）：强制表示在局部处处满足时间一致性，从而诱导出连贯的全局结构。不依赖 forecasting 类目标。与四个基线对比：同架构的分类目标、标准对比目标，以及TS-TCC、TS2Vec；并用超参数匹配的消融定位效果来源。

Result: 在五步动作序列判别任务上，全长序列时五者大致相当；在截断的不完整序列上SemTAC优势明显：序列完成前一步的中位F1为0.77，四个基线为0.27–0.70。消融实验表明该效应具体归因于时序对比目标。SemTAC的表示轨迹是五者中唯一呈现可解释几何结构的，在作者提出的位移平滑度指标上超过无结构随机基线。

Conclusion: 证据表明，真正产生时序有序轨迹的是其目标函数，而非单纯的分类准确率；该方法支持在训练未见的时间尺度上进行推理。

Abstract: Time series such as recordings of human motion data often encode structure across multiple temporal scales, and when a latent state determining this sequence evolves, this structure needs to survive in the representation space for downstream use. Yet the currently prevalent attention-based encoders paired with sequence-level pooling have made single-vector, pointwise comparison the default mode of inference, discarding the trajectory a sequence traces through the representation space. We propose recovering this geometric structure directly, without relying on forecasting-based objectives, by training an encoder model (SemTAC) with a temporal-neighborhood contrastive loss: representations that satisfy local consistency everywhere must give rise to a coherent global structure, letting inference happen at temporal scales beyond those seen during training. We evaluate SemTAC against four baselines on a five-step motion sequence discrimination task: the same architecture trained with a classification or a standard contrastive objective, and two established self-supervised time-series methods, TS-TCC and TS2Vec. At the full sequence length all five are broadly competitive, but on incomplete, truncated sequences SemTAC holds a large advantage: a median F1 of 0.77 one step before the sequence completes, against 0.27-0.70 for the four baselines. A hyperparameter-matched ablation attributes this effect specifically to the temporal-contrastive objective. SemTAC's representation trajectories are also the only ones among these five to exhibit interpretable geometric structures, exceeding a no-structure chance baseline on a displacement-smoothness metric which we introduce. This is evidence that its objective, not classification accuracy alone, produces genuinely temporally-ordered trajectories.

</details>


### [235] [ReFlow: Replay-Driven Decoupling for Efficient RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21640)
*Yukun Tian,Junwei Su,Haofeng Xu,Lansong Diao,Zhengping Qian,Weibo Zhao,Wenchao Zhou,Ruiting Zhou,Chuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReFlow：面向LLM强化学习后训练的系统-算法协同设计框架，通过近期轨迹经验回放实现样本复用，配合双回放数据循环、弹性资源池与自适应调度器，训练吞吐最高提升2.06倍且模型性能不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM RL后训练效率是主要瓶颈。现有系统依赖异步执行重叠rollout生成与策略优化，但存在固有矛盾：轻度策略陈旧（policy staleness）带来的训练效率提升有限，而激进异步又会损害模型质量且效率增益递减。此外，LLM RL与多轮agentic负载高度动态，资源需求随时间变化，静态资源分配与生成策略效率低下。

Method: 系统-算法协同设计。核心洞察：不同于现有框架对每条轨迹仅使用一次，ReFlow对近期轨迹进行经验回放以复用样本，摊薄rollout生成的高成本而不过度引入策略陈旧。为此采用解耦自适应架构，三项关键设计：（1）双回放数据循环（dual replay data cycle），支持高效经验复用并平衡训练吞吐与优化稳定性；（2）弹性资源池（elastic resource pool），动态资源放置以适配演化的RL负载；（3）自适应调度器（adaptive scheduler），协同生成策略与资源分配，提升资源利用率与生成吞吐。

Result: 在代表性单轮推理与多轮agentic RL任务上，ReFlow相较最先进的同步与异步RL系统训练吞吐最高提升2.06倍，同时保持相当或更优的模型性能。

Conclusion: 经验回放式样本复用是兼顾RL训练效率与策略新鲜度的有效途径；解耦自适应的系统架构能应对LLM RL负载的动态性，实现吞吐与质量的良好权衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has been a critical paradigm for improving large language model (LLM) reasoning and agentic capabilities, but its efficiency remains a major bottleneck. Existing RL systems commonly rely on asynchronous execution to overlap rollout generation with policy optimization for enhanced efficiency. However, this approach faces inherent limitations: mild policy staleness offers only limited training efficiency gains, whereas aggressive asynchrony can degrade model quality and yields diminishing efficiency gains. The dynamic nature of LLM RL and multi-turn agentic workloads further challenges RL system design, as resource demands shift over time, making static resource allocation and generation strategy inefficient.
  This paper presents ReFlow, a system-algorithm co-designed RL framework for efficient LLM post-training. Its key insight is that, unlike existing RL frameworks which consume each trajectory only once, experience replay of recent trajectories enables effective sample reuse, thereby amortizing the high cost of rollout generation without incurring excessive policy staleness. To fully exploit this opportunity, ReFlow adopts a decoupled and adaptive architecture with three key designs: a dual replay data cycle supports efficient experience reuse while balancing training throughput and optimization stability; an elastic resource pool enables dynamic resource placement to accommodate evolving RL workloads; an adaptive scheduler coordinates generation strategies and resource allocation to improve resource utilization and boost generation throughput. Experiments across representative single-turn reasoning and multi-turn agentic RL tasks show that ReFlow improves training throughput by up to $2.06\times$ over state-of-the-art synchronous and asynchronous RL systems, while maintaining comparable or better model performance.

</details>


### [236] [Efficient Pairwise Difference Learning: A Comparative Study of Encoding Strategies and Training Pair Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21644)
*Bernard Georges,Mohamed Karim Belaid,Manuel Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作系统研究成对差异学习分类（PDC）中输入表示与配对选择两类关键设计选择，提出三元素绝对差编码器并给出经验最佳配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDC通过二分类相似性模型将训练样本从n扩充到O(n²)，适合低数据场景，但输入表示与配对选择策略未明确，缺乏系统性指导。

Method: 在近200个基准分类数据集上，比较四种特征编码器（成对特征、相对差三元素、绝对差、新型绝对差三元素φ_3dabs）与四种配对选择策略（穷举到核稀疏化），基模型为决策树、多层感知机、高斯朴素贝叶斯与支持向量分类器。

Result: φ_3dabs平均F1最高；仅用上三角配对可降低约40%训练时间且精度损失可忽略；核稀疏化选择无精度收益却显著增加计算开销；PDC配置在94%的案例中优于基模型，但无单一配置普遍最优。

Conclusion: 推荐采用绝对差三元素编码器与上三角配对以兼顾精度与效率，核稀疏化选择应避免；数据集特定配置优化仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Pairwise Difference Learning for Classification (PDC) enhances supervised learning in low-data regimes by training a binary similarity classifier on pairs of instances rather than individual samples, effectively inflating the training set from $n$ to $O(n^2)$ examples. However, the PDC framework leaves critical implementation choices underspecified, particularly regarding input representation and pair selection strategies. This work systematically investigates these design decisions through extensive empirical evaluation. We compare four feature encoders (paired features, triplet with relative difference, absolute difference, and a novel triplet with absolute difference) alongside four pair selection strategies ranging from exhaustive to kernel-thinning-based schemes. Experiments across nearly 200 benchmark classification datasets using Decision Tree, Multilayer Perceptron, Gaussian Naive Bayes, and Support Vector Classifier as base models reveal that the proposed triplet with absolute difference encoder ($φ_{\mathrm{3dabs}}$) achieves the highest average F1 scores. More notably, restricting training to upper-triangle pairs reduces computational time by approximately 40% with negligible accuracy loss. Kernel thinning-based selection provided no accuracy benefits while substantially increasing computational overhead. Overall, PDC configurations outperformed base models in 94% of tested cases, though no single configuration dominated universally, suggesting dataset-specific optimization remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [237] [Approaching Backpropagation Accuracy with Weight Perturbation on MNIST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21665)
*Patrick Inoue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证考察权重扰动(WP)在MNIST全连接网络上的可达精度，受批大小、训练时长与估计器稳定化影响，并结合有限时域近似论证，指出"接近BP精度"是配置与预算依赖的可能性，而非效率等价或跨深度的无条件精度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WP仅用随机参数扰动与标量损失变化估计学习方向，无需反向传播导数，但其精度上界、效率与深度可扩展性尚不清楚，需厘清经验表现与理论保证的边界。

Method: 在全连接MNIST网络上系统扫描批大小、训练轮数与估计器稳定化手段（学习率衰减、独立扰动更新平均）；并将经验结果与有限时域近似论证区分开：在给定正则条件下证明种群WP可在任意固定有限深度下任意逼近固定梯度下降轨迹，进而由正分类间隔以任意高概率保持有限测试集上的参考预测。

Result: 浅层配置大batch下WP测试精度约97%，BP参考约97.5–98%；十轮预算下WP仅90.2%，BP达97.4%，存在显著效率差距；批大小超过4,000后收益递减；学习率衰减与独立扰动更新平均可使更深网络稳定，但未达BP水平。

Conclusion: WP接近BP的精度是依赖配置与计算预算的可能性，不能作为效率等同的证据，也不构成跨深度的无条件精度保证。

Abstract: Weight perturbation (WP) estimates learning directions from random parameter perturbations and a scalar change in loss, without backpropagating derivatives. We examine how batch size, training duration, and estimator stabilization affect its attainable accuracy in fully connected MNIST networks.
