Whereas the carrier of the internal state and the fusion mechanism are usually chosen independently in the literature, our main finding is that these two choices are coupled; the deciding factor is where the carrier enters the receiver's computation. Consumability is a property of the receiving computation rather than of the representation. We find that the key-value (KV) cache must be augmented rather than swapped in: the receiver cross-attends over a peer's cache while keeping its own, and with only a small query-side adapter trained, matches the accuracy it gets from reading the source document. Activations invert this. They are best written straight into the receiver, with no bridge, no adapter and no training, which beats exposing them alongside the receiver's own, though that advantage rests on knowing where in the receiver the state belongs. The rule is augment the KV cache, replace the activation, established under one protocol with a shared control ladder. One adapter, trained once, also serves publishers it never trained on, and a pool of several at once, but only those whose internal state lies close to the receiver's own.

</details>


### [272] [Selection Is Not Learning: Choosing Model Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22029)
*Haoyi Yang,Kshitij Mishra,Nils Lukas,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出自进化语言模型循环中存在"选择—更新鸿沟"，并提出 HeirCommit 与 EvoHeir 框架，用新鲜评测问题决定是否继承更新模型，并将参数继承与经验继承解耦，从而减少有害更新并提升数学与通用推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化循环（R-Zero、Agent0、Skill Self-Play）以"任务是否可学习"作为继承依据，默认继承所有由此产生的更新。然而在已验证任务上的多数更新并未提高正确答案概率，因此存在"任务看起来可学习但更新无益"的选择—更新鸿沟，导致有害更新被无差别继承。

Method: 提出 HeirCommit：冻结更新后的模型，用新一批评测问题判定是否继承该模型或保留当前模型。进一步提出 EvoHeir：将参数继承与经验继承分离，利用当前与归档经验修复失败、保留成功，再进行最终确认。理论分析刻画选择诱导的乐观偏差，并将遗憾分解为选择误差、吸收误差与确认误差。

Result: 在相同接受率下，HeirCommit 将有害更新占比相对随机继承降低 68–83%。在四个骨干模型上，EvoHeir 取得最佳数学与通用推理平均成绩，数学平均较最强基线 Agent0 提升 5.3%。

Conclusion: 继承决策应显式验证而非默认接受，且经验可与产生它的模型解耦独立留存，使有用经验在模型未被继承时仍能延续。

Abstract: Self-evolving language models train on tasks they generate, so every round must decide which updated model the next round inherits. Current loops such as R-Zero, Agent0, and Skill Self-Play make this decision through the task, admitting tasks that look learnable and inheriting every resulting update. We identify a selection-to-update gap in these loops, where a task that looks learnable need not produce a useful update. Most updates on verified tasks fail to make correct answers more likely, yet these loops inherit them all. HeirCommit freezes the updated model and uses fresh evaluation questions to decide whether to inherit it or retain the current model. EvoHeir separates parameter inheritance from experience inheritance, using current and archived experience to repair failures and preserve successes before final confirmation. Our analysis characterizes selection-induced optimism and decomposes regret into selection, absorption, and confirmation errors. HeirCommit cuts the harmful share of accepted updates by 68-83% relative to random inheritance at the same acceptance rate. Across four backbones, EvoHeir achieves the best mathematics and general-reasoning averages, raising the math average by 5.3% relative to the strongest baseline, Agent0. The framework lets useful experience persist even when the model that produced it is not inherited.

</details>


### [273] [Momentum, Commentary Tone, and Earnings Surprise in LLM Financial Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22043)
*Alina Khaybullina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过合成收益公告的受控实验，测量六个指令/对话检查点在财务预测中对动量、评论文本语气与实际EPS意外三类输入的依赖程度，发现语气与动量的影响普遍超过EPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在回答"哪些输入驱动语言模型的财务预测"这一问题，即揭示模型在财务预测任务中对不同信息维度的可观测依赖，而非检验其内在推理机制或收益可预测性。

Method: 构造合成收益公告，独立操纵三类因素：20日动量反转、非信息性评论语气、以及固定实际EPS是否超过被操纵的共识预期。选用来自四个模型家族、参数量0.49–7.61B的六个指令/对话检查点，在直接作答Q8_0协议下每个检查点输入2,016条提示。设计涵盖24个数值区块、6组评论文本对、盈利与亏损、年度EPS上升与下降、两种意外幅度；A/B答案分配与U/D方向覆盖每个转换的全部384个主场景。采用条件答案前缀分数的匹配变化进行估计，而非校准后的市场概率。

Result: 主EPS对比在检查点间范围为-0.19至86.80个百分点；主符号化语气对比的幅度在6个模型中有5个超过EPS，动量对比在5个模型中超过EPS；自然语言数值诊断准确率为51.0%–95.8%。答案编码、措辞、上下文与转换控制对结果构成限定。

Conclusion: 实验测量的是可观测的输入依赖与数值识别能力，既不确立内部推理机制，也不证明实际收益可预测性；研究同时公开基准提示、记录分数与交互式结果浏览器。

Abstract: Which inputs drive a language model's financial forecast? We construct synthetic earnings announcements that independently reverse 20-day momentum, non-informational commentary tone, and whether fixed actual EPS beats a manipulated consensus. Six instruction/chat checkpoint conversions from four families (0.49-7.61B base parameters) receive 2,016 prompts each under a direct-answer Q8_0 protocol. The design includes 24 numerical blocks, six commentary pairs, profits and losses, rising and falling annual EPS, and two surprise sizes. Both A/B answer assignments and U/D cover all 384 primary scenarios per conversion. We estimate matched changes in conditional answer-prefix scores, not calibrated market probabilities. Across these checkpoints, the primary EPS contrast ranges from -0.19 to 86.80 percentage points. The magnitude of the primary signed tone contrast exceeds that of EPS in 5 of six models, and momentum exceeds it in 5. Natural-word numerical diagnostic accuracy ranges from 51.0 to 95.8 percent. Answer-encoding, wording, context, and conversion controls qualify these results. The experiment measures observable input reliance and numerical recognition; it establishes neither an internal reasoning mechanism nor realized return predictability. Public benchmark prompts, recorded scores, and an interactive recorded-results explorer accompany the study.

</details>


### [274] [Effectiveness of Hidden-State Probes for Localizing Reasoning Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22046)
*Shubham Subhnil,Yannis Belkhiter,Erik Miehling,Adam Dahlgren Lindström,Elizabeth M. Daly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 隐藏状态探针能判断推理答案错误，但其对失败类型的排序依赖训练设计，且定位失败步骤不如基于轨迹长度的规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最终答案校验只能判断推理轨迹错误，无法区分三种情况：超出模型能力、重试可成功、单步导致不可恢复失败。标准隐藏状态探针以最终答案正确性为标签，混淆了这些情况。

Method: 用蒙特卡洛回滚区分失败类型并定位失败步骤（增加推理成本）；在4B与8B推理模型的隐藏状态上训练探针，检测失败类型与失败步骤，并与不使用隐藏状态的最强基线比较；比较不同训练设计的探针在同一状态上的表现。

Result: 在8B错误轨迹上，校准后的置信度探针（中点阈值）漏掉81%的单步不可恢复失败和47%的重试可成功失败；不同训练设计的探针在相同状态上反转了这两类失败的排序。两种规模下，没有探针在定位失败步骤上优于仅基于轨迹长度的规则；4B规模下，专门预测失败步骤的探针在10次相同轨迹运行中均未超越该规则。

Conclusion: 置信度探针可指示答案错误，但其失败类型排序取决于训练设计；隐藏状态未能提供优于简单长度规则的失败步骤定位能力。

Abstract: Verifying the final answer of a long reasoning trace may show that the trace is wrong but it does not distinguish three cases. The problem may lie beyond the model's capability, another attempt would succeed, or one step in an otherwise recoverable trace made the trace fail. Standard hidden-state probes are trained on final-answer correctness, a label that conflates these cases. Monte Carlo rollouts distinguish the cases and locate the failing step at additional inference cost. We test the hidden states of 4B and 8B reasoning models for the failure type and the failing step and compare each probe with the strongest baseline that uses no hidden states. On incorrect 8B traces, the confidence probe, a calibrated correctness probe at a midpoint cutoff, misses 81% of cases in which a single step makes the trace unrecoverable and 47% of cases in which another attempt would succeed. A probe with a different training design over the same states reverses the ordering of these two kinds of failure, so either ordering can be read from those states. At both scales, no single tested probe locates the failing step more accurately than a rule based only on trace length, and at 4B a probe trained to predict that step fails to outperform this rule in any of 10 runs on identical traces. The confidence probe therefore indicates that an answer is wrong, but its failure-type ordering depends on its training design.

</details>


### [275] [Decoding Naturalistic Speech from fMRI with Temporal Modeling and Multimodal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22049)
*Corentin Caris,Louis Jalouzot,Nona Rajabi,Géraud Ilinca,Ahmed Hassayoune,Asilbek Muminov,Thaddée Delebarre,Clara Fonteneau,Charles Maussion,Bertrand Thirion,Alexis Thual,Antoine Collas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NOEMA是一种时间卷积解码器，通过将连续fMRI窗口与多模态刺激嵌入（文本、音频、语音）对齐，从脑活动中检索自然语音片段，在2000个候选中top-10检索准确率超过42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从脑活动重建感知内容是检验神经记录是否携带有意义信息的严格测试。自然语音跨越多个时间尺度，是理想测试平台，但fMRI测量噪声大、时间模糊，难以恢复其时间结构。

Method: 提出NOEMA：时间卷积解码器，将连续fMRI测量窗口与多模态刺激嵌入对齐；嵌入融合同一刺激片段的文本、音频和语音表示，从而可直接从脑活动检索对应片段。在两个数据集（Cephalonauts One、LeBel）上评估，并进行控制消融实验。

Result: 在2000个候选中，对超过42%的留出查询其正确片段检索位于top-10；显著优于基线解码器，且在加入数千个训练中未出现的干扰项扩大候选池时优势依然保持。消融实验证实时间建模与互补刺激表示的贡献。

Conclusion: 结果表明fMRI活动携带可检索的、跨时间尺度的自然语音信息，时间卷积建模与多模态嵌入组合对解码至关重要。

Abstract: Reconstructing what a person perceives from their brain activity is a stringent test of whether neural recordings carry meaningful information. Naturalistic speech offers a rich testbed for this challenge: it unfolds over multiple timescales, but fMRI's noisy, temporally smeared measurements make this structure hard to recover. We introduce NOEMA, a temporal convolutional decoder that aligns windows of consecutive fMRI measurements with multimodal stimulus embeddings. These embeddings combine complementary text, audio, and speech representations of the same stimulus segment, enabling its retrieval directly from brain activity. On two naturalistic speech datasets, Cephalonauts One and LeBel, NOEMA retrieves the correct segment among 2,000 candidates within the top 10 for more than 42% of held-out queries. This substantially outperforms baseline decoders, an advantage that persists when the candidate pool is extended with thousands of distractors absent from training. Controlled ablations establish the contributions of temporal modeling and complementary stimulus representations.

</details>


### [276] [ABNAT: Attention-Based Noise-Aware Training for Robust Transformers on Analog Compute-in-Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22065)
*Wenshuai Yao,Wenyong Zhou,Hanyong Shao,Zhiyuan Ning,Yizhe Chen,Yuannuo Feng,Kechao Tang,Ru Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ABNAT：基于注意力蒸馏的噪声感知训练框架，让含噪CIM学生模型对齐干净教师模型的注意力路由，从而提升Transformer在模拟存内计算噪声下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer计算与数据搬运能耗高，模拟存内计算（CIM）虽节能但硬件非理想性会扰动权重、显著降低模型精度。现有噪声感知训练（NAT）仅在训练中注入噪声，忽视了注意力机制在推理中的关键作用。

Method: 先分析CIM噪声如何改变Transformer注意力，得出两点观察：深层中干净与含噪注意力图的偏差显著增大；常规NAT即使注意力路由严重偏离干净参考仍保持过度自信。据此提出ABNAT：以干净教师模型为参照，训练含噪学生模型保留逐头（headwise）注意力路由而非仅拟合输出答案；无需硬件改动、无推理开销，并提供降低训练成本的选择性变体。

Result: 在视觉、语言、多模态Transformer上的大量实验表明，ABNAT相较对应NAT基线一致提升鲁棒性，噪声导致下降的精度最多提升11.28%，注意力偏差最多降低85.77%。

Conclusion: 注意力层面的监督是提升CIM噪声鲁棒性的有效且低成本手段，可泛化于多模态Transformer架构，并可与选择性策略结合以节省训练开销。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have become the backbone of a wide range of tasks, but their intensive computation and data movement incur substantial energy overhead. Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) offers an energy-efficient alternative, yet its inherent hardware non-idealities perturb stored weights and can significantly degrade model performance. Although existing noise-aware training (NAT) methods improve robustness by injecting noise during training, they largely overlook the critical role of attention mechanisms in Transformer inference.To bridge this gap, we analyze how CIM-induced noise reshapes attention in Transformers and identify two key observations: (1) the divergence between clean and noisy attention maps increases substantially in deeper layers, and (2) conventional NAT can remain overconfident even when its attention routes deviate markedly from the clean reference. Motivated by these findings, we propose ABNAT, an attention-based noise-aware training framework that teaches the noisy student to preserve the clean teacher's headwise attention routes rather than merely reproduce answers, without hardware changes or inference overhead; selective variants further reduce training cost.Extensive experiments across vision, language, and multimodal Transformers show that ABNAT consistently improves robustness over corresponding NAT baselines, improving noise-degraded accuracy by up to 11.28% and reducing attention deviation by up to 85.77%.

</details>


### [277] [Can AI Forecast the Extremes? Evaluating Probabilistic Weather Models on Tropical Cyclones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22077)
*Nathan Chalumeau,Xavier Renard,Remi Meynadier,Hugo Rakotoarimanga,Marcin Detyniecki,Emmanuel de Bezenac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对 GenCast、FourCastNet 3、FGN 与 ECMWF IFS-ENS 进行热带气旋概率预报的广泛基准评测，发现领先 AI 模型在路径追踪上显著优于 IFS-ENS，但在强度与集合离散度上仍有局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 天气模型在平均意义上超越数值预报且计算成本更低，但其在极端事件上的能力仍有争议；已有评估多集中于确定性模型与指标，对最新概率模型的极端事件能力认识不足，因此需系统检验。

Method: 构建热带气旋预报基准，比较 GenCast、FourCastNet 3、FGN 与 IFS-ENS；设计一套概率指标与诊断分析，评估气旋追踪、强度偏差、不确定性与刻画风暴结构的风压关系。

Result: 尽管在强度和集合离散度上存在不足，领先 AI 模型在气旋追踪上大幅优于 IFS-ENS：FGN 在 2025 年 90% 的评估风暴上取得更优概率定位评分；分析还揭示各模型特有的失效模式，并提示预训练数据的持续影响。

Conclusion: AI 模型能够捕捉在典型天气条件之外仍具预测力的物理关系，值得进一步研究这些能力的形成机制并加以强化，同时针对强度、离散度等短板做定向改进。

Abstract: Weather prediction is undergoing an important shift, with data-driven AI models outperforming leading numerical weather prediction systems on average at a fraction of the computational cost. Yet, whether this success extends to extreme events remains contested. Earlier evaluations have exposed important shortcomings, but have largely focused on deterministic models and metrics, leaving the capabilities of the latest probabilistic models less understood. We conduct an extensive benchmark of tropical cyclone forecasting, comparing GenCast, FourCastNet 3, and FGN with ECMWF's operational physics-based ensemble, IFS-ENS. We develop a suite of probabilistic metrics and diagnostic analyses to assess cyclone tracking, intensity biases, uncertainty, and the wind-pressure relationship that characterizes storm structure. We find that, despite remaining limitations in intensity and ensemble spread, leading AI models substantially outperform IFS-ENS in cyclone tracking: FGN achieves better probabilistic location scores for 90% of evaluated storms in 2025. Further analyses reveal model-specific failure modes and suggest a lingering influence of pretraining data, pointing to opportunities for targeted improvement. Nonetheless, these results suggest that AI models are capable of capturing physical relationships that remain predictive well beyond typical weather conditions, motivating further investigation into how these capabilities emerge and how to strengthen them.

</details>


### [278] [TIPS: A Tiny Inference-time Policy for Scaling in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22099)
*Ya-Shi Zhang,Jian Tang,Pierre-André Noël,Oleksiy Ostapenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出约60万参数的推理期控制器TIPS，在冻结主干语言模型的前提下学习掩码扩散模型中的去掩码与重掩码策略，在GSM8K与MATH-500上显著提升精度-计算权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型的推理期存在"每轮去噪后哪些位置可见"的选择问题；现有解码方法多依赖手工规则，或要求可见集单调增长，缺乏对"何时揭示、何时重新考虑"的学习式控制。

Method: 设计约600K参数的轻量控制器：使用可见性感知的置信度特征与浅层Transformer选择可见token，由固定调度控制期望可见比例；允许去掩码与重掩码并存；训练采用监督预热+基于终止正确性奖励的强化学习；主干解码器保持冻结。

Result: 在LLaDA-8B-Instruct上，32次解码评估预算下GSM8K与MATH-500的微平均准确率：full-canvas变体58.46%、two-block变体57.97%，分别超过最强基线12.1与11.6个百分点；仅在32次预算训练，更高预算下无需重训仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 学习"何时揭示与重新审视token"可在不更新解码器主干的情况下改善扩散语言模型的精度-计算权衡。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models expose an inference-time choice: which positions become visible after each denoising pass. Many existing decoding methods use hand-designed rules or require visibility to grow monotonically. We present a $\textbf{T}$iny $\textbf{I}$nference-time $\textbf{P}$olicy for $\textbf{S}$caling ($\textbf{TIPS}$), an approximately $600$K-parameter controller that learns both unmasking and remasking while keeping the underlying language model frozen. TIPS uses visibility-aware confidence features and a shallow transformer to select which tokens should be visible, while a fixed schedule controls the expected visible fraction. We train the controller through supervised warmup followed by reinforcement learning from terminal correctness rewards. On GSM8K and MATH-500 with LLaDA-8B-Instruct, the full-canvas and two-block TIPS variants achieve $58.46\%$ and $57.97\%$ micro-averaged accuracy at a budget of $32$ decoder evaluations, exceeding the strongest baseline in our main comparison by $12.1$ and $11.6$ percentage points, respectively. Both variants are trained at a budget of $32$ evaluations and remain competitive at higher budgets without retraining. These results demonstrate that learning when to reveal and reconsider tokens can improve the accuracy-compute trade-off without updating the decoder backbone.

</details>


### [279] [Scene Reconstruction Using Ubiquitous Wireless Signals via Diffusion Posterior Inference Over physics-Grounded Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22120)
*Vahid Yazdnian,Yasaman Ghasempour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分层框架将无线信道测量反演为建筑占据图：先用遵守传播物理的无线基础模型提取场景结构表征，再用扩散模型对多测量做联合后验推断与采样，以样本共识得到重建结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机、LiDAR等传感器在黑暗、恶劣天气或遮挡（墙后）场景下会失效，而无线信号不受这些限制；每个基站—用户链路都受途经建筑的反射、遮挡塑造，信道中隐含场景信息。但由信道映射到环境是严重病态问题：单次测量只记录传播路径上与之交互的结构，且同一观测常可由多种合理场景解释，故需以概率方式刻画不确定性。

Method: 将无线场景重建形式化为后验推断，构建两级互补的层次化框架：（1）预训练的无线基础模型，遵循传播物理，将每条信道测量编码为塑造该观测的场景结构表征；（2）扩散模型联合整合多条测量的表征，建模并采样建筑占据的后验分布，通过对样本求共识得到最终重建。

Result: 仅利用无线信道测量即可准确恢复建筑轮廓（footprint）；消融显示性能关键依赖两个设计要素：物理约束的表征能显露出场景相关信息，联合后验推断可一致地整合互补观测。

Conclusion: 无线信道可作为被动、穿透性强的场景感知来源；"物理基础模型表征 + 扩散后验联合推断"的组合是应对病态逆问题的有效范式，两个组件缺一不可。

Abstract: Knowing the layout of the physical world matters for many applications, yet the sensors we rely on for it (e.g., cameras and LiDAR) can fail in darkness, in adverse weather, or behind walls. Wireless signals do not face these limitations: every link between a base station and a user is shaped by the buildings it passes, reflects from, or is blocked by, leaving information about the surrounding scene embedded in the wireless channel measured at the endpoints. However, mapping channel measurements to the environment is a severely ill-posed problem: a measurement records only the structures that interact with the transmitted electromagnetic waves along its propagation paths, and even these observations can often be explained by multiple plausible scenes. We therefore formulate wireless scene reconstruction as posterior inference and develop a hierarchical framework with two complementary stages to approach this problem. A wireless foundation model, pretrained to respect propagation physics, transforms each channel measurement into a representation of the scene structure that shaped the observation. A diffusion model then jointly integrates representations from multiple measurements to model and sample the posterior over building occupancy, with consensus over samples producing the final reconstruction. We demonstrate that the framework can accurately recover building footprints from wireless channel measurements alone and show that its performance depends critically on both design components: physics-grounded representations expose scene-relevant information, while joint posterior inference coherently integrates complementary observations.

</details>


### [280] [Beyond Fixed Acquisition Functions: Temporal Difference Active Learning for Perturbation Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22122)
*Xudong Zhang,Jianzhe Li,Yitong Tang,Peijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TDAL：将预算受限的扰动发现建模为序贯决策问题，用时序差分学习在线学习采集函数，配合Hellinger距离加权与leave-batch-out虚拟转移，在8个公开筛选任务上十一方法中平均排名1.63。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗传与药物扰动筛选有助揭示疾病机制、验证靶点，但实验成本限制了可测试候选数量。现有方法多用反馈更新代理模型却固定采集函数，难以自适应不同响应景观；而实验反馈既揭示当前命中，又提供"如何选实验"的学习信号。

Method: 将预算受限扰动发现形式化为序贯决策问题，提出Temporal Difference Active Learning（TDAL），从单条筛选轨迹出发用时序差分学习在线学习采集函数，兼顾当前命中与未来发现；以实验前后未测候选命中概率的Hellinger距离对后继价值加权；并用leave-batch-out重用已测结果构造虚拟转移以缓解训练数据稀缺。

Result: 在覆盖单、双、三扰动的8个公开筛选任务（多种实验配置）上，TDAL在11种方法中平均排名1.63，并在4个任务上位列第一，体现跨响应景观的适应性。

Conclusion: TDAL表明可从单条筛选轨迹中在线学习采集函数，将主动学习与强化学习结合，克服固定采集函数的局限与数据稀缺问题，在扰动筛选中具备稳定且领先的表现。

Abstract: Genetic and drug perturbation screens are important tools for elucidating disease mechanisms and validating therapeutic targets, but experimental costs limit the number of candidates that can be tested. Existing perturbation screening methods typically update the surrogate model with feedback while keeping the acquisition function fixed, limiting their ability to adapt selection to different response landscapes. Experimental feedback reveals current hits and informs subsequent selection by updating candidate predictions, providing a training signal for learning how to select experiments. We formulate budgeted perturbation discovery as a sequential decision problem and propose Temporal Difference Active Learning (TDAL), which learns an acquisition function online through temporal difference learning from a single screening trajectory, accounting for current hits and future discoveries. TDAL uses the Hellinger distance between hit probabilities for unmeasured candidates before and after an experiment to weight successor value, and reuses measured outcomes through leave-batch-out (LBO) to construct virtual transitions and alleviate training data scarcity. Across eight public screens spanning single, double, and triple perturbations under varied experimental configurations, TDAL achieves an average rank of 1.63 among eleven methods and ranks first on four tasks, demonstrating its adaptability across response landscapes.

</details>


### [281] [Causal Integrated Gradients: Scalable Feature Attribution Along Causal Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22126)
*Joran Michiels,Anis Ismail,Maarten De Vos,Alejandro Sifrim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出因果积分梯度（CIG）与因果期望梯度（CEG），将积分路径沿因果图路由以嵌入特征的先验因果结构，在保持计算效率的同时恢复上游驱动因子，并在合成基准、Dyngen单细胞模拟与真实干扰素-β数据上优于DeepSHAP与IG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有考虑特征依赖的归因方法难以扩展到高维；而已知的特征间因果结构（如转录因子调控靶基因表达）无法被现有梯度类方法利用，导致把下游效应归因于结果变量本身，例如把靶基因排在调控它的转录因子之前。

Method: CIG/CEG扩展积分梯度：将积分路径沿因果图路由，把每个特征的归因分解为直接效应与经其因果后代的介导效应；在因果图满足一定条件时保留IG的计算效率与公理化保证。

Result: 三级评估：（1）已知因果模型的合成基准上，CIG归因与Pearl do-演算的真值因果效应最吻合，精度与causal Shapley值相当而计算成本仅为其一小部分；（2）已知基因调控网络的Dyngen单细胞模拟数据上，最准确恢复驱动模拟的转录因子；（3）真实干扰素-β刺激数据（仅有近似因果图）上，比DeepSHAP与IG更好地优先排序驱动响应的转录因子。

Conclusion: 将因果结构嵌入梯度归因可恢复现有方法遗漏的上游驱动因子，且不牺牲可扩展性。

Abstract: Existing feature attribution methods that account for feature dependencies do not scale to high dimensions. We propose causal integrated gradients (CIG) and causal expected gradients (CEG), which extend integrated gradients to incorporate prior causal structure among input features while retaining its computational efficiency and, under conditions on the causal graph, its axiomatic guarantees. In many settings, some causal structure between features is already known, for example, that a transcription factor causally regulates a target gene's expression. Yet existing gradient-based methods cannot exploit this structure, instead crediting a feature's downstream effects over its true upstream cause, such as ranking the target gene above the transcription factor that regulates it. Our key idea is to embed this causal structure by routing the integration path along the causal graph. The method thereby decomposes each feature's attribution into a direct effect and effects mediated through its causal descendants. We evaluate CIG in three settings of increasing realism. First, on a synthetic benchmark with a known causal model, CIG's attributions best match the ground-truth causal effect from Pearl's do-calculus, matching the accuracy of causal Shapley values at a fraction of the computational cost. Second, on simulated single-cell gene expression data with a known gene regulatory network (Dyngen), CIG most accurately recovers the transcription factors driving the simulation. Third, on real interferon-$β$ stimulation data, where only an approximate causal graph is available, CIG better prioritizes the transcription factors driving the measured response than DeepSHAP and integrated gradients. Together, these results show that incorporating causal structure into gradient-based attributions recovers upstream drivers that existing methods miss, without sacrificing scalability.

</details>


### [282] [Specification Gaming and a Constrained Reinforcement Learning Mitigation in a Cleaning Gridworld](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22152)
*Hilda Adwubi Osei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 构建一个清洁机器人网格世界，其中智能体可通过"隐藏污渍"欺骗廉价传感器以刷高代理奖励；标准DQN完全利用该漏洞。作者提出约束强化学习，惩罚特定投机行为，消除游戏化策略并大幅恢复真实目标表现，同时给出代理奖励、真实奖励与代价信号的精确恒等式，并在硬约束变体下提供确定性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范游戏（specification gaming）现有证据多为观察性，缺乏"受控构造的代理/真实目标差距"与"带可验证保证的缓解方法"相结合的系统演示，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 设计最小化网格世界（清洁机器人可真除污或向廉价传感器隐藏污渍）；用标准深度Q网络仅依据传感器代理奖励训练；再引入约束强化学习，对利用差距的具体行为施加代价惩罚；推导并实证验证代理奖励、真实奖励与代价信号之间的精确核算恒等式，并考察硬约束变体。

Result: DQN收敛到完全游戏化策略：代理奖励接近最大，但真实目标从未达成；约束方法在最终策略中消除游戏行为，并显著恢复真实目标性能；恒等式经验验证表明代价信号精确度量而非仅追踪代理/真实差距，硬约束变体下给出确定性保证。

Conclusion: 受控环境证明规范游戏可被标准深度RL完全触发，而针对性的约束RL结合精确代价核算可消除该行为并提供可验证保证，为从经验性缓解走向形式化保障提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents optimize exactly the reward signal they are given, which can diverge sharply from an intended true objective when that signal is only a proxy for it; this failure mode is known as specification gaming. Existing evidence for this failure mode is largely observational, with comparatively few demonstrations pairing a controlled, deliberately constructed proxy/true gap with a mitigation that carries a verifiable guarantee rather than only an empirical improvement. We construct a minimal gridworld in which a cleaning-robot agent can either genuinely remove dirt or conceal it from a cheap sensor, and show that a standard deep Q-network trained only on the sensor's proxy reward converges to a policy that games it completely, reaching near-maximal proxy reward while never achieving the true objective. We then introduce a constrained reinforcement learning fix, penalizing the specific behaviors through which the agent exploits the gap, and show that it eliminates the gaming behavior from the final policy while substantially recovering true-objective performance. We further derive and empirically verify an exact accounting identity relating the proxy reward, true reward, and our cost signal for this environment, showing that the cost signal measures the proxy/true gap exactly rather than merely tracking it, and yields a deterministic guarantee under a hard-constrained variant of the method.

</details>


### [283] [The importance of tensor handling and implementation of physics-informed DeepONets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22157)
*Nahum Banks,Gustavo A. M. de Almeida,Sergio Maldonado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种改进的PI-DeepONet架构，通过消除Branch网络在配点上处理输入函数的冗余，在保持数学等价与精度的同时显著降低训练开销，使3D问题训练成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PI-DeepONet虽可学习解算子并跨问题族快速预测，但其在高维问题上的应用受限于训练的计算成本（时间与内存）。原始架构中Branch网络在配点维度上存在冗余计算。

Method: 设计修改后的PI-DeepONet架构：去除Branch网络跨配点处理输入函数时的冗余，减小输入张量规模并减少Branch网络评估次数；该公式与原架构数学等价。同时对两种架构进行计算复杂度分析，推导训练时间关于配点数与输入函数数的理论缩放律。

Result: 在1D、2D、3D线性对流问题上评估：精度与原PI-DeepONet相当，训练时间持续降低；2D实验最大加速3.75倍，实测训练时间缩放符合对配点数与输入函数的线性依赖预测；3D模型仅在所提架构下可于NVIDIA A100上训练（原架构超内存限制）。

Conclusion: PI-DeepONet的实现方式对其实际性能影响重大；合理的实现优化可显著提升效率并扩展其可处理的物理信息学习问题范围，使原本不可行的高维训练成为可能。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) reduce or eliminate the need for large labelled datasets by enforcing governing equations during training. Physics-informed Deep Operator Networks (PI-DeepONets) extend this approach to learning solution operators, enabling rapid prediction across families of problems. Despite these advantages, we show that their application to higher-dimensional problems remains limited by the computational cost of training. To address this issue, we propose a modified PI-DeepONet architecture that reduces this cost by removing redundancy in how the Branch network processes input functions across collocation points. The proposed formulation is mathematically equivalent to the original architecture but reduces input tensor sizes and the number of Branch network evaluations. We analyse the computational complexity of both architectures and derive theoretical scaling laws for training time with respect to the number of collocation points and input functions. The proposed architecture is evaluated on 1D, 2D, and 3D linear advection problems. In all cases, it achieves comparable predictive accuracy to the original PI-DeepONet while consistently reducing training time. In 2D experiments, a maximum speed-up of $3.75\times$ is observed, with empirical training-time scaling agreeing with the predicted linear dependence on collocation points and input functions. Furthermore, training a 3D model on an NVIDIA A100 GPU is only feasible using the proposed architecture, as the original formulation exceeds memory limits. These results demonstrate that careful consideration of how PI-DeepONets are implemented can yield substantial gains, enhancing the practical performance of PI-DeepONets and enabling their application to otherwise intractable physics-informed learning problems.

</details>


### [284] [LCT: Losslessly Compressed Tensors with GPU friendly execution for Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22179)
*Max Zhu,Bohang Sun,Pietro Lio,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LCT提出面向训练的GPU无损张量压缩执行框架，利用浮点数据冗余与预计算码本的Huffman编码，在训练/微调中最高获得1.455×压缩率并以小幅步时开销换取显著显存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练过程中显存占用大，而无损压缩虽可降低存储却需要张量被反复重新编解码，传统压缩方案的编解码开销难以承受。论文旨在为这种"重复编解码"场景设计专用GPU执行框架。

Method: LCT利用浮点数据的自然冗余压缩权重、激活与优化器状态；采用高度优化的GPU核实现基于预计算码本的Huffman编码，以降低压缩/解压开销；并分析其存储成本及对数值幅度漂移的适应性。

Result: 压缩率最高1.455×。162M参数NanoGPT上显存降低24.6%，步时增加7.2%；Qwen3-4B微调中峰值显存节省最高23.8%，同GPU下支持3.5×更长的上下文长度。

Conclusion: LCT证明了在训练与微调中引入无损压缩是可行且高效的，能以适度计算开销换取可观显存收益，并转化为更大上下文长度等实际训练能力。

Abstract: Lossless tensor compression could reduce memory usage during training, but training requires tensors to be repeatedly re-encoded. We introduce Losslessly Compressed Tensors (LCT), a GPU execution framework designed for this repeated encode-decode regime. LCT exploits the natural redundancy in floating point data to reduce storage for weights, activations and optimizer states. Highly optimized GPU kernels implement Huffman coding with precomputed codebooks for low compression and decompression overhead, essential for training. Our analysis characterizes LCT's storage costs and adaptation to magnitude shifts. LCT achieves a compression ratio of up to $1.455\times$, and is demonstrated in training and finetuning settings. On a 162M-parameter NanoGPT, LCT reduces memory usage by $24.6\%$ for a $7.2\%$ step-time increase. In Qwen3-4B fine-tuning, LCT saves up to $23.8\%$ of peak memory, enabling a $3.5\times$ larger context length in training on the same GPU.

</details>


### [285] [Generalization Guarantees of Randomized Block Coordinate Descent for Nonconvex Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22213)
*Ruixi Hu,Liang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为带非凸目标的随机块坐标下降（RBCD）建立了高概率泛化保证，分析经验风险与总体风险的梯度界，并刻画早停对总体梯度最优收敛率的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RBCD 是大规模机器学习中常用的优化算法，但现有理论分析主要集中于优化视角下的收敛性，对非凸问题的泛化行为理解不足。

Method: 在一般非凸设定下，仅依赖标准光滑性假设，推导经验风险与总体风险梯度的界；采用多项式衰减步长并通过调节迭代次数平衡优化与泛化，量化早停带来的总体梯度最优收敛率。在梯度主导（gradient-dominated）设定下，给出以函数次优性表示的界。针对特定结构的损失函数，进一步给出与维度无关的界。

Result: 一般非凸情形下获得经验与总体风险的梯度界，并通过迭代次数调节使总体梯度达到最优收敛率；梯度主导情形下的界在凸与低噪声设定下几乎匹配乐观速率；对特定结构损失得到维度无关的泛化保证，适用于高维问题。

Conclusion: RBCD 在非凸目标下具有良好的泛化性质，早停可作为平衡优化与泛化的有效手段，且所得保证可推广至高维问题。

Abstract: Randomized block coordinate descent (RBCD) is a popular optimization algorithm for many large-scale machine learning problems. The existing theoretical analysis of RBCD mainly focuses on its convergence behavior from an optimization perspective, while its generalization behavior remains less understood for nonconvex problems. In this paper, we establish high-probability generalization guarantees for RBCD with nonconvex objectives. In the general nonconvex setting, we derive bounds on the gradients of both empirical and population risks, requiring only standard smoothness assumptions. With polynomially decaying step sizes, we tune the iteration number to balance optimization and generalization, thereby quantifying how early stopping leads to the optimal convergence rate for the population gradients. In the gradient-dominated setting, we provide bounds in terms of functional suboptimality, which nearly match the optimistic rates in convex and low-noise settings. Finally, we develop dimension-independent bounds for loss functions of a specific structure, making the generalization guarantees applicable to high-dimensional problems.

</details>


### [286] [OS-Helix: Unifying GUI Interaction and Code Execution in Native Computer-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22234)
*Bowen Yang,Kaiming Jin,Zichen Ding,Zhenyu Wu,Zehao Li,Zhoumianze Liu,Kanzhi Cheng,Fangzhi Xu,Yanan Sun,Qiushi Sun,Yu Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OS-Helix 是一个端到端训练的多模态计算机使用智能体，将 GUI 操作与代码执行统一到单一动作空间，并通过混合数据 SFT 冷启动加在线 RL（Step-balanced GSPO）实现 GUI/代码协调能力的内化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实桌面自动化既非纯视觉也非纯代码：智能体需理解用户界面，同时高效操作文件、脚本与系统状态。但原生的 GUI/代码协调无法通过提示层面的工具组合获得——初步实验显示，简单加入代码工具反而会退化 GUI 智能体，说明这种路由能力必须通过高质量混合数据训练来内化。

Method: 1) 统一动作空间，将 GUI 动作与代码执行纳入同一策略；2) 数据构建：提出 application-artifact composition，将桌面应用与真实文件制品配对以实例化可执行的 Ubuntu 状态，并设计解耦的指令/评估器生成流水线，产生环境接地、且最终状态可跨方案模态验证的任务；3) 先在过滤后的混合数据上做 SFT 冷启动，再在真实桌面环境中进行在线 RL；4) 提出 Step-balanced GSPO，按动作步而非原始助手 token 聚合重要性权重，防止长代码动作在异构动作优化中占据主导。

Result: 在 OSWorld-Verified 上，OS-Helix-27B 与 OS-Helix-35B-A3B 相对同规模 Qwen3.5 混合基线分别取得 35.0% 和 33.7% 的相对提升；OS-Helix-27B-RL 进一步提升 42.9%，达到 66.44% 成功率。消融结果与"学习式 GUI/代码协调、可扩展可验证监督、动作步均衡优化"三方面的贡献一致，并在 WindowsAgentArena 上展示了跨平台迁移能力。

Conclusion: GUI 与代码的协调必须内化为训练目标而非外部提示组合；配合可验证的混合监督与动作步均衡的 RL 优化，可显著提升桌面自动化成功率，并具备跨操作系统平台的泛化潜力。

Abstract: Real desktop automation is neither purely visual nor purely programmatic. Agents must understand user-facing interfaces while efficiently manipulating files, scripts, and system state. However, native GUI/code coordination cannot be obtained by prompt-level tool composition. In our preliminary experiments, naively adding a code tool degrades a GUI agent, revealing that GUI/code routing must be internalized through training on high-quality hybrid data. We present OS-Helix, an end-to-end trained multimodal computer-using agent that unifies GUI actions and code execution in one action space. To construct the required supervision, we introduce application-artifact composition, pairing desktop applications with authentic file artifacts to instantiate executable Ubuntu states, and a decoupled instruction/evaluator generation pipeline that produces environment-grounded tasks whose final states can be verified regardless of the solution modality. We cold-start the policy with SFT on filtered hybrid data and further improve it through online RL in live desktop environments. For RL, we propose Step-balanced GSPO, which aggregates importance weight by action step rather than raw assistant tokens to prevent long code actions from dominating heterogeneous-action optimization. On OSWorld-Verified, OS-Helix-27B and OS-Helix-35B-A3B achieve relative improvements of 35.0% and 33.7% over their matched Qwen3.5 hybrid baselines, while OS-Helix-27B-RL further improves by 42.9% to reach 66.44% success. Ablations provide evidence consistent with contributions from learned GUI/code coordination, scalable verifiable supervision, and action step balanced optimization, while demonstrating cross-platform transfer on WindowsAgentArena. Our project homepage is at https://os-copilot.github.io/OS-Helix/.

</details>


### [287] [Training-Free On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22236)
*Bangji Yang,Jiajun Fan,Zhaoyu Zhu,Hongbo Ma,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练的on-policy蒸馏方法，仅用前向传播即可将多个专精模型的能力整合进单一通用模型，在数学与代码基准上保留大部分专家收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy蒸馏需为每个教师支付rollout、教师前传与梯度步的代价，成本高；作者希望省去训练过程，仅靠前向计算完成专家能力的整合。

Method: 当专家共享同一基座模型时，蒸馏存在精确的逐层解：融合模型的每个线性层需在该专家实际接收的输入上复现其输出（专家读取自身答案时），从而化为最小二乘问题，只需这些输入的二阶矩；对几百条rollout做一次前向即可获得。此外分析了rollout内容质量的重要性，如剔除重复循环导致的截断答案。

Result: 融合模型平均精度高于任一单个专家；剔除重复截断答案后，融合模型在AIME25上从任务算术水平提升到与专家持平（同等精度下）；在Olmo 3 7B上，每个专家数GPU小时的前向计算即可得到在7个数学与代码基准上保留各专家大部分增益的通用模型。

Conclusion: 专家整合无需训练，逐层最小二乘解加少量rollout的前向统计即可实现有效融合；rollout数据的质量比求解器选择更关键。

Abstract: On-policy distillation consolidates specialist models into one student at the price of rollouts, teacher passes and gradient steps for every teacher. We show that the consolidation does not need training: with forward passes only, the capabilities of separately tuned specialists can be aggregated into one model that is, on average, more accurate than either specialist alone. When specialists share a base model, distillation has an exact layer-wise solution: each linear layer of the fused model must reproduce every specialist's output on the inputs that specialist receives while reading its own answers, a least-squares problem whose solution needs only the second moments of those inputs. One forward pass over a few hundred rollouts provides them. We call this training-free on-policy distillation (\method). What the rollouts contain matters more than the solver: answers truncated in repetition loops are few among a mathematics specialist's rollouts but carry a large share of their tokens, and removing them lifts the fused model on AIME25 from task arithmetic's level to the specialist's at matched precision. On Olmo~3 7B, a few GPU-hours of forward computation per specialist yield a generalist that keeps most of each specialist's gain on each of seven mathematics and code benchmarks.

</details>


### [288] [Query-Efficient Rejection Sampling for Parallel Masked Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22238)
*Shiyuan Zhang,Xuheng Li,Qiwei Di,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIRS 用单次随机采样的增量估计对数接受概率，并结合 Bernoulli factory 与 Rao-Blackwell 化实现低成本、低偏差的并行解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDM 并行解掩多个 token 可加速生成，但独立采样忽略 token 间相关性导致输出偏差。Anari 等的拒绝采样虽纠正偏差，却需为每个解掩 token 做一次 score 查询，代价高昂。

Method: 提出 Single-Increment Rejection Sampler：用一次随机采样的增量、仅一次查询估计对数接受概率；将泊松数量级的截断估计通过改编自 Chen 等的 Bernoulli factory 转为接受/拒绝决策；利用每次查询返回块内所有剩余 token 条件分布的特性，对增量做 Rao-Blackwell 化以降低截断。

Result: 理论上证明了数据分布与 SIRS 输出间的 KL 散度被截断项加模型训练误差所界定；在精确 score 下，并行解码需承担完整的块内依赖代价，而 SIRS 仅承担超出截断范围的部分。

Conclusion: SIRS 在保持低偏差的同时显著降低拒绝采样的查询开销，为高效并行解码提供了有理论保证的方案。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) accelerate generation by unmasking multiple tokens in parallel, but sampling these tokens independently ignores their correlations and biases the output. Anari et al. (2026) corrected this bias with rejection sampling, decomposing the log acceptance probability into a sum of token-level terms, but the evaluation requires one score query per unmasked token. We propose the Single-Increment Rejection Sampler (SIRS), which provides an estimate of the log acceptance probability from a single randomly sampled increment at the cost of one query. SIRS then converts a Poisson number of clipped estimates into accept/reject decisions through a Bernoulli factory adapted from Chen et al. (2026). Since each query returns the conditionals of all remaining tokens in the block, we further Rao-Blackwellize each increment by averaging over these tokens at no extra cost, thereby reducing clipping. We show that the KL-divergence between the data distribution and the SIRS output is bounded by a clipping term plus the model's training error. With exact scores, parallel decoding pays the full within-block dependence, whereas SIRS pays only the part beyond the clipping range.

</details>


### [289] [Explore Once, Evaluate Many: Multiple Policy Evaluation for Stochastic Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22252)
*Spyros Dragazis,Yilei Chen,Aldo Pacchiano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究线性奖励结构随机上下文bandit中同时评估K个目标策略的问题，提出PRISMA算法，用共享采样设计与统一岭回归估计器达到精度ε，样本复杂度为Õ((L²S²+β²)(1∨ρ*)/ε²·log K)，且无需二阶矩特征值下界、不显式依赖上下文/动作空间规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略评估方法多针对单一策略，K个策略逐一评估代价高昂；如何设计共享采样分布以复用样本、降低总成本，同时避免对特征二阶矩下界与空间基数的不必要依赖，是本文动机所在。

Method: PRISMA由三部分组成：(1) 无奖励规划（reward-free planning）构造共享采样分布；(2) 正则化采样设计以处理难以共享的实例（用ρ*刻画难度）；(3) 单个岭回归估计器在所有目标策略间共享。并通过自适应倍增流程在未知ρ*的情况下选取正则化尺度；策略查询成本单独核算，每个共享规划上下文需K次查询。

Result: 在概率至少1-δ下，对每个目标策略价值达到ε精度，需Õ((L²S²+β²)(1∨ρ*)/ε²·log K)次上下文抽取与奖励观测，其中ρ*≤d为实例相关的共享采样难度度量，L、S、β分别为特征范数、奖励参数范数与噪声界，隐藏因子仅含对K、d、1/δ的对数项；保证不含二阶矩特征值的数值下界，也不显式依赖上下文与动作空间基数。

Conclusion: 共享采样分布加单一岭回归估计器可让K个策略评估的成本仅随log K增长，避免了逐策略独立评估的线性放大；实例难度由ρ*统一刻画，自适应倍增使算法无需预知ρ*，为多策略离线/在线策略评估提供了具有实用性的理论保证。

Abstract: We study how to evaluate $K$ target policies simultaneously in stochastic contextual bandits with linear reward structure. Given a known $d$-dimensional feature map and reward-free access to policy queries, we propose PRISMA (Policy evaluation through Regularized Information Sharing and Mixture Allocation), an algorithm that estimates every target policy's value to accuracy $\varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$. The algorithm uses $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\!\left( \frac{(L^2S^2+β^2)(1\veeρ^\star)}{\varepsilon^2} \cdot \log(K) \right)$ context draws and reward observations, where $ρ^\star\le d$ is an instance-dependent measure of the difficulty of designing a shared sampling distribution. Here, $L$, $S$, and $β$ bound the feature norm, reward parameter norm, and reward noise, respectively, and the suppressed factors are logarithmic in $K$, $d$, and $1/δ$. Our method combines reward-free planning, a regularized sampling design, and a single ridge regression estimator shared across all target policies. An adaptive doubling procedure selects the regularization scale without knowledge of $ρ^\star$. The guarantees require no numerical lower bound on second-moment eigenvalues and have no explicit dependence on the cardinalities of the context and action spaces. Policy-query costs are accounted for separately, with $K$ queries per shared planning context.

</details>


### [290] [V-SHARE: Provable Convergence Guarantees for Advantage-based Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21448)
*Kintan Saha,Hamed Masoudi,Xinyi Sheng,Dominik Baumann,Joni Pajarinen,Arsenii Mustafin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出V-SHARE：将动作价值分解为状态价值+动作优势，并用重要性加权的Bellman残差校正；首次给出优势类算法的完整理论刻画（渐近收敛+非渐近有限样本），揭示收缩—方差权衡，并给出递减残差预条件调度与双时间尺度分析，实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于优势的价值学习通过跨动作共享信息在实践中有效，但缺乏理论理解：缺少收敛性与有限样本保证，也不清楚其相对标准Q-learning的优劣条件与内在权衡。

Method: V-SHARE算法：优势分解的价值共享 + 重要性加权Bellman残差校正；理论分析包括渐近收敛与非渐近有限样本界，识别缓解异步Q-learning稀有动作瓶颈的区间，刻画确定性收缩强度与重要性加权噪声之间的收缩—方差权衡，据此设计递减的残差预条件调度，并给出去除单时间尺度残差权重阈值的双时间尺度分析；在离散MDP与深度RL基准上验证。

Result: 获得首个优势类算法的完整理论刻画（渐近收敛性与非渐近有限样本保证）；证明在特定区间内V-SHARE缓解稀有动作瓶颈；理论刻画收缩—方差权衡；递减预条件调度兼顾早期快速收缩与低渐近噪声；双时间尺度分析放宽单时间尺度阈值限制；离散MDP与深度RL实验与理论一致。

Conclusion: 优势分解+重要性加权残差校正可带来可证明的样本效率收益，但需按权衡调度预条件权重；该工作为优势类价值学习提供了首个完整理论框架，并为实践中的调度与时间尺度设计提供指导。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning, advantage-based value-learning methods, unlike standard Q-learning, share information across actions by decomposing the action-value function into a state-dependent value component and action-specific advantages. While this mechanism has proven effective in both tabular and deep reinforcement learning, its theoretical understanding remains incomplete. We introduce V-SHARE, an advantage-based value-learning algorithm that combines cross-action value sharing with an importance-weighted Bellman-residual correction. To our knowledge, we provide the first complete theoretical characterization of an advantage-based algorithm, including both asymptotic convergence and non-asymptotic finite-sample guarantees. We identify a regime in which V-SHARE mitigates the rare-action bottleneck of asynchronous Q-learning and uncover a contraction--variance tradeoff between stronger deterministic contraction and increased importance-weighting noise. Motivated by this tradeoff, we develop decreasing residual-preconditioning schedules that combine faster early contraction with lower asymptotic noise. We further develop a two-timescale analysis that removes the single-timescale residual-weight threshold while preserving convergence guarantees. Experiments on discrete Markov decision processes (MDPs) and deep reinforcement-learning benchmarks corroborate our results.

</details>


### [291] [BitSparse: Compact Activation Storage for ReLU Feed-Forward Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22256)
*Max Zhu,Bohang Sun,Pietro Lio,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BitSparse：面向稀疏激活的GPU驻留无损压缩格式，通过融合Triton内核收缩激活缓存并在反向传播精确恢复，降低每token显存约30%、开销≤1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM训练显存压力大。现有手段为activation checkpointing（重算换显存）与有损压缩（改变存储的激活值），而稀疏ReLU/ReLU²激活的复兴提供了第三条路径：对激活缓存本身做无损压缩。

Method: 提出BitSparse，一种GPU驻留的稀疏激活无损压缩格式；压缩与重建由定制Triton内核实现并融合进autograd函数，反向传播前精确还原；支持BF16与FP8激活，可选符号位打包（sign-bit packing）进一步逼近理论极限；并设计预分配缓冲区系统，避免可变尺寸张量分配时的主机同步。

Result: 在Modded-NanoGPT与Nemotron-H上，每token显存分别降低31%与30%，在实际序列长度下开销不超过1%；符号位打包使压缩率接近理论上限，论文同时给出压缩率与理论最优的对比分析。

Conclusion: 利用稀疏激活的可压缩性可实现无损的激活缓存压缩，在不损失精度的前提下显著节省显存且开销可忽略，是一种与checkpointing、有损压缩互补的显存优化方案。

Abstract: Reducing memory use in large language model (LLM) training is an ongoing challenge. Activation checkpointing saves memory by recomputation, while lossy compression changes the stored activations. The reemergence of sparse $\relu$ and $\relu^2$ activations creates a third opportunity: losslessly compressing the activation cache itself. We present BitSparse, a GPU-resident lossless compression format for sparse activations. Compression and reconstruction run in custom Triton kernels fused into an autograd function, shrinking the activation cache while recovering it exactly for the backward pass. Bfloat16 (BF16) and FP8 activations are supported. Optional sign-bit packing further increases compression up to close to the theoretical limit. We analyze its compression ratio and compare it to the theoretical optimum. BitSparse lowers per-token VRAM by 31\% and 30\% Modded-NanoGPT and Nemotron-H respectively, with overheads of no more than 1\% at practical sequence lengths. Additionally, a new preallocated buffer system avoids host synchronization during training when variable size tensors are allocated.

</details>


### [292] [Topology-Inspired Fine-Tuning for Wind-Field Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22262)
*Arjun Dadhwal,Brian Summa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对科学超分辨率模型"逐点重建损失与科学解释需求"之间的张力，提出以可微拓扑启发式辅助损失（梯度幅值监督+固定持久对监督）微调预训练CNN，在168场风场基准上同时改善了瓶颈持久图距离与合并树距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学超分辨率模型通常以逐点重建损失优化，但科学解释依赖极值、连通区域与多尺度标量结构。作者用预训练CNN与GAN风场SR模型刻画这一张力：逐点指标、瓶颈PD距离与MT距离偏向CNN，而二阶Wasserstein PD距离偏向更锐利的GAN——即使同一拓扑描述子的不同距离也奖励不同性质，说明单一逐点损失无法保证拓扑保真。

Method: 在固定168场基准（拓扑在每场固定160×160子区域上评估）上进行微调实验：(1) 对预训练CNN施加可微的拓扑启发式辅助损失，分别作用于标量风速、梯度幅值、软高速水平集与局部极大值；(2) 引入固定对监督，用Topology ToolKit离线定位真值持久对端点，约束端点处预测值与持久性；(3) 开展16组配置的损失研究，每组均与仅用原始重建损失的匹配标准微调对照比较，最终目标融合梯度监督与固定对监督两种信号。

Result: 标量场损失中，梯度监督带来最大的PD改善但不改善平均MT距离；固定对监督在PD与MT上给出最大且最一致的双重改善。最终组合目标相对预训练CNN将平均瓶颈、W_{2,∞}、W_{2,2}距离分别降低31%、25%、27%（相对对照组降低32–35%，而对照组自身使三者均恶化），在168场中150场上三者同时下降，PSNR提升1.30 dB（约为标准微调增益的一半）；平均MT距离亦改善，但仅在其中91场上改善。

Conclusion: 逐点重建指标与拓扑指标、以及不同拓扑距离之间目标并不一致，标准微调甚至会损害拓扑保真。梯度监督与固定对监督具有互补性，组合后可稳定获得PD与MT的联合改善；MT改善场数明显少于PD，表明PD与MT刻画的是互补结构，科学SR评估需多描述子、多度量并用。

Abstract: Scientific super-resolution (SR) models are typically optimized with pointwise reconstruction losses, yet scientific interpretation often hinges on extrema, connected regions, and multiscale scalar structure. We characterize this tension with pretrained CNN and GAN wind-field SR models. Pointwise metrics, bottleneck persistence-diagram (PD) distance, and merge-tree (MT) distance favor the CNN, whereas order-2 Wasserstein PD distances favor the sharper GAN, so even different distances on the same topological descriptor reward different properties. Motivated by this disagreement, we fine-tune the pretrained CNN with differentiable topology-inspired auxiliary losses on scalar speed, gradient magnitude, soft high-speed level sets, and local maxima, and with fixed-pair supervision, which constrains predicted values and persistence at ground-truth persistence-pair endpoints located offline with the Topology ToolKit. A loss study of 16 fine-tuning configurations, each compared with a matched standard fine-tuning control that uses only the original reconstruction loss, on a fixed 168-field benchmark (with topology evaluated on a fixed 160 by 160 subregion of each field), shows that, among the scalar-field losses, gradient supervision provides the strongest PD improvement but does not improve mean MT distance, whereas fixed-pair supervision gives the largest and most consistent joint improvement in PD and MT distances. The final objective combines these two signals. Relative to the pretrained CNN, it lowers mean bottleneck, $W_{2,\infty}$, and $W_{2,2}$ distances by 31%, 25%, and 27% (and by 32-35% relative to the control, which on its own worsens all three), lowers all three on 150 of 168 fields, and gains 1.30 dB PSNR, about half the gain of standard fine-tuning. Mean MT distance also improves, but on only 91 of those 150 fields, indicating that PD and MT capture complementary structure.

</details>


### [293] [Baselines and Score Centering in Policy Gradient Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22263)
*Shuo Han,Ziqing Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从协方差视角重新阐释正则化重要性采样下的策略梯度估计，揭示组均值基线与分数中心化的作用与关系，并给出序列级/词元级重要性采样下应否同时使用二者的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型后训练常用强化学习，但训练常为离策略（训练-推理不匹配、异步训练导致的策略陈旧），需用重要性采样修正；实际中比值常经截断、掩码或裁剪正则化，导致重要性加权策略梯度不再对奖励的常数平移不变，且基线/中心化技术的作用缺乏统一解释。

Method: 以奖励与重要性加权分数之间的协方差作为估计目标，使常数平移不变性在正则化下得以恢复（比值未正则化时与传统策略梯度一致）；分别在序列级和词元级重要性采样下建立该协方差视角，分析组均值基线（样本均值）与分数中心化（总体均值）两种中心化操作对应的不同无偏协方差估计量及其方差。

Result: 组均值基线与分数中心化本质上都是协方差估计中的中心化，但所减均值不同（样本均值 vs 总体均值），因而形成方差不同的无偏估计量；在序列级重要性采样下，已用组均值基线时分数中心化不再带来收益；在词元级重要性采样下，比值被正则化时只用组均值基线而不做分数中心化通常会引入偏差。

Conclusion: 是否同时使用两种中心化取决于重要性采样的层级：序列级无需叠加分数中心化，词元级在正则化条件下需注意分数中心化以避免偏差。协方差视角为策略梯度估计中的基线设计与正则化处理提供了统一的理论依据。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a widely used approach to post-training large language models. In a typical implementation, learning is off-policy: The policy generating the responses differs from the policy being trained because of training-inference mismatch or policy staleness in asynchronous training. This discrepancy is commonly addressed by importance sampling, whose ratio is usually regularized in practice through truncation, masking, or clipping. Under regularized importance sampling, the importance-weighted policy gradient is no longer invariant to constant shifts in the reward. This invariance is recovered by using the covariance between the reward and the importance-weighted score as the estimand, which coincides with the policy gradient when the ratio is not regularized. This note develops this covariance perspective for both sequence-level and token-level importance sampling. The covariance perspective reveals the roles of the group-mean baseline and score centering in policy gradient estimation. Both perform centering in covariance estimation, but they differ in the mean being subtracted: The group-mean baseline uses a sample mean, whereas score centering uses a population mean. The two lead to distinct unbiased covariance estimators with different variances. Whether both forms of centering should be used simultaneously depends on whether importance sampling is applied at the sequence level or at the token level. With sequence-level importance sampling, score centering adds no benefit once the group-mean baseline is used; with token-level importance sampling, using the group-mean baseline without score centering generally introduces bias when the importance ratio is regularized.

</details>


### [294] [Librarian Agents: Training LLMs to Curate Long-Context Workspaces that Anticipate Future Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22273)
*Anikait Singh,Teresa Zhang,Yoonho Lee,Roshen Sanjay Nair,Aviral Kumar,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Librarian Agents 框架，让智能体在推理前主动将原始文档重构为层级式合成文件系统工作区，并用强化学习直接以下游问答准确率为目标优化构建策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文档上下文推理仍是 LLM 的难题。标准检索方法是被动的、在推理时造成跨文档推理瓶颈；知识图谱等预计算结构依赖僵化启发式而效果有限。作者观察到现代智能体框架（如 Claude Code）中存在不对称性：智能体擅长在已有文件系统中导航完成即时任务，却很少被训练去构建可供未来复用的工作区。

Method: 将工作区构建形式化为马尔可夫决策过程；builder agent 将原始文档转化为含合成文件与引用链接的层级文件系统，供下游 answering agent 使用；通过强化学习优化 builder 策略，直接最大化下游问答准确率。

Result: 在 BrowseComp-Plus 上比最强检索与预计算基线高出 33 个百分点；前期构建成本可在重复查询中摊销；在 BRIGHT 上零样本泛化到未见领域，平均准确率提升 31 点。

Conclusion: 主动式定向整理（anticipatory directed curation）——即在推理前学习性地重组非结构化语料——是提升长文档跨文档推理的有效范式，且构建出的工作区具有可复用性与跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Reasoning over long document contexts remains a challenge for large language models (LLMs). Standard retrieval methods are inherently reactive, bottlenecking agents with complex, cross-document reasoning at inference time, while precomputed structures like knowledge graphs often fall short because they rely on rigid heuristics. Instead, can we allow agents to perform anticipatory directed curation: learning to proactively reorganize unstructured corpora prior to inference to efficiently serve diverse future queries. We build on a key asymmetry in modern agentic harnesses (e.g., Claude Code): while LLM agents are adept at navigating existing filesystems to solve an immediate task, they are rarely trained to construct reusable workspaces for future use. To instantiate this capability, we introduce Librarian Agents, a framework in which a builder agent transforms raw documents into a hierarchical filesystem of synthetic files and reference links, which forms a workspace tailored for a downstream answering agent. We formulate workspace construction as a Markov decision process and optimize the builder policy via reinforcement learning to directly maximize downstream answering accuracy. Empirically, Librarian Agents outperforms the strongest retrieval and precomputation baselines by 33 percentage points on BrowseComp-Plus, amortizes upfront construction costs across repeated queries, and generalizes zero-shot to unseen domains on BRIGHT with a 31-point average accuracy improvement.

</details>


### [295] [Learning Rational Strategies for Temporal Objectives in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22287)
*Omar Adalat,Edwin Hamel-De le Court,Francesco Belardinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究未知动力学下随机多智能体系统的理性合成，用PLTL_f描述各智能体概率时序目标，将Nash均衡等解概念作为约束，提出基于MARL的模型无关方法求解每智能体的理性合成问题，并给出收敛与置信保证及8个一般和并发随机博弈上的实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典理性合成通常假设系统动力学已知且为确定性，而实际多智能体系统往往动力学未知、具有随机性，且各智能体拥有各自的概率时序目标；需要将理性合成扩展到此类未知随机环境并能在模型无关设定下求解。

Method: 将每个智能体的PLTL_f概率时序目标编译为奖励自动机；以各智能体满足目标的概率导出收益，并把解概念（Nash均衡、子博弈完美均衡等）作为联合策略剖面的约束；在MARL环境中用无模型学习（tabular Nash-Q learning）逼近极值满足概率，通过同时有限样本置信界认证约束满足，并用近似最佳响应检查评估策略稳定性；给出条件收敛保证。

Result: 给出了tabular Nash-Q学习的条件收敛保证；能够以同时有限样本置信界认证时序约束的满足性；通过近似最佳响应检查评估策略稳定性；在8个一般和并发随机博弈上进行了实证验证，表明方法可自动求得不依赖模型的理性合成策略。

Conclusion: 该工作把理性合成推广到未知动力学的随机多智能体设定，将时序逻辑目标、均衡约束与MARL学习统一框架，提供理论保证与实验支撑；未来可在更复杂解概念、样本效率与更一般随机博弈上进一步拓展。

Abstract: We study rational synthesis for stochastic multi-agent systems with unknown dynamics, where each agent is equipped with its own probabilistic temporal objective specified using Probabilistic Linear Temporal Logic on Finite Traces ($\mathsf{PLTL}_f}$). We impose solution concepts (e.g., Nash equilibrium, subgame-perfect equilibrium) as constraints on the joint strategy profile, using payoffs derived from each agent's probability of satisfying its temporal objective. In particular, we formulate per-agent rational synthesis as finding a strategy profile -- if one exists -- such that (i) the designated agent's probabilistic temporal constraint is satisfied, and (ii) the profile represents a stable solution under a chosen solution concept. We give conditional convergence guarantees for tabular Nash-Q learning, certify satisfaction with simultaneous finite-sample confidence bounds, and assess strategic stability with approximate best-response checking. Our approach operates in a stochastic \emph{Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning} (MARL) environment, where model-free learning algorithms attempt to attain extremal satisfaction probabilities for temporal objectives automatically compiled into reward automata. Our approach is empirically validated using 8 general-sum concurrent stochastic games.

</details>


### [296] [CAT-alyzing Honesty: Continuous Adversarial Training for Transferable LLM Honesty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22292)
*Yuan-Chen Chang,Niklas Herbster,Gauthier Gidel,Tommaso Tosato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CATCH（Continuous Adversarial Training for Consistent Honesty），通过对比式对抗训练（拉近诚实回答、推远不诚实回答）在少量自报告场景数据上训练，使 LLM 的诚实行为可泛化、可迁移，并在保持模型效用上优于诚实激活引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 虽经对齐训练，仍会在关键场景中表现出不诚实行为，例如输出与知识相矛盾的断言，或给出不被上下文支持的说法；需要一种能稳健泛化、且不牺牲模型能力的诚实性训练方法。

Method: 借鉴用于稳健拒答有害查询的方法，设计 Continuous Adversarial Training for Consistent Honesty（CATCH）：训练模型趋向诚实回答、远离不诚实回答，同时用新颖选取的 utility 数据维持模型能力。仅使用数百条由自报告（self-reporting）场景构造的目标样本进行训练，并在两种架构（Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct、Qwen3-3.6-27B）上验证。

Result: CATCH 学到的诚实行为可稳健泛化并跨基准迁移：在 MASK 上持续降低压力下的不诚实；在 Among Us 多智能体博弈中提升"坦白"率；在 AuditBench 上提高模型隐藏行为的发现率。相比 prompting 与监督微调更优，与 SOTA 诚实激活引导（activation steering）持平，且在维持效用方面超过后者。

Conclusion: 少量自报告场景数据即可通过对抗式偏好训练获得可泛化、可迁移的诚实性；CATCH 在诚实性上优于提示与 SFT、媲美激活引导，并因 utility 数据的选取方式在效用保持上更佳，为诚实对齐提供了兼顾性能与能力保持的方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming prevalent in scenarios where honesty is critical. However, despite alignment training, these models still exhibit dishonest behaviour, such as outputting claims that contradict some knowledge or are not supported by what is accessible in context. We introduce Continuous Adversarial Training for Consistent Honesty (CATCH), inspired by a method originally developed to robustly refuse harmful queries. CATCH trains the model towards an honest answer and away from a dishonest one while maintaining the model's capabilities. We evaluate two architectures (Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct and Qwen3-3.6-27B). We show that trained with only hundreds of targets built from self-reporting scenarios, CATCH learns an honest behaviour that robustly generalizes and transfers across distinct benchmarks. It consistently reduces dishonesty under pressure on MASK, increases confessions in the multi-agent game of Among Us, and improves the discovery rates of the model's hidden behaviours in AuditBench. We show that CATCH outperforms prompting and supervised fine-tuning in improving honesty, and it is on par with state-of-the-art honesty activation steering. However, CATCH surpasses honesty steering in maintaining utility, thanks to a novel choice of utility data.

</details>


### [297] [Towards Continual Learning: Mitigating Forgetting With Subspace Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22293)
*Arnav Chavan,Kusha Sareen,Rishabh Tiwari,Abhranil Chandra,Johan Obando-Ceron,Kurt Keutzer,Scott Niekum,Inderjit S Dhillon,Devvrit Khatri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出子空间微调（SST），将权重更新限制在底部奇异子空间以消除主方向变化，从而在保持目标任务性能的同时大幅缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM微调会带来灾难性遗忘。已有研究表明遗忘与微调对权重的移动幅度及方向有关：RL、在线策略蒸馏等on-policy方法不更新权重矩阵的主奇异方向，因而遗忘较少；而SFT将更新集中沿主方向进行，因而易发生灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出Subspace Tuning（SST）：把每次权重更新限制在底部奇异子空间，从而完全消除沿主奇异方向的变化。SST与优化器无关，可组合SFT、自蒸馏等微调流程；其关键在于紧致子空间保护、稠密梯度估计与简单的刷新机制三项结合。

Result: 相比既有正交微调方法，SST学习能力更强；即使保护高达99%的权重子空间，仍能在保持目标任务性能的同时显著提升保持率，且该效果随模型与数据规模增大而增强，在大模型区间几乎消除遗忘；相比基线配方，KL漂移降低2–4倍，模型更接近基础分布。

Conclusion: SST推进了“学习—遗忘”帕累托前沿，可作为预训练语言模型微调的实用默认方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) acquire new skills through fine-tuning, but it can destroy capabilities they already have: catastrophic forgetting remains a central obstacle to continual learning in LLMs. Recent works connect forgetting to how far, and along which directions, fine-tuning moves the weights. On-policy methods such as reinforcement learning and on-policy distillation avoid updating the principal singular directions of weight matrices and thus forget less. In contrast, SFT concentrates updates along these directions and is susceptible to catastrophic forgetting. In this work, we introduce Subspace Tuning (SST), which restricts each weight update to the bottom singular subspace, eliminating changes along the principal directions. SST is optimizer-agnostic and composes well with fine-tuning recipes including SFT and self-distillation. SST combines tight subspace protection, dense gradient estimation, and a simple refresh mechanism, enabling stronger learning than prior orthogonal fine-tuning methods. With these mechanisms, protecting as much as 99\% of the weight subspace substantially improves retention while preserving strong target-task performance. This behavior strengthens with model and data size and nearly eliminates forgetting in the large-model regime. SST also keeps the model close to its base distribution, inducing $2$--$4\times$ less KL drift than the baseline recipes. Overall, SST advances the learning--forgetting Pareto frontier, making it a practical default for fine-tuning pre-trained language models.

</details>


### [298] [Learning to Steer LLM Agents and Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22316)
*Leyang Shen,Yaxin Gong,Yang Zhang,Chenxiao Fan,Jiakai Wang,Dong Wang,Yang Liu,Fuli Feng,Chun Kai Ling,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"引导智能体"（steering agent）在线监督LLM智能体及多智能体系统的执行过程，通过快/慢双路径干预机制纠正"目标漂移"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体与多智能体系统在执行长程工具调用和协作任务时，会随执行推进逐渐偏离整体目标（目标漂移），浪费执行资源并导致不良结果，因此需要在线监督与纠偏机制。

Method: 设计引导智能体观察执行上下文，用低成本快路径决定"何时干预"、用高成本慢路径决定"如何干预"；联合训练两条路径，采用强化学习，结合成对排序（pairwise ranking）目标与组相对策略优化（GRPO）。

Result: 在信息检索搜索任务上，引导智能体对单智能体与多智能体系统均提供有效监督，提升其性能与效率；所学引导能力可泛化到域外任务、迁移到未见执行者，并支持无需重训练的可控干预频率。

Conclusion: 在线监督式引导智能体是缓解目标漂移的有效方案，且其引导智能具备良好的泛化性、可迁移性与干预频率可控性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents and multi-agent systems increasingly tackle complex tasks through long-horizon tool use and inter-agent collaboration. However, these systems are prone to deviating from the overall goal as execution unfolds, which wastes execution resources and leads to undesired outcomes, a failure termed goal drift. In this work, we propose a steering agent that supervises execution on the fly and steers the system back on track when drift occurs. The steering agent observes the execution context, deciding when to intervene with a low-cost fast path and how to intervene with a high-cost slow path. To unlock its steering intelligence, the ability to assess goal drift and generate effective interventions, we jointly train the two paths with reinforcement learning, combining a pairwise ranking objective with group-relative policy optimization (GRPO). Experiments on information-seeking search tasks show that the steering agent provides effective supervision for both single-agent and multi-agent systems, improving their performance and efficiency. The learned steering intelligence generalizes to out-of-domain tasks, transfers to unseen executors, and supports controllable intervention frequency without retraining.

</details>


### [299] [Twice Upon a Time: Complementary Representations for Time-Series Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22327)
*Hedi Zisling,Liran Nochumsohn,Ilan Naiman,Mihir Parmar,Omri Azencot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“互补表示”设计原则：将折线图（保留绝对量纲与坐标帧）与归一化延迟嵌入（消除正幅度缩放与偏移、暴露时序结构）作为两种视觉视图，经专用编码器与多级融合注入同一语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列语言模型（TSLM）通常只向语言模型暴露单一表示，从而对所有下游任务强加固定的归纳偏置，无法兼顾“保留原始测量帧”与“提取时序结构”的冲突需求。核心问题是：时间序列表示应保留哪些信息、应因子化消除哪些变化。

Method: 双视图视觉表示：①折线图保留绝对测量值与观测坐标帧；②归一化延迟嵌入消除正幅度缩放与偏移并显式暴露时序结构。两个专用视觉编码器将视图转为视觉 token，经多级特征融合注入共享语言模型（基座 Qwen3-VL-8B）；并通过评测期干预验证两种视图的差异性不变性，以及单视图消融分析各自贡献。

Result: TSRBench 总体准确率由仅折线图的 44.5% 提升至 53.2%，远高于最强专用 TSLM 基线 36.7%；TSExam 达 89.9%，多变量 ChatTS 对齐数值准确率 86.8%。干预实验表明两视图具有不同的不变性轮廓，单视图消融显示组合收益在超越基础感知的预测与推理任务上尤为显著。

Conclusion: 互补表示是通用 TSLM 的有效设计原则：使模型既能利用时序结构，又不牺牲对原始测量帧的访问能力，从而在感知、预测与推理任务上取得一致提升。

Abstract: Time Series Language Models (TSLMs) enable language-based understanding and reasoning over temporal data, yet a fundamental question remains: what information should their time-series representations preserve, and what variation should they factor out? Existing TSLMs typically expose the language model to a single representation, imposing a fixed inductive bias across downstream tasks. We instead introduce complementary representations as a design principle for time-series reasoning. Our model combines two visual views: line plots preserve absolute measurements and the observed coordinate frame, while normalized delay embeddings factor out positive amplitude rescaling and offsets while exposing temporal structure. Dedicated visual encoders integrate both views into a shared language model through visual tokens and multi-level feature fusion. Evaluation-time interventions verify that the two views exhibit distinct invariance profiles, while single-view ablations show that their combination is particularly beneficial beyond basic perception, with the large gains in prediction and reasoning. Built on Qwen3-VL-8B, our dual-view model improves overall TSRBench accuracy from 44.5% with line plots alone to 53.2%, substantially exceeding the strongest evaluated dedicated TSLM baseline at 36.7%. It further achieves 89.9% accuracy on TSExam and 86.8% numerical accuracy on multivariate ChatTS alignment. Together, these results demonstrate that complementary representations provide an effective design principle for general-purpose TSLMs, enabling models to exploit temporal structure without sacrificing access to the original measurement frame.

</details>


### [300] [Robust Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Treatment Regimes under Model and Measurement Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22336)
*Chengze Xie,Xuan Wang,Ying Ding,Hairong Wang,Yan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出鲁棒强化学习框架，将患者建模为隐上下文，用鲁棒上下文马尔可夫决策过程统一处理泛化与鲁棒性，并在转移性前列腺癌自适应治疗仿真中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应治疗策略难以在不同患者间泛化，且在生物标志物测量误差与疾病动态变化下不可靠，需要一种同时兼顾泛化性与鲁棒性的原则性方法。

Method: 构建鲁棒RL框架：将患者视为隐上下文（hidden context），统一建模为鲁棒上下文马尔可夫决策过程（robust contextual MDP）；在基于临床数据校准的转移性前列腺癌仿真器中学习自适应治疗策略。

Result: 所得策略可泛化且鲁棒，大幅优于现有自适应治疗方法；在标志物准确、疾病按预期演化时几乎无性能损失即获得鲁棒性；在多种情景下Pareto支配现有方法。

Conclusion: 鲁棒RL可作为学习可靠自适应治疗策略的原则性方法，具有较大应用前景。

Abstract: We develop a robust reinforcement learning (RL) framework for learning adaptive treatment policies that generalize across patients and remain reliable under errors in the measured biomarker and shifts in the disease dynamics. By modeling the patient as a hidden context, we address generalization and robustness in a unified manner through robust contextual Markov decision processes. Applying our framework to adaptive therapy for metastatic prostate cancer in a simulator calibrated to clinical data, we obtain generalizable and robust policies that substantially outperform the existing adaptive therapy method. In particular, we attain robustness with almost no loss of performance when the biomarker is accurate and the disease evolves as anticipated, and find that the resulting policy Pareto-dominates the existing method across a diverse array of scenarios. Our findings suggest that robust RL holds great promise as a principled methodology for learning reliable adaptive treatment policies.

</details>


### [301] [Lean Graph Search for Formal Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22339)
*Yihan Xu,Jia-Chen Gu,Junyi Zhang,Di Wu,Haikang Deng,Po-Nien Kung,Kai-Wei Chang,Amit Sahai,Wei Wang,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LeanGraphSearch：基于 Mathlib 正向依赖图的多跳检索方法，提升引理召回并在端到端定理证明中提高通过率与成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索系统将库声明匹配到目标定理或其中间证明步骤，但对声明间的多跳依赖利用不足；这类依赖可揭示与目标定理词面不相似、却在相关问题证明中反复出现的支撑引理。

Method: 针对 Lean 4 构建基于 Mathlib 正向依赖的图：将每个声明链接到其依赖的前提，用图排序算法把相关性从语义匹配的声明传播到其依赖项，再以原始查询对扩展候选进行重排序，并通过多跳依赖链挖掘支撑引理。

Result: 在 MathlibQR 上召回率较最强基线 LeanSearch v2 最高提升 12%；在 MathlibMPR 上完整标注证明路径覆盖多出最多 3.7% 的组。集成到由 Lean 反馈引导的迭代证明循环后，在三个定理证明基准上，{8,16,32} 反思轮次下通过率较原始证明器最高提升 40%，较 LeanSearch v2 最高提升 10%，精度–成本效率更高。

Conclusion: 依赖图搜索对定理证明具有价值，支持结构化感知的检索用于证明发现，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Retrieving relevant lemmas is crucial for formal theorem proving, enabling provers to build machine-checkable proofs from established mathematical results. Recent retrieval systems match library declarations to a target theorem or its intermediate proof steps, but make limited use of multi-hop dependencies among declarations. These dependencies can reveal supporting lemmas that are not necessarily lexically similar to the target theorem but recur in proofs of related problems. To this end, we propose LeanGraphSearch, a graph-based retrieval method for Lean 4 that constructs a graph from Mathlib's forward dependencies and traverses it to uncover supporting lemmas through multi-hop dependency chains. Specifically, LeanGraphSearch links each declaration to the premises it depends on, uses a graph-based ranking algorithm to propagate relevance from semantically matched declarations to their dependencies, and reranks the expanded candidates against the original query. Across two mathematical retrieval benchmarks, LeanGraphSearch improves recall by up to 12% over the strongest baseline, LeanSearch v2, on MathlibQR and increases coverage of complete annotated proof routes on MathlibMPR, covering up to 3.7% of extra groups. More importantly, we evaluate whether these retrieval gains translate into improved end-to-end theorem proving by integrating LeanGraphSearch into an iterative proving loop guided by Lean feedback. Across three theorem proving benchmarks, LeanGraphSearch improves pass rate at {8, 16, 32} reflection turns by as much as 40% compared to vanilla prover and at most 10% compared to LeanSearch v2, showing higher accuracy-cost efficiency. These findings highlight the value of dependency graph search for theorem proving and motivate further research on structure-aware search for proof discovery.

</details>


### [302] [Rethinking Routing Exploration in Modern Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22353)
*Bingcong Li,Hao Ma,Michael Muehlebach,Niao He,Yi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 细粒度专家分割与共享专家的组合存在"探索困境"，MirrorMoE通过token自适应抑制冗余专家来缩小探索空间，从而缓解该困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代MoE架构常同时采用细粒度专家分割和共享专家。细粒度分割扩大路由组合空间、需要更强探索，而共享专家会降低token表示多样性、削弱路由探索性，导致细粒度分割带来的表达力难以真正兑现。

Method: 提出MirrorMoE，通过对不必要的专家进行token自适应抑制（token-adaptive suppression）来收缩有效探索空间，调和上述探索困境。

Result: MirrorMoE在不同模型规模下均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过缩小探索空间可有效协调细粒度分割与共享专家之间的冲突，使MoE的表达力得以更充分发挥。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models have become an important approach for scaling model parameters. Modern MoE architectures increasingly combine two design choices: fine-grained expert segmentation and shared experts. The former increases expressiveness by enabling more expert combinations at a fixed activation ratio, and the latter introduces experts that are active for every token. We identify an exploration dilemma between these designs: shared experts can make routing less exploratory by reducing the variability of token representations. This conflicts with the greater need for exploration induced by fine-grained expert segmentation due to the combinatorially enlarged routing space. Consequently, the additional expressiveness offered by fine-grained segmentation may not be fully realized in practice. MirrorMoE is proposed to reconcile this exploration dilemma by shrinking the exploration space through token-adaptive suppression of unnecessary experts. MirrorMoE consistently improves performance across model scales.

</details>


### [303] [PathFM: Fast Generative Forecasting of Multivariate Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22358)
*Jiachen Yao,Xiyuan Zhang,Annan Yu,Boran Han,Abdul Fatir Ansari,Shuai Zhang,Michael Bohlke-Schneider,Bernie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于flow matching的轨迹式预测模型PathFM，通过蒸馏采样器一次生成多条完整未来路径，并构建fps-bench评估路径依赖指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流时间序列基础模型只预测边际分位数，未刻画未来轨迹的联合依赖结构，而累积需求、峰值负荷、事件时点等轨迹依赖量无法由点预测或边际指标反映。

Method: 用flow matching生成完整未来路径；将模型蒸馏到1–2步并跨多次采样共享历史注意力缓存，使单序列多次路径采样接近一次分位数查询开销；蒸馏采样器支持直接优化校准损失；并提出fps-bench，在fev-bench任务上增加energy score、variogram score及下游指标。

Result: 在fev-bench与TIME上边际精度具竞争力；在fps-bench上聚合路径得分高于所有外部基线；在混沌系统实验中保持长期变异性，而强基线迅速坍塌。

Conclusion: PathFM使生成式时间序列预测兼具竞争力与实用性，可支撑多样化下游应用。

Abstract: Time series foundation models are becoming increasingly popular for general-purpose forecasting. However, most leading models predict marginal quantiles, leaving the dependence structure of future trajectories unspecified. This distinction matters for trajectory-dependent quantities such as cumulative demand, peak load, and event timing, yet point and marginal scores do not reveal it. To model this dependence, we introduce PathFM, a trajectory-based forecasting model that uses flow matching to generate complete future paths. We further distill the model to one or two steps and share history attention cache across draws. Together, these enable PathFM to generate multiple paths for a single series in roughly the time needed for one query to a quantile model. The distilled sampler also makes it practical to optimize a calibration loss directly. PathFM achieves competitive marginal accuracy on the established forecasting benchmarks fev-bench and TIME. To complement these evaluations, we introduce Forecast Path Score Bench (fps-bench), which augments fev-bench tasks with energy and variogram scores and downstream metrics. PathFM achieves a higher aggregate path score on fps-bench than all evaluated external baselines. Experiments on chaotic systems further show that our flow model sustains long-run variability, while strong baselines rapidly collapse. PathFM makes generative time series forecasting both competitive and practical, enabling diverse downstream applications.

</details>
